class Any (*args, **kwargs)
Expand source code 
class Any(metaclass=_AnyMeta):
    """Special type indicating an unconstrained type.

    - Any is compatible with every type.
    - Any assumed to have all methods.
    - All values assumed to be instances of Any.

    Note that all the above statements are true from the point of view of
    static type checkers. At runtime, Any should not be used with instance
    checks.
    """

    def __new__(cls, *args, **kwargs):
        if cls is Any:
            raise TypeError("Any cannot be instantiated")
        return super().__new__(cls)

class AstroLinear (in_features: int,
out_features: int,

Expand source code 
class AstroLinear(nn.Module):
    """Linear layer using Astro uniform quantization with STE backpropagation support."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        in_features: int,
        out_features: int,
        bias: bool = True,
        bits: int = 8,
        symmetric: bool = True,
        per_channel: bool = False,
        per_channel_bits: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    ):
        super().__init__()
        self.in_features = in_features
        self.out_features = out_features
        self.nf = out_features  # Conv1D compatibility
        self.bits = bits
        self.symmetric = symmetric
        self.per_channel = per_channel

        if per_channel_bits is not None:
            self.register_buffer("per_channel_bits", per_channel_bits.clone().long())
        else:
            self.register_buffer("per_channel_bits", None)

        self.weight = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(out_features, in_features))
        if bias:
            self.bias = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(out_features))
        else:
            self.register_parameter("bias", None)
        nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.weight, a=5**0.5)
        if self.bias is not None:
            nn.init.zeros_(self.bias)

    @classmethod
    def from_linear(
        cls,
        linear: nn.Module,
        bits: int = 8,
        symmetric: bool = True,
        per_channel: bool = False,
        per_channel_bits: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    ) -> AstroLinear:
        cls_name = linear.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(linear, "nf")
        raw_w = linear.weight.data

        if is_conv1d:
            in_f = raw_w.shape[0]
            out_f = raw_w.shape[1]
            w_data = raw_w.t()
        else:
            out_f = raw_w.shape[0]
            in_f = raw_w.shape[1]
            w_data = raw_w

        layer = cls(
            in_features=in_f,
            out_features=out_f,
            bias=getattr(linear, "bias", None) is not None and linear.bias is not None,
            bits=bits,
            symmetric=symmetric,
            per_channel=per_channel,
            per_channel_bits=per_channel_bits,
        )
        layer.weight.data.copy_(w_data)
        if getattr(linear, "bias", None) is not None and linear.bias is not None:
            layer.bias.data.copy_(linear.bias.data)
        return layer

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        w_q = quantize_dequantize_tensor(
            self.weight,
            bits=self.bits,
            symmetric=self.symmetric,
            per_channel=self.per_channel or (self.per_channel_bits is not None),
            use_ste=self.training,
            per_channel_bits=self.per_channel_bits,
        )
        b_q = None
        if self.bias is not None:
            bias_bits = (
                int(torch.round(self.per_channel_bits.float().mean()).item())
                if self.per_channel_bits is not None
                else self.bits
            )
            b_q = quantize_dequantize_tensor(
                self.bias,
                bits=bias_bits,
                symmetric=self.symmetric,
                per_channel=False,
                use_ste=self.training,
            )
        return nn.functional.linear(
            x,
            w_q.to(dtype=x.dtype, device=x.device),
            b_q.to(dtype=x.dtype, device=x.device) if b_q is not None else None,
        )

Linear layer using Astro uniform quantization with STE backpropagation support.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

Define the computation performed at every call.

Should be overridden by all subclasses.

Note

Although the recipe for forward pass needs to be defined within this function, one should call the :class:Module instance afterwards instead of this since the former takes care of running the registered hooks while the latter silently ignores them.

class AstroQuantizer (bits: int = 4)
Expand source code 
class AstroQuantizer:
    """
    Non-uniform quantizer with INT2 / INT4 / INT8 codebooks optimized for
    Gaussian weight distributions.

    RS-FIX-01: quantize() returns (indices int8, centroids fp32) so the
    caller can store indices compactly and reconstruct with a tiny lookup.
    """

    SUPPORTED_BITS = (2, 4, 8)

    def __init__(self, bits: int = 4) -> None:
        self.bits = bits if bits in self.SUPPORTED_BITS else 4
        self.num_centroids = 2**self.bits
        k = torch.arange(self.num_centroids, dtype=torch.float32)
        mid = (self.num_centroids - 1.0) / 2.0
        # Gaussian-shaped centroid grid (still spread over [-1, 1] before scaling).
        denom = max(mid, 1e-6)
        self.centroids_base = torch.sign(k - mid) * torch.pow(torch.abs(k - mid) / denom, 1.4)

    def quantize(self, w: torch.Tensor) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
        """
        Symmetric per-tensor quantization.

        Returns:
            indices:   (..., ) int8 tensor of centroid indices in [0, C)
            centroids: (C,) fp32 tensor of scaled centroids
        """
        if w.numel() == 0:
            return torch.zeros_like(w, dtype=torch.int8), self.centroids_base.clone()

        w_min = w.min()
        w_max = w.max()
        c_min = self.centroids_base.min()
        c_max = self.centroids_base.max()
        denom = (c_max - c_min).clamp_min(1e-9)
        s = (w_max - w_min) / denom
        z = w_min - c_min * s

        centroids = self.centroids_base.to(w.device, dtype=torch.float32) * s + z
        # (..., 1) vs (1, ..., C) → (..., C)
        distances = torch.abs(w.unsqueeze(-1).float() - centroids)
        indices = torch.argmin(distances, dim=-1).to(torch.int8)
        return indices, centroids.to(torch.float32)

    @staticmethod
    def dequantize(indices: torch.Tensor, centroids: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Reconstruct floats from indices and centroids."""
        flat_idx = indices.reshape(-1).long()
        return centroids[flat_idx].reshape(indices.shape)

Non-uniform quantizer with INT2 / INT4 / INT8 codebooks optimized for Gaussian weight distributions.

RS-FIX-01: quantize() returns (indices int8, centroids fp32) so the caller can store indices compactly and reconstruct with a tiny lookup.

class AstroQuant4Bit (bits: int = 4)
Expand source code 
class AstroQuantizer:
    """
    Non-uniform quantizer with INT2 / INT4 / INT8 codebooks optimized for
    Gaussian weight distributions.

    RS-FIX-01: quantize() returns (indices int8, centroids fp32) so the
    caller can store indices compactly and reconstruct with a tiny lookup.
    """

    SUPPORTED_BITS = (2, 4, 8)

    def __init__(self, bits: int = 4) -> None:
        self.bits = bits if bits in self.SUPPORTED_BITS else 4
        self.num_centroids = 2**self.bits
        k = torch.arange(self.num_centroids, dtype=torch.float32)
        mid = (self.num_centroids - 1.0) / 2.0
        # Gaussian-shaped centroid grid (still spread over [-1, 1] before scaling).
        denom = max(mid, 1e-6)
        self.centroids_base = torch.sign(k - mid) * torch.pow(torch.abs(k - mid) / denom, 1.4)

    def quantize(self, w: torch.Tensor) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
        """
        Symmetric per-tensor quantization.

        Returns:
            indices:   (..., ) int8 tensor of centroid indices in [0, C)
            centroids: (C,) fp32 tensor of scaled centroids
        """
        if w.numel() == 0:
            return torch.zeros_like(w, dtype=torch.int8), self.centroids_base.clone()

        w_min = w.min()
        w_max = w.max()
        c_min = self.centroids_base.min()
        c_max = self.centroids_base.max()
        denom = (c_max - c_min).clamp_min(1e-9)
        s = (w_max - w_min) / denom
        z = w_min - c_min * s

        centroids = self.centroids_base.to(w.device, dtype=torch.float32) * s + z
        # (..., 1) vs (1, ..., C) → (..., C)
        distances = torch.abs(w.unsqueeze(-1).float() - centroids)
        indices = torch.argmin(distances, dim=-1).to(torch.int8)
        return indices, centroids.to(torch.float32)

    @staticmethod
    def dequantize(indices: torch.Tensor, centroids: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Reconstruct floats from indices and centroids."""
        flat_idx = indices.reshape(-1).long()
        return centroids[flat_idx].reshape(indices.shape)

def quantize(self, w: torch.Tensor) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:

    Symmetric per-tensor quantization.

    Returns:
        indices:   (..., ) int8 tensor of centroid indices in [0, C)

class BaseCompressor (model: torch.nn.modules.module.Module, name: str = 'base', **kwargs)
Expand source code 
class BaseCompressor:
    """Base class for all compressors. Not a nn.Module to avoid parameter storage."""

    def __init__(self, model: nn.Module, name: str = "base", **kwargs) -> None:
        self.model = model
        self.name = name
        self.kwargs = kwargs
        self._original_params = self._count_params(model)

    def __call__(self, *args, **kwargs) -> Any:
        """Allow compressor instance to act as a callable model forward wrapper."""
        if not getattr(self, "_compressed_once", False):
            self.compress()
            self._compressed_once = True
        return self.model(*args, **kwargs)

    @abstractmethod
    def compress(self) -> tuple[nn.Module, dict[str, Any]]:
        """Return (compressed_model, statistics)."""
        raise NotImplementedError("Subclasses of BaseCompressor must implement compress()")

    def _count_params(self, model: nn.Module) -> int:
        """count params."""
        return sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters())

    def get_compression_stats(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Return the compression stats."""
        compressed = self._count_params(self.model)
        orig = self._original_params
        return {
            "compressor_name": self.name,
            "original_parameters": orig,
            "compressed_parameters": compressed,
            "saved_parameters": orig - compressed,
            "compression_ratio": compressed / orig if orig > 0 else 1.0,
            "reduction_percentage": ((orig - compressed) / orig * 100 if orig > 0 else 0.0),
        }

    def summary(self) -> str:
        """Summary."""
        s = self.get_compression_stats()
        return (
            f"\n========================================\n"
            f"Compression Summary: {self.name}\n"
            f"========================================\n"
            f"Original parameters:  {s['original_parameters']:,}\n"
            f"Compressed parameters: {s['compressed_parameters']:,}\n"
            f"Parameters saved:     {s['saved_parameters']:,}\n"
            f"Compression ratio:    {s['compression_ratio']:.3f}\n"
            f"Reduction:            {s['reduction_percentage']:.2f}%\n"
            f"========================================\n"
        )

Base class for all compressors. Not a nn.Module to avoid parameter storage.

class Conv1D (nf, nx)
Expand source code 
class Conv1D(nn.Module):
    """
    1D-convolutional layer as defined by Radford et al. for OpenAI GPT (and also used in GPT-2).

    Basically works like a linear layer but the weights are transposed.

    Args:
        nf (`int`): The number of output features.
        nx (`int`): The number of input features.
    """

    def __init__(self, nf, nx):
        super().__init__()
        self.nf = nf
        self.nx = nx
        self.weight = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(nx, nf))
        self.bias = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(nf))
        nn.init.normal_(self.weight, std=0.02)

    def __repr__(self) -> str:
        return "Conv1D(nf={nf}, nx={nx})".format(**self.__dict__)

    def forward(self, x):
        size_out = x.size()[:-1] + (self.nf,)
        x = torch.addmm(self.bias, x.view(-1, x.size(-1)), self.weight)
        x = x.view(size_out)
        return x

1D-convolutional layer as defined by Radford et al. for OpenAI GPT (and also used in GPT-2).

Basically works like a linear layer but the weights are transposed.

Args -----= nf (int): The number of output features. nx (int): The number of input features. Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

  • torch.nn.modules.module.Module

    x = x.view(size_out)

class CoopQLinear (in_features: int,

class CoopQLinear(nn.Module):
    """
    CoopQ Linear layer with per-channel asymmetric quantization and STE backpropagation.
    Supports both standard nn.Linear and Conv1D weight representations.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        in_features: int,
        out_features: int,
        bias: bool = True,
        bits: int = 4,
        symmetric: bool = False,
        per_channel: bool = True,
        lora_r: int = 0,
        lora_alpha: float = 1.0,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.in_features = in_features
        self.out_features = out_features
        self.nf = out_features  # Conv1D compatibility
        self.bits = max(4, int(bits))
        self.symmetric = symmetric
        self.per_channel = per_channel
        self.lora_r = int(lora_r)
        self.lora_alpha = float(lora_alpha)
        self.scaling = (self.lora_alpha / self.lora_r) if self.lora_r > 0 else 1.0

        self.weight = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(out_features, in_features))
        if bias:
            self.bias = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(out_features))
        else:
            self.register_parameter("bias", None)

        if self.lora_r > 0:
            self.lora_A = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.lora_r, in_features))
            self.lora_B = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(out_features, self.lora_r))
            nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
            nn.init.zeros_(self.lora_B)
        else:
            self.register_parameter("lora_A", None)
            self.register_parameter("lora_B", None)

        nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.weight, a=5**0.5)
        if self.bias is not None:
            nn.init.zeros_(self.bias)

    @classmethod
    def from_linear(
        cls,
        linear: nn.Module,
        bits: int = 4,
        symmetric: bool = False,
        per_channel: bool = True,
        lora_r: int = 0,
        lora_alpha: float = 1.0,
    ) -> CoopQLinear:
        cls_name = linear.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(linear, "nf")
        raw_w = linear.weight.data

        if is_conv1d:
            in_f = raw_w.shape[0]
            out_f = raw_w.shape[1]
            w_data = raw_w.t().contiguous()
        else:
            out_f = raw_w.shape[0]
            in_f = raw_w.shape[1]
            w_data = raw_w.contiguous()

        layer = cls(
            in_features=in_f,
            out_features=out_f,
            bias=getattr(linear, "bias", None) is not None and linear.bias is not None,
            bits=bits,
            symmetric=symmetric,
            per_channel=per_channel,
            lora_r=lora_r,
            lora_alpha=lora_alpha,
        )
        layer.weight.data.copy_(w_data)
        if getattr(linear, "bias", None) is not None and linear.bias is not None:
            layer.bias.data.copy_(linear.bias.data)
        return layer

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        w_q = quantize_dequantize_coopq(
            self.weight,
            bits=self.bits,
            symmetric=self.symmetric,
            per_channel=self.per_channel,
            use_ste=self.training,
        )
        b_q = None
        if self.bias is not None:
            b_q = quantize_dequantize_coopq(
                self.bias,
                bits=self.bits,
                symmetric=self.symmetric,
                per_channel=False,
                use_ste=self.training,
            )
        out = F.linear(
            x,
            w_q.to(dtype=x.dtype, device=x.device),
            b_q.to(dtype=x.dtype, device=x.device) if b_q is not None else None,
        )
        if self.lora_r > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
            lora_out = F.linear(F.linear(x, self.lora_A), self.lora_B)
            out = out + (self.scaling * lora_out).to(out.dtype)
        return out

CoopQ Linear layer with per-channel asymmetric quantization and STE backpropagation. Supports both standard nn.Linear and Conv1D weight representations.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

        self.weight,
        bits=self.bits,
        symmetric=self.symmetric,
        per_channel=self.per_channel,
        use_ste=self.training,

            self.bias,
            bits=self.bits,
            symmetric=self.symmetric,
            per_channel=False,
            use_ste=self.training,
        )
    out = F.linear(
        x,
        w_q.to(dtype=x.dtype, device=x.device),
        b_q.to(dtype=x.dtype, device=x.device) if b_q is not None else None,

Define the computation performed at every call.

Should be overridden by all subclasses.

Note

Although the recipe for forward pass needs to be defined within this function, one should call the :class:Module instance afterwards instead of this since the former takes care of running the registered hooks while the latter silently ignores them.

Expand source code 
class CoopQQuantizer(BaseCompressor):
    """
    CoopQ: Cooperative Game-Theoretic Bit Allocation Quantization.
    Allocates discrete bit-widths (>= 4 bits) across transformer layers
    proportional to layer sensitivity (Frobenius energy / variance), guaranteeing
    a target mean bit-budget under strict distortion minimization.

    Args:
        model: PyTorch model.
        bits: Target average bit-width (default 4).
        min_bits: Minimum allowable bit-width per layer (floor enforced at >= 4).
        max_bits: Maximum allowable bit-width per layer (default 8).
        symmetric: Whether to use symmetric quantization (default False for asymmetric calibration).
        per_channel: Whether to use per-channel scale factors (default True).
        use_ste: Whether to substitute nn.Linear with CoopQLinear for QAT (default True).
        target_attention_only: If True, only quantize attention projection layers (skip FFN/MLP).
        cfg: Optional configuration dictionary, namespace, or object.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        model: nn.Module,
        bits: int = 4,
        min_bits: int = 4,
        max_bits: int = 8,
        symmetric: bool = False,
        per_channel: bool = True,
        use_ste: bool = True,
        recalibrate_ln: bool = True,
        target_attention_only: bool = False,
        tokenizer: Any | None = None,
        calibration_loader: Any | None = None,
        calibration_texts: list[str] | None = None,
        device: str | torch.device | None = None,
        cfg: Any | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(model, name="coopq", **kwargs)
        # Extract config values with _cfg_get fallbacks
        c_bits = _cfg_get(cfg, "coopq_bits", _cfg_get(cfg, "bits", bits))
        c_min_bits = _cfg_get(cfg, "coopq_min_bits", _cfg_get(cfg, "min_bits", min_bits))
        c_max_bits = _cfg_get(cfg, "coopq_max_bits", _cfg_get(cfg, "max_bits", max_bits))
        c_symmetric = _cfg_get(cfg, "coopq_symmetric", _cfg_get(cfg, "symmetric", symmetric))
        c_per_channel = _cfg_get(
            cfg, "coopq_per_channel", _cfg_get(cfg, "per_channel", per_channel)
        )
        c_use_ste = _cfg_get(cfg, "coopq_use_ste", _cfg_get(cfg, "use_ste", use_ste))
        c_recalibrate_ln = _cfg_get(
            cfg, "coopq_recalibrate_ln", _cfg_get(cfg, "recalibrate_ln", recalibrate_ln)
        )
        c_target_attn = _cfg_get(
            cfg,
            "coopq_target_attention_only",
            _cfg_get(cfg, "target_attention_only", target_attention_only),
        )

        self.min_bits = max(4, int(c_min_bits))
        self.max_bits = max(self.min_bits, int(c_max_bits))
        self.bits = max(self.min_bits, min(int(c_bits), self.max_bits))
        self.symmetric = bool(c_symmetric)
        self.per_channel = bool(c_per_channel)
        self.use_ste = bool(c_use_ste)
        self.recalibrate_ln = bool(c_recalibrate_ln)
        self.target_attention_only = bool(c_target_attn)

        self.tokenizer = tokenizer or getattr(model, "tokenizer", None)
        self.calibration_loader = calibration_loader
        self.calibration_texts = calibration_texts
        self.target_device = (
            torch.device(device)
            if device is not None
            else (next(model.parameters(), torch.tensor(0.0)).device)
        )
        self.stats: dict[str, Any] = {}

    def _is_target_module(self, name: str, module: nn.Module) -> bool:
        """Identify candidate linear/Conv1D/LoRALinear modules."""
        cls_name = module.__class__.__name__.lower()
        has_base = hasattr(module, "base_layer")
        base_cls = module.base_layer.__class__.__name__.lower() if has_base else ""

        is_linear = isinstance(module, nn.Linear) or "linear" in cls_name
        is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(module, "nf")
        is_lora = has_base and (
            isinstance(module.base_layer, nn.Linear)
            or "conv1d" in base_cls
            or hasattr(module.base_layer, "nf")
        )

        if not (is_linear or is_conv1d or is_lora):
            return False

        if isinstance(module, CoopQLinear) or (
            has_base and isinstance(module.base_layer, CoopQLinear)
        ):
            return False

        name_lower = name.lower()
        if any(
            skip in name_lower
            for skip in [
                "classifier",
                "score",
                "lm_head",
                "pooler",
                "embed",
                "logits_processor",
                "pre_classifier",
                "head",
            ]
        ):
            return False

        if self.target_attention_only:
            ffn_patterns = [
                "ffn",
                "mlp",
                "lin1",
                "lin2",
                "intermediate",
                "gate_proj",
                "up_proj",
                "down_proj",
                "c_fc",
            ]
            if any(p in name_lower for p in ffn_patterns):
                return False
            if "output.dense" in name_lower and "attention" not in name_lower:
                return False
            attn_patterns = [
                "attn",
                "attention",
                "q_proj",
                "k_proj",
                "v_proj",
                "o_proj",
                "out_lin",
                "q_lin",
                "k_lin",
                "v_lin",
                "c_attn",
                "c_proj",
                "query",
                "key",
                "value",
                "in_proj_qkv",
                "out_proj",
            ]
            if not any(p in name_lower for p in attn_patterns):
                return False

        return True

    def _calculate_layer_sensitivities(self) -> dict[str, float]:
        """Compute sensitivity metric per target layer based on normalized Frobenius energy and variance."""
        sensitivities: dict[str, float] = {}
        for name, module in self.model.named_modules():
            if self._is_target_module(name, module):
                target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", getattr(module, "linear", module))
                raw_w = target_mod.weight.data
                cls_name = module.__class__.__name__.lower()
                is_conv1d = (
                    "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(module, "nf") or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
                )
                W = raw_w.t().float() if is_conv1d else raw_w.float()
                frob_norm = torch.norm(W, p="fro").item() / (W.numel() ** 0.5 + 1e-8)
                std_val = torch.std(W).item() if W.numel() > 1 else 0.0
                sensitivities[name] = float(frob_norm + std_val)
        return sensitivities

    def _allocate_bits(self, sensitivities: dict[str, float]) -> dict[str, int]:
        """Allocate discrete integer bit-widths satisfying mean(bits) <= self.bits with floor >= min_bits (>= 4)."""
        if not sensitivities:
            return {}

        total_sens = sum(sensitivities.values()) + 1e-8
        num_layers = len(sensitivities)
        target_total_bits = self.bits * num_layers

        # Allowed bit-levels with floor >= self.min_bits (>= 4)
        allowed_bits = [b for b in [4, 5, 6, 7, 8] if self.min_bits <= b <= self.max_bits]
        if not allowed_bits:
            allowed_bits = [self.min_bits]

        # Continuous allocation proportional to sensitivity
        raw_allocations: dict[str, float] = {}
        mean_sens = total_sens / num_layers
        for name, sens in sensitivities.items():
            sens_ratio = sens / mean_sens
            raw_bits = self.bits + (sens_ratio - 1.0) * (self.max_bits - self.min_bits) / 2.0
            raw_allocations[name] = max(float(self.min_bits), min(float(self.max_bits), raw_bits))

        discrete_allocations: dict[str, int] = {}
        for name, raw_b in raw_allocations.items():
            closest_b = min(allowed_bits, key=lambda b: abs(b - raw_b))
            discrete_allocations[name] = closest_b

        # Adjust sum to match target total bits budget
        current_sum = sum(discrete_allocations.values())
        sorted_names = sorted(sensitivities.keys(), key=lambda k: sensitivities[k], reverse=True)

        if current_sum > target_total_bits:
            for name in reversed(sorted_names):
                curr_b = discrete_allocations[name]
                valid_lower = [b for b in allowed_bits if b < curr_b]
                if valid_lower:
                    discrete_allocations[name] = max(valid_lower)
                    current_sum = sum(discrete_allocations.values())
                    if current_sum <= target_total_bits:
                        break
        elif current_sum < target_total_bits:
            for name in sorted_names:
                curr_b = discrete_allocations[name]
                valid_higher = [b for b in allowed_bits if b > curr_b]
                if valid_higher:
                    discrete_allocations[name] = min(valid_higher)
                    current_sum = sum(discrete_allocations.values())
                    if current_sum >= target_total_bits:
                        break

        # Ensure strict min_bits floor on all layers
        for k in discrete_allocations:
            discrete_allocations[k] = max(self.min_bits, discrete_allocations[k])

        return discrete_allocations

    def compress(self) -> tuple[nn.Module, dict[str, Any]]:
        """Quantize linear layers with layer-specific bit allocation (>= 4 bits) and per-channel asymmetric calibration."""
        sensitivities = self._calculate_layer_sensitivities()
        bit_allocations = self._allocate_bits(sensitivities)

        total_bits_weighted = 0
        total_params_linear = 0
        compressed_layers = []
        fallback_layers = []

        for name, module in list(self.model.named_modules()):
            if not self._is_target_module(name, module):
                # When target_attention_only is True, unselected FFN/MLP layers must be frozen during fine-tuning
                if self.target_attention_only:
                    is_lin_like = (
                        isinstance(module, nn.Linear)
                        or "conv1d" in module.__class__.__name__.lower()
                        or hasattr(module, "base_layer")
                    )
                    if is_lin_like:
                        for p in module.parameters():
                            p.requires_grad = False
                continue

            if name not in bit_allocations:
                continue

            target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", getattr(module, "linear", module))
            cls_name = module.__class__.__name__.lower()
            is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(module, "nf") or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
            has_lora = (hasattr(module, "lora_A") and module.lora_A is not None) or hasattr(
                module, "base_layer"
            )

            layer_bits = bit_allocations[name]
            num_params = target_mod.weight.numel()
            total_bits_weighted += layer_bits * num_params
            total_params_linear += num_params

            if self.use_ste:
                src_lora_r = 0
                src_lora_alpha = 1.0
                if (
                    hasattr(module, "lora_A")
                    and hasattr(module, "lora_B")
                    and module.lora_A is not None
                    and module.lora_B is not None
                ):
                    src_lA = getattr(module.lora_A, "weight", module.lora_A)
                    if hasattr(src_lA, "shape") and len(src_lA.shape) == 2:
                        src_lora_r = src_lA.shape[0]
                    src_lora_alpha = getattr(module, "lora_alpha", float(src_lora_r))

                coopq_layer = CoopQLinear.from_linear(
                    target_mod,
                    bits=layer_bits,
                    symmetric=self.symmetric,
                    per_channel=self.per_channel,
                    lora_r=src_lora_r,
                    lora_alpha=src_lora_alpha,
                )
                new_layer = coopq_layer
                compressed_layer = new_layer
                # Preserve existing LoRA adapter weights if the source module has them
                if (
                    hasattr(module, "lora_A")
                    and hasattr(module, "lora_B")
                    and module.lora_A is not None
                    and module.lora_B is not None
                ):
                    src_lA = getattr(module.lora_A, "weight", module.lora_A)
                    src_lB = getattr(module.lora_B, "weight", module.lora_B)
                    if hasattr(new_layer, "lora_A") and new_layer.lora_A is not None:
                        if hasattr(src_lA, "data") and src_lA.shape == new_layer.lora_A.shape:
                            new_layer.lora_A.data.copy_(src_lA.data)
                    if hasattr(new_layer, "lora_B") and new_layer.lora_B is not None:
                        if hasattr(src_lB, "data") and src_lB.shape == new_layer.lora_B.shape:
                            new_layer.lora_B.data.copy_(src_lB.data)

                if hasattr(module, "base_layer"):
                    module.base_layer = compressed_layer  # put compressed layer inside wrapper
                    # module itself (LoRALinear wrapper) remains the replacement
                    new_module = module
                else:
                    new_module = compressed_layer

                if not hasattr(module, "base_layer"):
                    if name == "":
                        self.model = new_module
                    else:
                        replace_module(self.model, name, new_module)
            else:
                raw_w = target_mod.weight.data
                W = raw_w.t().float() if is_conv1d else raw_w.float()
                W_quant = quantize_dequantize_coopq(
                    W,
                    bits=layer_bits,
                    symmetric=self.symmetric,
                    per_channel=self.per_channel,
                    use_ste=False,
                )
                if is_conv1d:
                    target_mod.weight.data.copy_(
                        W_quant.t().contiguous().to(dtype=raw_w.dtype, device=raw_w.device)
                    )
                else:
                    target_mod.weight.data.copy_(
                        W_quant.contiguous().to(dtype=raw_w.dtype, device=raw_w.device)
                    )

                if target_mod.bias is not None:
                    b_quant = quantize_dequantize_coopq(
                        target_mod.bias.data.float(),
                        bits=layer_bits,
                        symmetric=self.symmetric,
                        per_channel=False,
                        use_ste=False,
                    )
                    target_mod.bias.data.copy_(
                        b_quant.to(dtype=target_mod.bias.dtype, device=target_mod.bias.device)
                    )

            # Preserve pre-existing LoRA adapter parameters and trainability
            if has_lora:
                for p_name, p in module.named_parameters():
                    if any(t in p_name.lower() for t in ("lora_a", "lora_b", "adapter")):
                        p.requires_grad = True

            compressed_layers.append(name)

        # LayerNorm Recalibration
        if self.recalibrate_ln:
            recalibrate_layernorms(
                self.model,
                tokenizer=self.tokenizer,
                calibration_loader=self.calibration_loader,
                calibration_texts=self.calibration_texts,
                device=self.target_device,
            )

        avg_bits = (
            total_bits_weighted / total_params_linear
            if total_params_linear > 0
            else float(self.bits)
        )
        effective_compression_rate = 16.0 / max(avg_bits, 1e-3)
        stats = self.get_compression_stats()
        stats.update(
            {
                "target_bits": self.bits,
                "min_bits_floor": self.min_bits,
                "average_allocated_bits": round(avg_bits, 2),
                "layer_bit_allocations": bit_allocations,
                "compressed_layers": compressed_layers,
                "compressed_layer_count": len(compressed_layers),
                "fallback_layers": fallback_layers,
                "fallback_layer_count": len(fallback_layers),
                "symmetric": self.symmetric,
                "per_channel": self.per_channel,
                "use_ste": self.use_ste,
                "target_attention_only": self.target_attention_only,
                "effective_compression_rate": round(effective_compression_rate, 2),
                "recalibrate_ln": self.recalibrate_ln,
                "method": "coopq_asymmetric_ste_bmin4",
                "theoretical_vram_saving": f"{(1.0 - avg_bits / 16.0) * 100:.1f}% vs FP16",
            }
        )
        self.stats = stats
        return self.model, stats

CoopQ: Cooperative Game-Theoretic Bit Allocation Quantization. Allocates discrete bit-widths (>= 4 bits) across transformer layers proportional to layer sensitivity (Frobenius energy / variance), guaranteeing a target mean bit-budget under strict distortion minimization.

Args
-----=
model
PyTorch model.
bits
Target average bit-width (default 4).
min_bits
Minimum allowable bit-width per layer (floor enforced at >= 4).
max_bits
Maximum allowable bit-width per layer (default 8).
symmetric
Whether to use symmetric quantization (default False for asymmetric calibration).
per_channel
Whether to use per-channel scale factors (default True).
use_ste
Whether to substitute nn.Linear with CoopQLinear for QAT (default True).
target_attention_only
If True, only quantize attention projection layers (skip FFN/MLP).
cfg
Optional configuration dictionary, namespace, or object.

def compress(self) -> tuple[nn.Module, dict[str, Any]]:
    """Quantize linear layers with layer-specific bit allocation (>= 4 bits) and per-channel asymmetric calibration."""
    sensitivities = self._calculate_layer_sensitivities()
    bit_allocations = self._allocate_bits(sensitivities)

    total_bits_weighted = 0
    total_params_linear = 0
    compressed_layers = []
    fallback_layers = []

    for name, module in list(self.model.named_modules()):
        if not self._is_target_module(name, module):
            # When target_attention_only is True, unselected FFN/MLP layers must be frozen during fine-tuning
            if self.target_attention_only:
                is_lin_like = (
                    isinstance(module, nn.Linear)
                    or "conv1d" in module.__class__.__name__.lower()
                    or hasattr(module, "base_layer")
                )
                if is_lin_like:
                    for p in module.parameters():
                        p.requires_grad = False
            continue

        if name not in bit_allocations:
            continue

        target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", getattr(module, "linear", module))
        cls_name = module.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(module, "nf") or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
        has_lora = (hasattr(module, "lora_A") and module.lora_A is not None) or hasattr(
            module, "base_layer"
        )

        layer_bits = bit_allocations[name]
        num_params = target_mod.weight.numel()
        total_bits_weighted += layer_bits * num_params
        total_params_linear += num_params

        if self.use_ste:
            src_lora_r = 0
            src_lora_alpha = 1.0
            if (
                hasattr(module, "lora_A")
                and hasattr(module, "lora_B")
                and module.lora_A is not None
                and module.lora_B is not None
            ):
                src_lA = getattr(module.lora_A, "weight", module.lora_A)
                if hasattr(src_lA, "shape") and len(src_lA.shape) == 2:
                    src_lora_r = src_lA.shape[0]
                src_lora_alpha = getattr(module, "lora_alpha", float(src_lora_r))

            coopq_layer = CoopQLinear.from_linear(
                target_mod,
                bits=layer_bits,
                symmetric=self.symmetric,
                per_channel=self.per_channel,
                lora_r=src_lora_r,
                lora_alpha=src_lora_alpha,
            )
            new_layer = coopq_layer
            compressed_layer = new_layer
            # Preserve existing LoRA adapter weights if the source module has them
            if (
                hasattr(module, "lora_A")
                and hasattr(module, "lora_B")
                and module.lora_A is not None
                and module.lora_B is not None
            ):
                src_lA = getattr(module.lora_A, "weight", module.lora_A)
                src_lB = getattr(module.lora_B, "weight", module.lora_B)
                if hasattr(new_layer, "lora_A") and new_layer.lora_A is not None:
                    if hasattr(src_lA, "data") and src_lA.shape == new_layer.lora_A.shape:
                        new_layer.lora_A.data.copy_(src_lA.data)
                if hasattr(new_layer, "lora_B") and new_layer.lora_B is not None:
                    if hasattr(src_lB, "data") and src_lB.shape == new_layer.lora_B.shape:
                        new_layer.lora_B.data.copy_(src_lB.data)

            if hasattr(module, "base_layer"):
                module.base_layer = compressed_layer  # put compressed layer inside wrapper
                # module itself (LoRALinear wrapper) remains the replacement
                new_module = module
            else:
                new_module = compressed_layer

            if not hasattr(module, "base_layer"):
                if name == "":
                    self.model = new_module
                else:
                    replace_module(self.model, name, new_module)
        else:
            raw_w = target_mod.weight.data
            W = raw_w.t().float() if is_conv1d else raw_w.float()
            W_quant = quantize_dequantize_coopq(
                W,
                bits=layer_bits,
                symmetric=self.symmetric,
                per_channel=self.per_channel,
                use_ste=False,
            )
            if is_conv1d:
                target_mod.weight.data.copy_(
                    W_quant.t().contiguous().to(dtype=raw_w.dtype, device=raw_w.device)
                )
            else:
                target_mod.weight.data.copy_(
                    W_quant.contiguous().to(dtype=raw_w.dtype, device=raw_w.device)
                )

            if target_mod.bias is not None:
                b_quant = quantize_dequantize_coopq(
                    target_mod.bias.data.float(),
                    bits=layer_bits,
                    symmetric=self.symmetric,
                    per_channel=False,
                    use_ste=False,
                )
                target_mod.bias.data.copy_(
                    b_quant.to(dtype=target_mod.bias.dtype, device=target_mod.bias.device)
                )

        # Preserve pre-existing LoRA adapter parameters and trainability
        if has_lora:
            for p_name, p in module.named_parameters():
                if any(t in p_name.lower() for t in ("lora_a", "lora_b", "adapter")):
                    p.requires_grad = True

        compressed_layers.append(name)

    # LayerNorm Recalibration
    if self.recalibrate_ln:
        recalibrate_layernorms(
            self.model,
            tokenizer=self.tokenizer,
            calibration_loader=self.calibration_loader,
            calibration_texts=self.calibration_texts,
            device=self.target_device,
        )

    avg_bits = (
        total_bits_weighted / total_params_linear
        if total_params_linear > 0
        else float(self.bits)
    )
    effective_compression_rate = 16.0 / max(avg_bits, 1e-3)
    stats = self.get_compression_stats()
    stats.update(
        {
            "target_bits": self.bits,
            "min_bits_floor": self.min_bits,
            "average_allocated_bits": round(avg_bits, 2),
            "layer_bit_allocations": bit_allocations,
            "compressed_layers": compressed_layers,
            "compressed_layer_count": len(compressed_layers),
            "fallback_layers": fallback_layers,
            "fallback_layer_count": len(fallback_layers),
            "symmetric": self.symmetric,
            "per_channel": self.per_channel,
            "use_ste": self.use_ste,
            "target_attention_only": self.target_attention_only,
            "effective_compression_rate": round(effective_compression_rate, 2),
            "recalibrate_ln": self.recalibrate_ln,
            "method": "coopq_asymmetric_ste_bmin4",
            "theoretical_vram_saving": f"{(1.0 - avg_bits / 16.0) * 100:.1f}% vs FP16",
        }
    )
    self.stats = stats
    return self.model, stats

Quantize linear layers with layer-specific bit allocation (>= 4 bits) and per-channel asymmetric calibration.

class CoopQSTEFakeQuantize (*args, **kwargs)
Expand source code 
class CoopQSTEFakeQuantize(torch.autograd.Function):
    """
    Straight-Through Estimator (STE) fake-quantization autograd function for CoopQ.
    Forwards per-channel quantized-dequantized weights and passes gradients through on backward pass.
    """

    @staticmethod
    def forward(
        ctx: Any,
        x: torch.Tensor,
        scale: torch.Tensor,
        zero_point: torch.Tensor,
        qmin: float,
        qmax: float,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        ctx.save_for_backward(scale, zero_point)
        ctx.qmin = qmin
        ctx.qmax = qmax

        # Quantize and clamp
        q = torch.clamp(torch.round(x / scale) + zero_point, qmin, qmax)
        # De-quantize
        dq = (q - zero_point) * scale
        return dq

    @staticmethod
    def backward(
        ctx: Any, grad_output: torch.Tensor
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, None, None, None, None]:
        # STE passes gradient uninhibited to parameter tensor
        grad_x = grad_output.clone()
        return grad_x, None, None, None, None

Straight-Through Estimator (STE) fake-quantization autograd function for CoopQ. Forwards per-channel quantized-dequantized weights and passes gradients through on backward pass.

Define a formula for differentiating the operation with backward mode automatic differentiation.

This function is to be overridden by all subclasses. (Defining this function is equivalent to defining the vjp function.)

It must accept a context :attr:ctx as the first argument, followed by as many outputs as the :func:forward returned (None will be passed in for non tensor outputs of the forward function), and it should return as many tensors, as there were inputs to :func:forward. Each argument is the gradient w.r.t the given output, and each returned value should be the gradient w.r.t. the corresponding input. If an input is not a Tensor or is a Tensor not requiring grads, you can just pass None as a gradient for that input.

The context can be used to retrieve tensors saved during the forward pass. It also has an attribute :attr:ctx.needs_input_grad as a tuple of booleans representing whether each input needs gradient. E.g., :func:backward will have ctx.needs_input_grad[0] = True if the first input to :func:forward needs gradient computed w.r.t. the output.

@staticmethod
def forward(
    ctx: Any,
    x: torch.Tensor,
    scale: torch.Tensor,
    zero_point: torch.Tensor,
    qmin: float,
    qmax: float,
) -> torch.Tensor:
    ctx.save_for_backward(scale, zero_point)
    ctx.qmin = qmin
    ctx.qmax = qmax

    # Quantize and clamp
    q = torch.clamp(torch.round(x / scale) + zero_point, qmin, qmax)
    # De-quantize
    dq = (q - zero_point) * scale
    return dq

Define the forward of the custom autograd Function.

This function is to be overridden by all subclasses. There are two ways to define forward:

Usage 1 (Combined forward and ctx)::

@staticmethod
def forward(ctx: Any, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
    pass
  • It must accept a context ctx as the first argument, followed by any number of arguments (tensors or other types).
  • See :ref:combining-forward-context for more details

Usage 2 (Separate forward and ctx)::

@staticmethod
def forward(*args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
    pass


@staticmethod
def setup_context(ctx: Any, inputs: Tuple[Any, ...], output: Any) -> None:
    pass
  • The forward no longer accepts a ctx argument.
  • Instead, you must also override the :meth:torch.autograd.Function.setup_context staticmethod to handle setting up the ctx object. output is the output of the forward, inputs are a Tuple of inputs to the forward.
  • See :ref:extending-autograd for more details

The context can be used to store arbitrary data that can be then retrieved during the backward pass. Tensors should not be stored directly on ctx (though this is not currently enforced for backward compatibility). Instead, tensors should be saved either with :func:ctx.save_for_backward if they are intended to be used in backward (equivalently, vjp) or :func:ctx.save_for_forward if they are intended to be used for in jvp.

Expand source code 
class FAQLinear(nn.Module):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True
    """Linear with FAQ activation-aware quantization, STE backprop and optional LoRA."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        in_features: int,
        out_features: int,
        bias: bool = True,
        bits: int = 8,
        symmetric: bool = True,
        lora_r: int = 0,
        lora_alpha: float = 1.0,
        device: torch.device | None = None,
        dtype: torch.dtype | None = None,
    ):
        super().__init__()
        self.in_features = in_features
        self.out_features = out_features
        self.nf = out_features  # Conv1D compat
        self.bits = bits
        self.symmetric = symmetric
        self.lora_r = int(lora_r)
        self.lora_alpha = float(lora_alpha)
        self.scaling = (self.lora_alpha / self.lora_r) if self.lora_r > 0 else 1.0

        requires_grad = not (self.lora_r > 0)
        self.weight = nn.Parameter(
            torch.empty(out_features, in_features, device=device, dtype=dtype),
            requires_grad=requires_grad,
        )
        if bias:
            self.bias = nn.Parameter(
                torch.empty(out_features, device=device, dtype=dtype),
                requires_grad=requires_grad,
            )
        else:
            self.register_parameter("bias", None)

        self.register_buffer(
            "act_scale",
            torch.ones(in_features, device=device, dtype=dtype),
            persistent=False,  # FQ-FIX-03
        )
        nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.weight, a=5**0.5)
        if self.bias is not None:
            nn.init.zeros_(self.bias)

        # FQ-FIX-01: LoRA in base dtype.
        if self.lora_r > 0:
            self.lora_A = nn.Parameter(
                torch.empty((self.lora_r, in_features), dtype=dtype, device=device)
            )
            self.lora_B = nn.Parameter(
                torch.zeros((out_features, self.lora_r), dtype=dtype, device=device)
            )
            with torch.no_grad():
                tmp = self.lora_A.data.float()
                nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(tmp, a=5**0.5)
                self.lora_A.data.copy_(tmp.to(dtype))
        else:
            self.register_parameter("lora_A", None)
            self.register_parameter("lora_B", None)

    @classmethod
    def from_linear(
        cls,
        module: nn.Linear | Any,
        act_scale: torch.Tensor | None = None,  # FQ-FIX-11: optional
        bits: int = 8,
        symmetric: bool = True,
        lora_r: int = 0,
        lora_alpha: float = 1.0,
    ) -> FAQLinear:
        target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
        raw_w = target_mod.weight.data
        if is_conv1d:
            in_f, out_f = raw_w.shape[0], raw_w.shape[1]
            W = raw_w.t().contiguous()
        else:
            out_f, in_f = raw_w.shape
            W = raw_w

        eff_lora_r = lora_r
        eff_lora_alpha = lora_alpha
        if hasattr(module, "lora_A") and hasattr(module, "lora_B"):
            r_attr = getattr(module, "r", None)
            if r_attr is not None and int(r_attr) > 0:
                eff_lora_r = int(r_attr)
            else:
                la = getattr(module, "lora_A", None)
                if la is not None and hasattr(la, "weight") and la.weight is not None:
                    eff_lora_r = int(la.weight.shape[0])
            eff_lora_alpha = float(getattr(module, "lora_alpha", eff_lora_alpha))

        layer = cls(
            in_features=in_f,
            out_features=out_f,
            bias=(getattr(target_mod, "bias", None) is not None and target_mod.bias is not None),
            bits=bits,
            symmetric=symmetric,
            lora_r=eff_lora_r,
            lora_alpha=eff_lora_alpha,
            device=W.device,
            dtype=W.dtype,
        )
        with torch.no_grad():
            layer.weight.data.copy_(W)
            if (
                getattr(target_mod, "bias", None) is not None
                and target_mod.bias is not None
                and layer.bias is not None
            ):
                layer.bias.data.copy_(target_mod.bias.data)

            # FQ-FIX-11: act_scale optional and shape-safe.
            if act_scale is not None:
                s = act_scale.detach().view(-1)[:in_f].to(device=W.device, dtype=W.dtype)
                n = min(layer.act_scale.numel(), s.numel())
                layer.act_scale[:n].copy_(s[:n])

            # Copy existing LoRA weights.
            if (
                hasattr(module, "lora_A")
                and hasattr(module, "lora_B")
                and layer.lora_A is not None
                and layer.lora_B is not None
            ):
                try:
                    la = getattr(module.lora_A, "weight", module.lora_A)
                    lb = getattr(module.lora_B, "weight", module.lora_B)
                    if hasattr(la, "data") and la.data.shape == layer.lora_A.data.shape:
                        layer.lora_A.data.copy_(la.data)
                    if hasattr(lb, "data") and lb.data.shape == layer.lora_B.data.shape:
                        layer.lora_B.data.copy_(lb.data)
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug("FAQ LoRA copy failed: %s", e)

        return layer

    def get_weight(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        s = (
            torch.clamp(self.act_scale, min=1e-6)
            .unsqueeze(0)
            .to(device=self.weight.device, dtype=self.weight.dtype)
        )
        w_scaled = self.weight * s
        w_q = quantize_dequantize_tensor(
            w_scaled,
            bits=self.bits,
            symmetric=self.symmetric,
            per_channel=True,
            use_ste=False,
        )
        return w_q / s

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        s = (
            torch.clamp(self.act_scale, min=1e-6)
            .unsqueeze(0)
            .to(device=self.weight.device, dtype=self.weight.dtype)
        )
        w_scaled = self.weight * s
        w_q = quantize_dequantize_tensor(
            w_scaled,
            bits=self.bits,
            symmetric=self.symmetric,
            per_channel=True,
            use_ste=self.training,
        )
        w_restored = w_q / s

        b_q = None
        if self.bias is not None:
            # FQ-FIX-07: same STE flag as weight.
            b_q = quantize_dequantize_tensor(
                self.bias,
                bits=self.bits,
                symmetric=self.symmetric,
                per_channel=False,
                use_ste=self.training,
            )

        out = nn.functional.linear(
            x,
            w_restored.to(dtype=x.dtype, device=x.device),
            b_q.to(dtype=x.dtype, device=x.device) if b_q is not None else None,
        )

        if self.lora_r > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
            # FQ-FIX-08: LoRA scaling matches PEFT convention.
            lora_out = nn.functional.linear(nn.functional.linear(x, self.lora_A), self.lora_B)
            out = out + self.scaling * lora_out

        return out

Base class for all neural network modules.

Your models should also subclass this class.

Modules can also contain other Modules, allowing them to be nested in a tree structure. You can assign the submodules as regular attributes::

import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F


class Model(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.conv1 = nn.Conv2d(1, 20, 5)
        self.conv2 = nn.Conv2d(20, 20, 5)

    def forward(self, x):
        x = F.relu(self.conv1(x))
        return F.relu(self.conv2(x))

Submodules assigned in this way will be registered, and will also have their parameters converted when you call :meth:to, etc.

Note

As per the example above, an __init__() call to the parent class must be made before assignment on the child.

:ivar training: Boolean represents whether this module is in training or evaluation mode. :vartype training: bool

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

Ancestors

  • torch.nn.modules.module.Module

def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    s = (
        torch.clamp(self.act_scale, min=1e-6)
        .unsqueeze(0)
        .to(device=self.weight.device, dtype=self.weight.dtype)
    )

    w_q = quantize_dequantize_tensor(
        w_scaled,
        bits=self.bits,
        symmetric=self.symmetric,
        per_channel=True,
        use_ste=self.training,
    )
    w_restored = w_q / s

    b_q = None
    if self.bias is not None:
        # FQ-FIX-07: same STE flag as weight.
        b_q = quantize_dequantize_tensor(
            self.bias,
            bits=self.bits,
            symmetric=self.symmetric,
            per_channel=False,
            use_ste=self.training,
        )

    out = nn.functional.linear(
        x,
        w_restored.to(dtype=x.dtype, device=x.device),
        b_q.to(dtype=x.dtype, device=x.device) if b_q is not None else None,
    )

        lora_out = nn.functional.linear(nn.functional.linear(x, self.lora_A), self.lora_B)
        out = out + self.scaling * lora_out

    return out

Define the computation performed at every call.

Should be overridden by all subclasses.

Note

Although the recipe for forward pass needs to be defined within this function, one should call the :class:Module instance afterwards instead of this since the former takes care of running the registered hooks while the latter silently ignores them.

@COMPRESSOR_REGISTRY.register("faq_quantizer")
class FAQQuantizer(BaseCompressor):
    """
    FAQQuantizer: Feature-Activation-Aware Quantization.

    Collects activation statistics to scale channel-wise quantization step sizes,
    minimizing activation-weighted reconstruction distortion.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        model: nn.Module,
        tokenizer: Any | None = None,
        bits: int = 8,
        calibration_texts: list[str] | None = None,
        calibration_data: Any | None = None,
        alpha: float = 0.5,
        symmetric: bool = True,
        use_ste: bool = True,
        lora_r: int = 16,
        lora_alpha: float = 1.0,
        max_error: float = 0.35,
        target_attention_only: bool = False,
        fallback_to_original: bool = True,
        skip_attention_qkv: bool = False,
        cfg: Any | None = None,
        num_calibration_batches: int = 8,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(model, name="faq", **kwargs)
        cfg = cfg or kwargs.get("comp_config")
        self.tokenizer = tokenizer
        self.bits = int(_cfg_get(cfg, "faq_bits", _cfg_get(cfg, "bits", bits)))
        self.calibration_texts = calibration_texts
        self.calibration_data = calibration_data
        self.alpha = float(_cfg_get(cfg, "faq_alpha", _cfg_get(cfg, "alpha", alpha)))
        self.symmetric = bool(_cfg_get(cfg, "faq_symmetric", _cfg_get(cfg, "symmetric", symmetric)))
        self.use_ste = bool(_cfg_get(cfg, "faq_use_ste", _cfg_get(cfg, "use_ste", use_ste)))
        self.lora_r = int(_cfg_get(cfg, "lora_r", _cfg_get(cfg, "lora_rank", lora_r)))
        self.lora_alpha = float(_cfg_get(cfg, "lora_alpha", lora_alpha))
        self.max_error = float(
            _cfg_get(cfg, "faq_max_error", _cfg_get(cfg, "max_error", max_error))
        )
        self.target_attention_only = bool(
            _cfg_get(
                cfg,
                "faq_target_attention_only",
                _cfg_get(cfg, "target_attention_only", target_attention_only),
            )
        )
        self.fallback_to_original = bool(
            _cfg_get(
                cfg,
                "faq_fallback_to_original",
                _cfg_get(cfg, "fallback_to_original", fallback_to_original),
            )
        )
        self.skip_attention_qkv = bool(_cfg_get(cfg, "skip_attention_qkv", skip_attention_qkv))
        self.cfg = cfg
        self.num_calibration_batches = max(
            1, int(_cfg_get(cfg, "num_calibration_batches", num_calibration_batches))
        )

        self.compressed_layers: list[str] = []
        self.fallback_layers: list[str] = []
        self.skipped_layers: list[str] = []
        self.layer_errors: dict[str, float] = {}
        self._compressed_params: int = 0  # FQ-FIX-10
        self._injected: bool = False  # FQ-FIX-14

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Target selection
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _is_gqa_asymmetric(self, name: str, module: nn.Module) -> bool:
        if not self.skip_attention_qkv:
            return False
        cfg = getattr(self.model, "config", None)
        num_heads = getattr(cfg, "num_attention_heads", None)
        num_kv_heads = getattr(cfg, "num_key_value_heads", None)
        if num_heads is not None and num_kv_heads is not None and num_kv_heads < num_heads:
            name_lower = name.lower()
            if any(
                p in name_lower
                for p in [
                    "q_proj",
                    "k_proj",
                    "v_proj",
                    "query",
                    "key",
                    "value",
                    "c_attn",
                ]
            ):
                return True
        return False

    def _is_target_module(self, name: str, module: nn.Module) -> bool:
        name_lower = name.lower()
        if any(part in name.split(".") for part in ["lora_A", "lora_B", "base_layer"]):
            return False
        if any(
            skip in name_lower
            for skip in [
                "classifier",
                "score",
                "lm_head",
                "pooler",
                "embedding",
                "router",
                "gate",
                "head",
                "output_layer",
            ]
        ):
            return False

        target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_linear_like = (
            isinstance(target_mod, nn.Linear) or "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
        )
        if not is_linear_like or isinstance(target_mod, FAQLinear):
            return False

        if not should_compress_linear(self.model, name):
            return False

        if self.target_attention_only:
            last_part = name.split(".")[-1]
            is_attn = (last_part in _ATTN_NAMES) or any(
                k in name_lower for k in ("attn", "attention")
            )
            if not is_attn:
                return False

        return True

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # FQ-FIX-12: per-feature E[x^2] accumulation (diagonal of covariance).
    # FQ-FIX-13: DataLoader support.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def _normalize_calibration_batches(data: Any) -> list[Any]:
        """FQ-FIX-13: convert DataLoader / list / tensor / dict into a list of batches."""
        if data is None:
            return []
        if isinstance(data, torch.Tensor):
            return [data]
        if isinstance(data, dict):
            return [data]
        if isinstance(data, (list, tuple)):
            return list(data)
        if hasattr(data, "__iter__"):
            return list(data)
        return [data]

    def _collect_activation_scales(self) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
        """
        FQ-FIX-12: accumulate E[x^2] per input feature.
        This is the diagonal of the input covariance; sufficient for
        per-channel quantization.
        """
        running_sq: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
        sample_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
        hooks = []

        def make_hook(layer_name: str):
            def hook(module, inp, outp):
                if not inp or not isinstance(inp[0], torch.Tensor):
                    return
                x = inp[0].detach()
                target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
                cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
                is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
                in_f = target_mod.weight.shape[0] if is_conv1d else target_mod.weight.shape[1]

                x = x.reshape(-1, in_f).float()
                n = x.shape[0]
                if n == 0:
                    return

                sq = (x * x).sum(dim=0).cpu()  # (in_f,)
                if layer_name not in running_sq:
                    running_sq[layer_name] = sq
                    sample_counts[layer_name] = n
                else:
                    running_sq[layer_name] += sq
                    sample_counts[layer_name] += n

            return hook

        for name, module in self.model.named_modules():
            if self._is_target_module(name, module) and not self._is_gqa_asymmetric(name, module):
                hooks.append(module.register_forward_hook(make_hook(name)))

        self.model.eval()
        first_p = next(self.model.parameters(), None)
        dev = first_p.device if first_p is not None else torch.device("cpu")

        try:
            with torch.no_grad():
                if self.tokenizer is not None and self.calibration_texts:
                    bs = max(1, self.num_calibration_batches)
                    for idx in range(0, len(self.calibration_texts), bs):
                        sub = self.calibration_texts[idx : idx + bs]
                        if not sub:
                            break
                        try:
                            inputs = self.tokenizer(
                                sub,
                                return_tensors="pt",
                                padding=True,
                                truncation=True,
                                max_length=128,
                            )
                            inputs = {k: v.to(dev) for k, v in inputs.items()}
                            self.model(**inputs)
                        except Exception as e:
                            logger.debug("FAQ calibration batch failed: %s", e)

                elif self.calibration_data is not None:
                    batches = self._normalize_calibration_batches(self.calibration_data)
                    for i, batch in enumerate(batches):
                        if i >= self.num_calibration_batches:
                            break
                        try:
                            if isinstance(batch, torch.Tensor):
                                self.model(batch.to(dev))
                            elif isinstance(batch, dict):
                                b = {
                                    k: v.to(dev)
                                    for k, v in batch.items()
                                    if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor)
                                }
                                self.model(**b)
                            elif isinstance(batch, (list, tuple)):
                                self.model(
                                    *[
                                        b.to(dev) if isinstance(b, torch.Tensor) else b
                                        for b in batch
                                    ]
                                )
                        except Exception as e:
                            logger.debug("FAQ calibration batch %d failed: %s", i, e)
                else:
                    first_linear = next(
                        (m for m in self.model.modules() if isinstance(m, nn.Linear)),
                        None,
                    )
                    if first_linear is not None:
                        dummy = torch.randn(2, 16, first_linear.in_features, device=dev)
                        try:
                            self.model(dummy)
                        except Exception as e:
                            logger.debug("FAQ fallback forward failed: %s", e)
        finally:
            for h in hooks:
                h.remove()

        act_scales: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
        for name, module in self.model.named_modules():
            target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
            cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
            is_linear_like = (
                isinstance(target_mod, nn.Linear)
                or "conv1d" in cls_name
                or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
            )
            if is_linear_like and hasattr(target_mod, "weight") and target_mod.weight is not None:
                is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
                in_f = target_mod.weight.shape[0] if is_conv1d else target_mod.weight.shape[1]
                if name in running_sq and sample_counts.get(name, 0) > 0:
                    mean_sq = running_sq[name] / float(sample_counts[name])
                    channel_scale = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(mean_sq, min=1e-6))
                    channel_scale = channel_scale / channel_scale.mean().clamp(min=1e-6)
                    act_scales[name] = channel_scale
                else:
                    act_scales[name] = torch.ones(in_f)
        return act_scales

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Main compress
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def compress(self) -> tuple[nn.Module, dict[str, Any]]:
        # FQ-FIX-14: double-injection guard.
        if self._injected:
            logger.warning("FAQQuantizer.compress called twice; returning current stats.")
            try:
                return self.model, self.get_compression_stats()
            except Exception:
                return self.model, {}

        act_scales = self._collect_activation_scales()
        self.compressed_layers = []
        self.fallback_layers = []
        self.skipped_layers = []
        self.layer_errors = {}
        seen_weights: set = set()

        # Mark non-target attention as skipped when target_attention_only is set.
        if self.target_attention_only:
            for name, module in self.model.named_modules():
                if any(part in name.split(".") for part in ["lora_A", "lora_B", "base_layer"]):
                    continue
                target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
                cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
                is_linear_like = (
                    isinstance(target_mod, nn.Linear)
                    or "conv1d" in cls_name
                    or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
                )
                if is_linear_like and not self._is_target_module(name, module):
                    for p in module.parameters():
                        p.requires_grad = False
                    self.skipped_layers.append(name)

        for name, module in list(self.model.named_modules()):
            if not self._is_target_module(name, module):
                continue

            target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
            if not hasattr(target_mod, "weight") or target_mod.weight is None:
                continue

            w_id = id(target_mod.weight)
            if w_id in seen_weights:
                self.skipped_layers.append(name)
                continue
            seen_weights.add(w_id)

            cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
            is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
            raw_w = target_mod.weight.data
            W_orig = raw_w.t().float() if is_conv1d else raw_w.float()
            in_f = W_orig.shape[1]

            s_act = act_scales.get(name, torch.ones(in_f)).to(raw_w.device)
            s_pow = torch.pow(torch.clamp(s_act, min=1e-6), self.alpha).unsqueeze(0)

            try:
                if self.use_ste or self.lora_r > 0:
                    faq_layer = FAQLinear.from_linear(
                        module,
                        act_scale=s_act,
                        bits=self.bits,
                        symmetric=self.symmetric,
                        lora_r=self.lora_r,
                        lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
                    )
                    W_recon = faq_layer.get_weight().float()
                    W_norm = torch.norm(W_orig, p="fro").clamp_min(1e-8)
                    err = float(torch.norm(W_orig - W_recon, p="fro").item() / W_norm)
                    self.layer_errors[name] = err

                    if err > self.max_error and self.fallback_to_original:
                        self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                        for p in module.parameters():
                            p.requires_grad = False
                        continue

                    replace_module(self.model, name, faq_layer)
                    self.compressed_layers.append(name)
                else:
                    W_scaled = W_orig * s_pow
                    W_q = quantize_dequantize_tensor(
                        W_scaled,
                        bits=self.bits,
                        symmetric=self.symmetric,
                        per_channel=True,
                        use_ste=False,
                    )
                    W_recon = W_q / s_pow
                    W_norm = torch.norm(W_orig, p="fro").clamp_min(1e-8)
                    err = float(torch.norm(W_orig - W_recon, p="fro").item() / W_norm)
                    self.layer_errors[name] = err

                    if err > self.max_error and self.fallback_to_original:
                        self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                        for p in module.parameters():
                            p.requires_grad = False
                        continue

                    # FQ-FIX-02: no_grad mutation.
                    with torch.no_grad():
                        if is_conv1d:
                            target_mod.weight.data.copy_(W_recon.t().to(dtype=raw_w.dtype))
                        else:
                            target_mod.weight.data.copy_(W_recon.to(dtype=raw_w.dtype))

                        if (
                            getattr(target_mod, "bias", None) is not None
                            and target_mod.bias is not None
                        ):
                            b_q = quantize_dequantize_tensor(
                                target_mod.bias.data,
                                bits=self.bits,
                                symmetric=self.symmetric,
                                per_channel=False,
                                use_ste=False,
                            )
                            target_mod.bias.data.copy_(b_q.to(dtype=target_mod.bias.dtype))

                        if not hasattr(target_mod, "act_scale"):
                            target_mod.register_buffer(
                                "act_scale",
                                s_act.to(dtype=raw_w.dtype, device=raw_w.device),
                                persistent=False,
                            )

                    # Preserve LoRA trainability.
                    if hasattr(module, "lora_A") and hasattr(module, "lora_B"):
                        if hasattr(module.lora_A, "weight") and module.lora_A.weight is not None:
                            module.lora_A.weight.requires_grad = True
                        if hasattr(module.lora_B, "weight") and module.lora_B.weight is not None:
                            module.lora_B.weight.requires_grad = True

                    self.compressed_layers.append(name)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning("FAQ quantization failed on %s: %s", name, e)
                self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                for p in module.parameters():
                    p.requires_grad = False

        # FQ-FIX-04: forward sanity check with attention_mask.
        forward_check_success = True
        try:
            with torch.no_grad():
                dev = next(self.model.parameters(), torch.tensor(0.0)).device
                dummy = torch.randint(0, 100, (1, 8), device=dev, dtype=torch.long)
                attn = torch.ones_like(dummy)
                try:
                    self.model(input_ids=dummy, attention_mask=attn)
                except Exception:
                    try:
                        self.model(dummy)
                    except Exception:
                        first_linear = next(
                            (
                                m
                                for m in self.model.modules()
                                if isinstance(m, (nn.Linear, FAQLinear))
                            ),
                            None,
                        )
                        in_dim = getattr(first_linear, "in_features", 8)
                        first_p = next(self.model.parameters(), None)
                        self.model(
                            torch.randn(
                                1,
                                8,
                                in_dim,
                                device=dev,
                                dtype=(first_p.dtype if first_p is not None else torch.float32),
                            )
                        )
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("FAQ forward sanity check failed: %s", e)
            forward_check_success = False

        try:
            self._compressed_params = self._count_params(self.model)
        except Exception:
            self._compressed_params = sum(p.numel() for p in self.model.parameters())

        try:
            stats = self.get_compression_stats()
        except Exception:
            stats = {}

        avg_err = (
            (sum(self.layer_errors.values()) / len(self.layer_errors)) if self.layer_errors else 0.0
        )

        # FQ-FIX-05: effective compression reflects actual quantized layers.
        num_targets = len(self.compressed_layers) + len(self.fallback_layers)
        if num_targets > 0 and self.bits > 0:
            quantized_fraction = len(self.compressed_layers) / num_targets
            fp16_to_int_ratio = 16.0 / float(self.bits)
            effective_compression_rate = 1.0 + quantized_fraction * (fp16_to_int_ratio - 1.0)
        else:
            effective_compression_rate = 1.0

        stats.update(
            {
                "compressed_layers": self.compressed_layers,
                "compressed_layer_count": len(self.compressed_layers),
                "fallback_layers": self.fallback_layers,
                "fallback_layer_count": len(self.fallback_layers),
                "skipped_layers": self.skipped_layers,
                "skipped_layer_count": len(self.skipped_layers),
                "num_compressed": len(self.compressed_layers),
                "num_fallback": len(self.fallback_layers),
                "num_skipped": len(self.skipped_layers),
                "layer_errors": self.layer_errors,
                "avg_reconstruction_error": avg_err,
                "quantization_bits": self.bits,
                "alpha": self.alpha,
                "symmetric": self.symmetric,
                "use_ste": self.use_ste,
                "lora_r": self.lora_r,
                "lora_alpha": self.lora_alpha,
                "target_attention_only": self.target_attention_only,
                "forward_check_success": forward_check_success,
                "effective_compression_rate": round(effective_compression_rate, 2),
                "theoretical_vram_saving": f"{(1.0 - self.bits / 16.0) * 100:.1f}% vs FP16",
                "method": "faq_quantization",
            }
        )

        # FQ-FIX-14: mark as injected.
        self._injected = True
        return self.model, stats

FAQQuantizer: Feature-Activation-Aware Quantization.

Collects activation statistics to scale channel-wise quantization step sizes, minimizing activation-weighted reconstruction distortion.
