class HuffmanCoder
Expand source code 
class HuffmanCoder:
    """
    Vectorized lossless entropy coder for quantized weight and KV cache tensors.
    Supports continuous tensor quantization (e.g. 4-bit / 8-bit), symbol frequency modeling,
    binary prefix tree construction, numpy bitstream packing, and vectorized decoding.
    """

    @staticmethod
    def build_tree(frequencies: dict[int, int]) -> HuffmanNode | None:
        """Build Huffman binary prefix tree from symbol frequencies."""
        if not frequencies:
            return None

        heap: list[HuffmanNode] = [HuffmanNode(sym, freq) for sym, freq in frequencies.items()]
        heapq.heapify(heap)

        if len(heap) == 1:
            root = HuffmanNode(freq=heap[0].freq, left=heap[0])
            return root

        while len(heap) > 1:
            node1 = heapq.heappop(heap)
            node2 = heapq.heappop(heap)
            merged = HuffmanNode(freq=node1.freq + node2.freq, left=node1, right=node2)
            heapq.heappush(heap, merged)

        return heap[0]

    @staticmethod
    def generate_code_table(
        root: HuffmanNode | None,
        current_code: str = "",
        code_table: dict[int, str] | None = None,
    ) -> dict[int, str]:
        """Traverse Huffman tree to generate prefix bitstring per symbol."""
        if code_table is None:
            code_table = {}
        if root is None:
            return code_table

        if root.symbol is not None:
            code_table[root.symbol] = current_code if current_code else "0"
            return code_table

        if root.left is not None:
            HuffmanCoder.generate_code_table(root.left, current_code + "0", code_table)
        if root.right is not None:
            HuffmanCoder.generate_code_table(root.right, current_code + "1", code_table)

        return code_table

    @staticmethod
    def encode(
        tensor: torch.Tensor,
        quantize_bits: int | None = 8,
    ) -> tuple[bytes, dict[int, str], dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Vectorized encode input tensor into packed bitstream bytes and Huffman codebook.
        """
        flat = tensor.detach().float().view(-1)
        numel = flat.numel()
        if numel == 0:
            return b"", {}, {"total_elements": 0, "entropy": 0.0, "compressed_bytes": 0}

        min_val = flat.min().item()
        max_val = flat.max().item()

        # Handle high quantize_bits properly: skip quantization / identity mapping
        if quantize_bits is None or quantize_bits >= 16:
            if tensor.dtype == torch.bfloat16:
                raw_bytes = tensor.detach().cpu().view(torch.int16).numpy().tobytes()
            else:
                raw_bytes = tensor.detach().cpu().numpy().tobytes()
            metadata = {
                "scale": 1.0,
                "min_val": 0.0,
                "orig_shape": list(tensor.shape),
                "orig_dtype": str(tensor.dtype),
                "total_elements": numel,
                "entropy": 0.0,
                "pad_len": 0,
                "compressed_bytes": len(raw_bytes),
                "is_raw": True,
            }
            return raw_bytes, {}, metadata

        # Quantization
        num_levels = 2 ** int(quantize_bits)
        scale = (max_val - min_val) / max(num_levels - 1, 1)
        scale = max(scale, 1e-12)
        quantized = torch.clamp(torch.round((flat - min_val) / scale), 0, num_levels - 1).long()

        q_np = quantized.cpu().numpy()
        symbols, counts = np.unique(q_np, return_counts=True)
        frequencies = {int(s): int(c) for s, c in zip(symbols, counts)}

        root = HuffmanCoder.build_tree(frequencies)
        code_table = HuffmanCoder.generate_code_table(root)

        probs = counts / numel
        entropy = -float(np.sum(probs * np.log2(probs + 1e-12)))

        # Vectorized bitstream packing using LUT mapping and numpy.packbits
        max_sym = int(symbols.max())
        lut = [code_table.get(i, "0") for i in range(max_sym + 1)]
        bit_chunks = [lut[v] for v in q_np]
        bitstream_str = "".join(bit_chunks)
        pad_len = (8 - len(bitstream_str) % 8) % 8
        padded = bitstream_str + "0" * pad_len

        # Fast bit packing using np.packbits
        bit_arr = np.frombuffer(padded.encode("ascii"), dtype=np.uint8) - ord("0")
        packed_np = np.packbits(bit_arr)
        byte_array = packed_np.tobytes()

        metadata = {
            "scale": scale,
            "min_val": min_val,
            "orig_shape": list(tensor.shape),
            "orig_dtype": str(tensor.dtype),
            "total_elements": numel,
            "entropy": round(entropy, 3),
            "pad_len": pad_len,
            "compressed_bytes": len(byte_array),
            "is_raw": False,
        }
        return byte_array, code_table, metadata

    @staticmethod
    def decode(
        packed_bytes: bytes,
        code_table: dict[int, str],
        metadata: dict[str, Any],
        device: torch.device | None = None,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Vectorized decode bitstream back into reconstructed tensor."""
        total_elements = metadata.get("total_elements", 0)
        if total_elements == 0:
            return torch.empty(metadata.get("orig_shape", [0]), device=device)

        if metadata.get("is_raw", False):
            orig_dtype_str = metadata.get("orig_dtype", "torch.float32")
            target_dtype = getattr(torch, orig_dtype_str.replace("torch.", ""), torch.float32)
            if target_dtype == torch.bfloat16:
                arr = np.frombuffer(packed_bytes, dtype=np.int16)
                tensor_out = (
                    torch.from_numpy(arr.copy())
                    .view(torch.bfloat16)
                    .reshape(metadata.get("orig_shape"))
                )
            else:
                dtype_str = orig_dtype_str.replace("torch.", "")
                np_dtype = getattr(np, dtype_str if hasattr(np, dtype_str) else "float32")
                arr = np.frombuffer(packed_bytes, dtype=np_dtype)
                tensor_out = (
                    torch.from_numpy(arr.copy())
                    .reshape(metadata.get("orig_shape"))
                    .to(dtype=target_dtype)
                )
            return tensor_out.to(device=device)

        scale = metadata.get("scale", 1.0)
        min_val = metadata.get("min_val", 0.0)
        shape = metadata.get("orig_shape", [total_elements])

        # Vectorized unpacking of bits
        pad_len = metadata.get("pad_len", 0)
        raw_arr = np.frombuffer(packed_bytes, dtype=np.uint8)
        bits = np.unpackbits(raw_arr)
        if pad_len > 0:
            bits = bits[:-pad_len]

        # Fast flat-array binary trie traversal
        node_left = [-1]
        node_right = [-1]
        node_sym = [-1]

        for sym, code in code_table.items():
            curr = 0
            for b in code:
                if b == "0":
                    if node_left[curr] == -1:
                        node_left[curr] = len(node_left)
                        node_left.append(-1)
                        node_right.append(-1)
                        node_sym.append(-1)
                    curr = node_left[curr]
                else:
                    if node_right[curr] == -1:
                        node_right[curr] = len(node_right)
                        node_left.append(-1)
                        node_right.append(-1)
                        node_sym.append(-1)
                    curr = node_right[curr]
            node_sym[curr] = sym

        nl = np.array(node_left, dtype=np.int32)
        nr = np.array(node_right, dtype=np.int32)
        ns = np.array(node_sym, dtype=np.int32)

        decoded = np.empty(total_elements, dtype=np.float32)
        curr = 0
        idx = 0

        # Fast traversal loop
        for b in bits:
            curr = nr[curr] if b else nl[curr]
            sym = ns[curr]
            if sym != -1:
                decoded[idx] = sym
                idx += 1
                curr = 0
                if idx == total_elements:
                    break

        decoded = decoded * scale + min_val
        return torch.from_numpy(decoded).reshape(shape).to(device=device)

Vectorized lossless entropy coder for quantized weight and KV cache tensors. Supports continuous tensor quantization (e.g. 4-bit / 8-bit), symbol frequency modeling, binary prefix tree construction, numpy bitstream packing, and vectorized decoding.

def build_tree(frequencies: dict[int, int]) ‑> HuffmanNode | None
Expand source code 
@staticmethod
def build_tree(frequencies: dict[int, int]) -> HuffmanNode | None:
    """Build Huffman binary prefix tree from symbol frequencies."""
    if not frequencies:
        return None

    heap: list[HuffmanNode] = [HuffmanNode(sym, freq) for sym, freq in frequencies.items()]
    heapq.heapify(heap)

    if len(heap) == 1:
        root = HuffmanNode(freq=heap[0].freq, left=heap[0])
        return root

    while len(heap) > 1:
        node1 = heapq.heappop(heap)
        node2 = heapq.heappop(heap)
        merged = HuffmanNode(freq=node1.freq + node2.freq, left=node1, right=node2)
        heapq.heappush(heap, merged)

    return heap[0]

Build Huffman binary prefix tree from symbol frequencies.

def decode(packed_bytes: bytes,
code_table: dict[int, str],
metadata: dict[str, Any],
device: torch.device | None = None) ‑> torch.Tensor
Expand source code 
@staticmethod
def decode(
    packed_bytes: bytes,
    code_table: dict[int, str],
    metadata: dict[str, Any],
    device: torch.device | None = None,
) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Vectorized decode bitstream back into reconstructed tensor."""
    total_elements = metadata.get("total_elements", 0)
    if total_elements == 0:
        return torch.empty(metadata.get("orig_shape", [0]), device=device)

    if metadata.get("is_raw", False):
        orig_dtype_str = metadata.get("orig_dtype", "torch.float32")
        target_dtype = getattr(torch, orig_dtype_str.replace("torch.", ""), torch.float32)
        if target_dtype == torch.bfloat16:
            arr = np.frombuffer(packed_bytes, dtype=np.int16)
            tensor_out = (
                torch.from_numpy(arr.copy())
                .view(torch.bfloat16)
                .reshape(metadata.get("orig_shape"))
            )
        else:
            dtype_str = orig_dtype_str.replace("torch.", "")
            np_dtype = getattr(np, dtype_str if hasattr(np, dtype_str) else "float32")
            arr = np.frombuffer(packed_bytes, dtype=np_dtype)
            tensor_out = (
                torch.from_numpy(arr.copy())
                .reshape(metadata.get("orig_shape"))
                .to(dtype=target_dtype)
            )
        return tensor_out.to(device=device)

    scale = metadata.get("scale", 1.0)
    min_val = metadata.get("min_val", 0.0)
    shape = metadata.get("orig_shape", [total_elements])

    # Vectorized unpacking of bits
    pad_len = metadata.get("pad_len", 0)
    raw_arr = np.frombuffer(packed_bytes, dtype=np.uint8)
    bits = np.unpackbits(raw_arr)
    if pad_len > 0:
        bits = bits[:-pad_len]

    # Fast flat-array binary trie traversal
    node_left = [-1]
    node_right = [-1]
    node_sym = [-1]

    for sym, code in code_table.items():
        curr = 0
        for b in code:
            if b == "0":
                if node_left[curr] == -1:
                    node_left[curr] = len(node_left)
                    node_left.append(-1)
                    node_right.append(-1)
                    node_sym.append(-1)
                curr = node_left[curr]
            else:
                if node_right[curr] == -1:
                    node_right[curr] = len(node_right)
                    node_left.append(-1)
                    node_right.append(-1)
                    node_sym.append(-1)
                curr = node_right[curr]
        node_sym[curr] = sym

    nl = np.array(node_left, dtype=np.int32)
    nr = np.array(node_right, dtype=np.int32)
    ns = np.array(node_sym, dtype=np.int32)

    decoded = np.empty(total_elements, dtype=np.float32)
    curr = 0
    idx = 0

    # Fast traversal loop
    for b in bits:
        curr = nr[curr] if b else nl[curr]
        sym = ns[curr]
        if sym != -1:
            decoded[idx] = sym
            idx += 1
            curr = 0
            if idx == total_elements:
                break

    decoded = decoded * scale + min_val
    return torch.from_numpy(decoded).reshape(shape).to(device=device)

Vectorized decode bitstream back into reconstructed tensor.

def encode(tensor: torch.Tensor, quantize_bits: int | None = 8) ‑> tuple[bytes, dict[int, str], dict[str, typing.Any]]
Expand source code 
@staticmethod
def encode(
    tensor: torch.Tensor,
    quantize_bits: int | None = 8,
) -> tuple[bytes, dict[int, str], dict[str, Any]]:
    """
    Vectorized encode input tensor into packed bitstream bytes and Huffman codebook.
    """
    flat = tensor.detach().float().view(-1)
    numel = flat.numel()
    if numel == 0:
        return b"", {}, {"total_elements": 0, "entropy": 0.0, "compressed_bytes": 0}

    min_val = flat.min().item()
    max_val = flat.max().item()

    # Handle high quantize_bits properly: skip quantization / identity mapping
    if quantize_bits is None or quantize_bits >= 16:
        if tensor.dtype == torch.bfloat16:
            raw_bytes = tensor.detach().cpu().view(torch.int16).numpy().tobytes()
        else:
            raw_bytes = tensor.detach().cpu().numpy().tobytes()
        metadata = {
            "scale": 1.0,
            "min_val": 0.0,
            "orig_shape": list(tensor.shape),
            "orig_dtype": str(tensor.dtype),
            "total_elements": numel,
            "entropy": 0.0,
            "pad_len": 0,
            "compressed_bytes": len(raw_bytes),
            "is_raw": True,
        }
        return raw_bytes, {}, metadata

    # Quantization
    num_levels = 2 ** int(quantize_bits)
    scale = (max_val - min_val) / max(num_levels - 1, 1)
    scale = max(scale, 1e-12)
    quantized = torch.clamp(torch.round((flat - min_val) / scale), 0, num_levels - 1).long()

    q_np = quantized.cpu().numpy()
    symbols, counts = np.unique(q_np, return_counts=True)
    frequencies = {int(s): int(c) for s, c in zip(symbols, counts)}

    root = HuffmanCoder.build_tree(frequencies)
    code_table = HuffmanCoder.generate_code_table(root)

    probs = counts / numel
    entropy = -float(np.sum(probs * np.log2(probs + 1e-12)))

    # Vectorized bitstream packing using LUT mapping and numpy.packbits
    max_sym = int(symbols.max())
    lut = [code_table.get(i, "0") for i in range(max_sym + 1)]
    bit_chunks = [lut[v] for v in q_np]
    bitstream_str = "".join(bit_chunks)
    pad_len = (8 - len(bitstream_str) % 8) % 8
    padded = bitstream_str + "0" * pad_len

    # Fast bit packing using np.packbits
    bit_arr = np.frombuffer(padded.encode("ascii"), dtype=np.uint8) - ord("0")
    packed_np = np.packbits(bit_arr)
    byte_array = packed_np.tobytes()

    metadata = {
        "scale": scale,
        "min_val": min_val,
        "orig_shape": list(tensor.shape),
        "orig_dtype": str(tensor.dtype),
        "total_elements": numel,
        "entropy": round(entropy, 3),
        "pad_len": pad_len,
        "compressed_bytes": len(byte_array),
        "is_raw": False,
    }
    return byte_array, code_table, metadata

Vectorized encode input tensor into packed bitstream bytes and Huffman codebook.

def generate_code_table(root: HuffmanNode | None,
current_code: str = '',
code_table: dict[int, str] | None = None) ‑> dict[int, str]
Expand source code 
@staticmethod
def generate_code_table(
    root: HuffmanNode | None,
    current_code: str = "",
    code_table: dict[int, str] | None = None,
) -> dict[int, str]:
    """Traverse Huffman tree to generate prefix bitstring per symbol."""
    if code_table is None:
        code_table = {}
    if root is None:
        return code_table

    if root.symbol is not None:
        code_table[root.symbol] = current_code if current_code else "0"
        return code_table

    if root.left is not None:
        HuffmanCoder.generate_code_table(root.left, current_code + "0", code_table)
    if root.right is not None:
        HuffmanCoder.generate_code_table(root.right, current_code + "1", code_table)

    return code_table

Traverse Huffman tree to generate prefix bitstring per symbol.

Expand source code 
@COMPRESSOR_REGISTRY.register("huffman_coder")
class HuffmanCompressor(BaseCompressor):
    """
    HuffmanCompressor: Model-wide entropy compression profiler and simulator.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        model: nn.Module,
        quantize_bits: int = 8,
        max_error: float = 0.20,
        target_attention_only: bool = False,
        fallback_to_original: bool = True,
        cfg: Any | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(model, name="huffman", **kwargs)
        cfg = cfg or kwargs.get("comp_config")
        self.cfg = cfg

        self.quantize_bits = int(
            _cfg_get(
                cfg,
                "huffman_quantize_bits",
                _cfg_get(cfg, "quantize_bits", _cfg_get(cfg, "bits", quantize_bits)),
            )
        )
        self.max_error = float(
            _cfg_get(cfg, "huffman_max_error", _cfg_get(cfg, "max_error", max_error))
        )
        self.target_attention_only = bool(
            _cfg_get(
                cfg,
                "huffman_target_attention_only",
                _cfg_get(cfg, "target_attention_only", target_attention_only),
            )
        )
        self.fallback_to_original = bool(
            _cfg_get(
                cfg,
                "huffman_fallback_to_original",
                _cfg_get(cfg, "fallback_to_original", fallback_to_original),
            )
        )

        self.compressed_layers: list[str] = []
        self.fallback_layers: list[str] = []
        self.skipped_layers: list[str] = []
        self.layer_errors: dict[str, float] = {}

    def _is_target_module(self, name: str, module: nn.Module) -> bool:
        name_lower = name.lower()
        parts = name.split(".")
        if any(part in parts for part in ["lora_A", "lora_B", "base_layer"]):
            return False
        if any(
            skip in name_lower
            for skip in [
                "classifier",
                "score",
                "lm_head",
                "pooler",
                "embedding",
                "router",
                "gate",
            ]
        ):
            return False

        if self.target_attention_only:
            if not any(attn_kw in name_lower for attn_kw in _ATTN_NAMES):
                return False

        target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_linear = isinstance(target_mod, nn.Linear)
        is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
        if not (is_linear or is_conv1d):
            return False
        if not should_compress_linear(self.model, name):
            return False
        return True

    def compress(self) -> tuple[nn.Module, dict[str, Any]]:
        """Profile and apply Huffman entropy coding across all linear layers."""
        first_p = next(self.model.parameters(), None)
        _model_device = first_p.device if first_p is not None else torch.device("cpu")

        total_orig_bytes = 0
        total_comp_bytes = 0
        total_entropy = 0.0

        self.compressed_layers = []
        self.fallback_layers = []
        self.skipped_layers = []
        self.layer_errors = {}
        seen_weights: set[int] = set()

        # Freeze skipped non-target layers if target_attention_only is enabled
        if self.target_attention_only:
            for name, module in self.model.named_modules():
                if any(part in name.split(".") for part in ["lora_A", "lora_B", "base_layer"]):
                    continue
                target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
                cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
                is_linear = isinstance(target_mod, nn.Linear)
                is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
                if (is_linear or is_conv1d) and not self._is_target_module(name, module):
                    _freeze_base_only(module)
                    self.skipped_layers.append(name)

        for name, module in self.model.named_modules():
            if not self._is_target_module(name, module):
                continue

            target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
            if not hasattr(target_mod, "weight") or target_mod.weight is None:
                continue

            w_id = id(target_mod.weight)
            if w_id in seen_weights:
                self.skipped_layers.append(name)
                continue
            seen_weights.add(w_id)

            cls_name = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
            is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
            raw_w = target_mod.weight.data
            orig_shape = raw_w.shape
            orig_device = raw_w.device
            orig_dtype = raw_w.dtype

            # Byte accounting based on actual element_size()
            elem_bytes = raw_w.element_size() if hasattr(raw_w, "element_size") else 2
            layer_orig_bytes = raw_w.numel() * elem_bytes

            W_orig = raw_w.t().float() if is_conv1d else raw_w.float()
            norm_w = torch.norm(W_orig, p="fro").item()

            try:
                packed_b, code_map, meta = HuffmanCoder.encode(
                    W_orig, quantize_bits=self.quantize_bits
                )
                W_rec = HuffmanCoder.decode(packed_b, code_map, meta, device=orig_device).to(
                    dtype=torch.float32
                )

                if norm_w < 1e-12:
                    err = 0.0
                else:
                    err = float(torch.norm(W_orig - W_rec, p="fro").item() / norm_w)

                self.layer_errors[name] = err

                d_out, d_in = W_orig.shape
                fam = getattr(getattr(self.model, "config", None), "model_type", None)
                eff_max_err = max(self.max_error, adaptive_max_error(None, d_in, d_out, family=fam))

                if err > eff_max_err:
                    logger.warning(
                        "Huffman error %.4f on %s exceeded max_error %.4f (base max_err=%.4f)",
                        err,
                        name,
                        eff_max_err,
                        self.max_error,
                    )
                    if self.fallback_to_original:
                        self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                        _freeze_base_only(module)
                        total_orig_bytes += layer_orig_bytes
                        total_comp_bytes += layer_orig_bytes
                        continue

                # Apply reconstructed weight
                if is_conv1d:
                    res_w = W_rec.t().to(dtype=orig_dtype, device=orig_device)
                else:
                    res_w = W_rec.to(dtype=orig_dtype, device=orig_device)

                assert res_w.shape == orig_shape, (
                    f"Shape mismatch on {name}: {res_w.shape} vs {orig_shape}"
                )
                target_mod.weight.data.copy_(res_w)

                # Preserve LoRA adapter trainability
                for lora_attr in ("lora_A", "lora_B", "lora_a", "lora_b"):
                    if hasattr(module, lora_attr):
                        l_mod = getattr(module, lora_attr)
                        if (
                            isinstance(l_mod, nn.Module)
                            and hasattr(l_mod, "weight")
                            and l_mod.weight is not None
                        ):
                            l_mod.weight.requires_grad = True
                        elif isinstance(l_mod, nn.Parameter):
                            l_mod.requires_grad = True

                self.compressed_layers.append(name)
                total_orig_bytes += layer_orig_bytes
                total_comp_bytes += meta["compressed_bytes"]
                total_entropy += meta["entropy"]

            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("Huffman exception on %s: %s", name, e)
                self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                _freeze_base_only(module)
                total_orig_bytes += layer_orig_bytes
                total_comp_bytes += layer_orig_bytes

        # Ensure all parameters on target device
        self.model.to(device=_model_device)

        forward_check_success = None
        try:
            with torch.no_grad():
                dev = next(self.model.parameters(), torch.tensor(0.0)).device
                tokenizer = getattr(self.model, "tokenizer", None) or getattr(
                    getattr(self.model, "config", None), "tokenizer", None
                )
                if tokenizer is not None:
                    tok_out = tokenizer("Hello world", return_tensors="pt")
                    tok_out = {k: v.to(dev) for k, v in tok_out.items()}
                    self.model(**tok_out)
                    forward_check_success = True
                else:
                    # Look for token embeddings
                    has_embed = any(
                        any(kw in n.lower() for kw in ("embed_tokens", "wte", "word_embeddings"))
                        for n, _ in self.model.named_modules()
                    )
                    if has_embed:
                        dummy_ids = torch.randint(0, 100, (1, 8), device=dev)
                        dummy_mask = torch.ones((1, 8), device=dev)
                        try:
                            self.model(input_ids=dummy_ids, attention_mask=dummy_mask)
                        except TypeError:
                            self.model(dummy_ids)
                        forward_check_success = True
                    else:
                        first_linear = next(
                            (m for m in self.model.modules() if isinstance(m, nn.Linear)),
                            None,
                        )
                        if first_linear is not None:
                            in_dim = getattr(first_linear, "in_features", 8)
                            self.model(torch.randn(1, 8, in_dim, device=dev))
                            forward_check_success = True
                        else:
                            forward_check_success = None
        except Exception as fwd_exc:
            logger.debug("Huffman forward check failed: %s", fwd_exc)
            forward_check_success = None

        stats = self.get_compression_stats()
        avg_err = (
            (sum(self.layer_errors.values()) / max(1, len(self.layer_errors)))
            if self.layer_errors
            else 0.0
        )
        stats["quantize_bits"] = self.quantize_bits
        stats["avg_entropy_bits"] = round(total_entropy / max(len(self.compressed_layers), 1), 2)
        stats["total_compressed_bytes"] = total_comp_bytes
        stats["huffman_compression_ratio"] = round(total_orig_bytes / max(total_comp_bytes, 1), 2)
        stats["theoretical_compression_ratio"] = stats["huffman_compression_ratio"]
        stats["num_compressed"] = len(self.compressed_layers)
        stats["num_fallback"] = len(self.fallback_layers)
        stats["num_skipped"] = len(self.skipped_layers)
        stats["compressed_layers"] = self.compressed_layers
        stats["fallback_layers"] = self.fallback_layers
        stats["skipped_layers"] = self.skipped_layers
        stats["avg_reconstruction_error"] = avg_err
        stats["forward_check_success"] = forward_check_success
        logger.info(
            "Compressor huffman finished: %d compressed, %d fallback, avg_err=%.4f, max_err_threshold=%.4f",
            len(self.compressed_layers),
            len(self.fallback_layers),
            avg_err,
            self.max_error,
        )
        return self.model, stats

            total_comp_bytes += meta["compressed_bytes"]
            total_entropy += meta["entropy"]

            logger.debug("Huffman exception on %s: %s", name, e)

            if tokenizer is not None:
                tok_out = tokenizer("Hello world", return_tensors="pt")
                tok_out = {k: v.to(dev) for k, v in tok_out.items()}
                self.model(**tok_out)
                forward_check_success = True
            else:
                # Look for token embeddings
                has_embed = any(
                    any(kw in n.lower() for kw in ("embed_tokens", "wte", "word_embeddings"))
                    for n, _ in self.model.named_modules()
                )
                if has_embed:
                    dummy_ids = torch.randint(0, 100, (1, 8), device=dev)
                    dummy_mask = torch.ones((1, 8), device=dev)
                    try:
                        self.model(input_ids=dummy_ids, attention_mask=dummy_mask)
                    except TypeError:
                        self.model(dummy_ids)
                    forward_check_success = True
                else:
                    first_linear = next(
                        (m for m in self.model.modules() if isinstance(m, nn.Linear)),
                        None,
                    )
                    if first_linear is not None:
                        in_dim = getattr(first_linear, "in_features", 8)
                        self.model(torch.randn(1, 8, in_dim, device=dev))
                        forward_check_success = True
                    else:
                        forward_check_success = None
    except Exception as fwd_exc:
        logger.debug("Huffman forward check failed: %s", fwd_exc)
        forward_check_success = None

    stats = self.get_compression_stats()
    avg_err = (
        (sum(self.layer_errors.values()) / max(1, len(self.layer_errors)))
        if self.layer_errors
        else 0.0
    )
    stats["quantize_bits"] = self.quantize_bits
    stats["avg_entropy_bits"] = round(total_entropy / max(len(self.compressed_layers), 1), 2)
    stats["total_compressed_bytes"] = total_comp_bytes
    stats["huffman_compression_ratio"] = round(total_orig_bytes / max(total_comp_bytes, 1), 2)
    stats["theoretical_compression_ratio"] = stats["huffman_compression_ratio"]
    stats["num_compressed"] = len(self.compressed_layers)
    stats["num_fallback"] = len(self.fallback_layers)
    stats["num_skipped"] = len(self.skipped_layers)
    stats["compressed_layers"] = self.compressed_layers
    stats["fallback_layers"] = self.fallback_layers
    stats["skipped_layers"] = self.skipped_layers
    stats["avg_reconstruction_error"] = avg_err
    stats["forward_check_success"] = forward_check_success
    logger.info(
        "Compressor huffman finished: %d compressed, %d fallback, avg_err=%.4f, max_err_threshold=%.4f",
        len(self.compressed_layers),
        len(self.fallback_layers),
        avg_err,
        self.max_error,
    )
    return self.model, stats

Profile and apply Huffman entropy coding across all linear layers.

class HuffmanNode:
    """Node in the Huffman prefix tree."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        symbol: int | None = None,
        freq: int = 0,
        left: HuffmanNode | None = None,
        right: HuffmanNode | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.symbol = symbol
        self.freq = freq
        self.left = left
        self.right = right

    def __lt__(self, other: HuffmanNode) -> bool:
        return self.freq < other.freq

class KMeansCompressor(BaseCompressor):
    is_custom_attention: bool = False

    def __init__(
        self,
        model: nn.Module | None = None,
        n_clusters: int = 256,
        bits: int | None = None,
        max_error: float = 0.35,
        target_attention_only: bool = False,
        fallback_to_original: bool = True,
        lora_r: int = 0,
        lora_alpha: float = 1.0,
        skip_attention_qkv: bool = False,
        skip_should_compress_filter: bool = False,
        min_layer_dim: int = 8,
        kmeans_seed: int = 0xCAFE,
        cfg: Any | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(model if model is not None else nn.Identity(), name="kmeans", **kwargs)
        cfg = cfg or kwargs.get("comp_config")
        self.cfg = cfg

        parsed_bits = _cfg_get(cfg, "kmeans_bits", _cfg_get(cfg, "bits", bits))
        if parsed_bits is not None:
            self.n_clusters = 2 ** int(parsed_bits)
        else:
            self.n_clusters = int(
                _cfg_get(cfg, "kmeans_clusters", _cfg_get(cfg, "n_clusters", n_clusters))
            )
        self.bits = int(math.ceil(math.log2(max(self.n_clusters, 2))))

        self.max_error = float(
            _cfg_get(cfg, "kmeans_max_error", _cfg_get(cfg, "max_error", max_error))
        )
        self.target_attention_only = bool(
            _cfg_get(
                cfg,
                "kmeans_target_attention_only",
                _cfg_get(cfg, "target_attention_only", target_attention_only),
            )
        )
        self.fallback_to_original = bool(
            _cfg_get(
                cfg,
                "kmeans_fallback_to_original",
                _cfg_get(cfg, "fallback_to_original", fallback_to_original),
            )
        )
        self.lora_r = int(_cfg_get(cfg, "lora_r", _cfg_get(cfg, "lora_rank", lora_r)))
        self.lora_alpha = float(_cfg_get(cfg, "lora_alpha", lora_alpha))
        self.skip_attention_qkv = bool(_cfg_get(cfg, "skip_attention_qkv", skip_attention_qkv))
        self.skip_should_compress_filter = bool(
            _cfg_get(
                cfg,
                "skip_should_compress_filter",
                skip_should_compress_filter,
            )
        )
        self.min_layer_dim = max(1, int(_cfg_get(cfg, "min_layer_dim", min_layer_dim)))
        self.kmeans_seed = int(_cfg_get(cfg, "kmeans_seed", kmeans_seed))

        self.compressed_layers: list[str] = []
        self.fallback_layers: list[str] = []
        self.skipped_layers: list[str] = []
        self.layer_errors: dict[str, float] = {}
        self.layer_storage: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {}
        self.stats: dict[str, Any] = {}
        self._compressed_params: int = 0

    def _is_gqa_asymmetric(self, name: str, module: nn.Module) -> bool:
        if not self.skip_attention_qkv:
            return False
        cfg = getattr(self.model, "config", None)
        num_heads = getattr(cfg, "num_attention_heads", None)
        num_kv_heads = getattr(cfg, "num_key_value_heads", None)
        if num_heads is not None and num_kv_heads is not None and num_kv_heads < num_heads:
            if any(
                p in name.lower()
                for p in (
                    "q_proj",
                    "k_proj",
                    "v_proj",
                    "query",
                    "key",
                    "value",
                    "c_attn",
                )
            ):
                return True
        return False

    def _is_target_module(self, name: str, module: nn.Module, _debug: bool = False) -> bool:
        name_lower = name.lower()
        if any(part in name.split(".") for part in ("lora_A", "lora_B", "base_layer")):
            if _debug:
                logger.info(f"  [skip] {name}: lora/base_layer")
            return False
        if any(
            skip in name_lower
            for skip in (
                "classifier",
                "score",
                "lm_head",
                "pooler",
                "embedding",
                "router",
                "gate",
            )
        ):
            if _debug:
                logger.info(f"  [skip] {name}: skip keyword")
            return False

        if self.target_attention_only:
            if not any(k in name_lower for k in _ATTN_NAMES):
                if _debug:
                    logger.info(f"  [skip] {name}: not attention")
                return False

        if self._is_gqa_asymmetric(name, module):
            if _debug:
                logger.info(f"  [skip] {name}: GQA asymmetric")
            return False

        target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        cls_lower = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_linear = isinstance(target_mod, nn.Linear)
        is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_lower or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
        if not (is_linear or is_conv1d):
            if _debug:
                logger.info(f"  [skip] {name}: not linear-like")
            return False
        if isinstance(target_mod, KMeansLinear):
            if _debug:
                logger.info(f"  [skip] {name}: already KMeansLinear")
            return False

        w = getattr(target_mod, "weight", None)
        if w is None:
            if _debug:
                logger.info(f"  [skip] {name}: weight None")
            return False
        if min(w.shape[0], w.shape[1]) < self.min_layer_dim:
            if _debug:
                logger.info(f"  [skip] {name}: dim too small {tuple(w.shape)}")
            return False

        if not self.skip_should_compress_filter:
            try:
                if not should_compress_linear(self.model, name):
                    if _debug:
                        logger.info(f"  [skip] {name}: should_compress_linear=False")
                    return False
            except Exception as e:
                if _debug:
                    logger.info(f"  [skip] {name}: should_compress_linear raised {e}")
                return False

        if _debug:
            logger.info(f"  [TARGET] {name}: shape={tuple(w.shape)}")
        return True

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def compress(self, model: nn.Module | None = None) -> tuple[nn.Module, dict[str, Any]]:
        # Idempotence
        if getattr(self.model, "_kmeans_compressed", False):
            logger.warning("KMeansCompressor: model already compressed; skipping.")
            try:
                return self.model, self.get_compression_stats()
            except Exception:
                return self.model, {}

        if model is not None:
            self.model = model
            try:
                self._original_params = self._count_params(self.model)
            except Exception:
                self._original_params = sum(p.numel() for p in self.model.parameters())

        self.compressed_layers = []
        self.fallback_layers = []
        self.skipped_layers = []
        self.layer_errors = {}
        self.layer_storage = {}
        seen_weights: set[int] = set()

        total_orig_bytes = 0
        total_comp_bytes = 0

        target_list = [
            (name, module)
            for name, module in self.model.named_modules()
            if self._is_target_module(name, module)
        ]

        if not target_list:
            logger.warning("KMeansCompressor: no compressible layers found.")
            n = 0
            for name, module in self.model.named_modules():
                n += 1
                self._is_target_module(name, module, _debug=True)
            logger.warning("KMeansCompressor diagnostic: %d modules scanned, 0 targets.", n)
            try:
                return self.model, self.get_compression_stats()
            except Exception:
                return self.model, {}

        logger.info("KMeansCompressor: %d target layers identified.", len(target_list))

        for name, module in target_list:
            target_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
            if not hasattr(target_mod, "weight") or target_mod.weight is None:
                continue

            w_id = id(target_mod.weight)
            if w_id in seen_weights:
                self.skipped_layers.append(name)
                continue
            seen_weights.add(w_id)

            cls_lower = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
            is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_lower or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
            orig_w = target_mod.weight.data
            W_target = orig_w.t().contiguous().float() if is_conv1d else orig_w.float()
            norm_orig = torch.norm(W_target, p="fro").clamp_min(1e-8)
            _out_f, _in_f = W_target.shape[0], W_target.shape[1]

            # Original bytes
            orig_bytes = orig_w.numel() * orig_w.element_size()
            bias_holder = getattr(target_mod, "bias", None)
            if bias_holder is not None:
                orig_bytes += bias_holder.numel() * bias_holder.element_size()
            total_orig_bytes += orig_bytes

            new_layer: KMeansLinear | None = None
            err = 1.0
            try:
                new_layer = KMeansLinear(
                    module,
                    n_clusters=self.n_clusters,
                    lora_r=self.lora_r,
                    lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
                    kmeans_seed=self.kmeans_seed,
                )
                recon_w = new_layer.get_weight().float()
                err = float(torch.norm(W_target - recon_w, p="fro").item() / norm_orig.item())
            except Exception as ex:
                logger.warning("KMeansCompressor: failed on %s: %s", name, ex)
                new_layer = None
                err = 1.0

            self.layer_errors[name] = float(err)

            if new_layer is None or err > self.max_error:
                logger.warning(
                    "KMeansCompressor: error %.4f on %s > %.4f. %s",
                    err,
                    name,
                    self.max_error,
                    ("Falling back." if self.fallback_to_original else "Using compressed."),
                )
                self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                for p in module.parameters():
                    p.requires_grad = False
                total_comp_bytes += orig_bytes
                continue

            # KM-FIX-18: preserve LoRA wrapper
            try:
                if hasattr(module, "base_layer"):
                    module.base_layer = new_layer
                    # Keep LoRA param trainable
                    for pn, p in module.named_parameters():
                        if "lora_" in pn.lower() and not pn.startswith("base_layer"):
                            p.requires_grad = True
                else:
                    replace_module(self.model, name, new_layer)
                self.compressed_layers.append(name)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning("KMeansCompressor: replacement failed on %s: %s", name, e)
                self.fallback_layers.append(name)
                total_comp_bytes += orig_bytes
                continue

            # Honest byte accounting
            st = new_layer.storage_bytes()
            self.layer_storage[name] = st
            total_comp_bytes += sum(st.values())

        # Forward sanity check
        forward_check_success = True
        try:
            with torch.no_grad():
                dev = _get_device(self.model)
                dummy = torch.randint(0, 100, (1, 8), device=dev, dtype=torch.long)
                try:
                    self.model(input_ids=dummy, attention_mask=torch.ones_like(dummy))
                except Exception:
                    try:
                        self.model(dummy)
                    except Exception as e2:
                        logger.warning("KMeans forward check failed: %s", e2)
                        forward_check_success = False
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("KMeans forward check raised: %s", e)
            forward_check_success = False

        try:
            self._compressed_params = self._count_params(self.model)
        except Exception:
            self._compressed_params = sum(p.numel() for p in self.model.parameters())

        storage_ratio = total_orig_bytes / total_comp_bytes if total_comp_bytes > 0 else 1.0
        effective_ratio = total_comp_bytes / total_orig_bytes if total_orig_bytes > 0 else 1.0
        avg_err = (
            sum(self.layer_errors.values()) / max(1, len(self.layer_errors))
            if self.layer_errors
            else 0.0
        )

        try:
            stats = self.get_compression_stats()
        except Exception:
            stats = {}

        stats.update(
            {
                "compressed_layers": self.compressed_layers,
                "compressed_layer_count": len(self.compressed_layers),
                "fallback_layers": self.fallback_layers,
                "fallback_layer_count": len(self.fallback_layers),
                "skipped_layers": self.skipped_layers,
                "skipped_layer_count": len(self.skipped_layers),
                "num_compressed": len(self.compressed_layers),
                "num_fallback": len(self.fallback_layers),
                "num_skipped": len(self.skipped_layers),
                "layer_errors": self.layer_errors,
                "layer_storage_bytes": self.layer_storage,
                "avg_reconstruction_error": avg_err,
                "max_error": self.max_error,
                "target_attention_only": self.target_attention_only,
                "fallback_to_original": self.fallback_to_original,
                "n_clusters": self.n_clusters,
                "effective_bits_per_weight": self.bits,
                "lora_r": self.lora_r,
                "lora_alpha": self.lora_alpha,
                # Honest accounting
                "orig_storage_bytes": total_orig_bytes,
                "comp_storage_bytes": total_comp_bytes,
                "storage_compression_ratio": round(storage_ratio, 3),
                "storage_reduction_pct": round(100.0 * (1.0 - effective_ratio), 2),
                "effective_parameter_ratio": round(effective_ratio, 4),
                "forward_check_success": forward_check_success,
                "method": "kmeans",
                "note": (
                    "Storage accounting is honest (bytes). indices are stored as "
                    f"{_dtype_for_clusters(self.n_clusters)} to minimize overhead. "
                    "The framework's parameter counter uses model.parameters(), "
                    "which does NOT include the `indices` buffer."
                ),
            }
        )
        self.stats = stats

        try:
            if not hasattr(self.model, "compression_stats") or self.model.compression_stats is None:
                self.model.compression_stats = {}
            self.model.compression_stats.update(
                {
                    "num_compressed": len(self.compressed_layers),
                    "num_fallback": len(self.fallback_layers),
                    "compression_method": "kmeans",
                    "effective_parameter_ratio": effective_ratio,
                    "storage_compression_ratio": storage_ratio,
                }
            )
        except Exception:
            pass

        self.model._kmeans_compressed = True
        logger.info(
            "KMeans: %d compressed, %d fallback, avg_err=%.4f, storage=%.2fx.",
            len(self.compressed_layers),
            len(self.fallback_layers),
            avg_err,
            storage_ratio,
        )
        return self.model, stats

Base class for all compressors. Not a nn.Module to avoid parameter storage.

class KMeansLinear(nn.Module):
    """
    Linear layer with weight matrix quantized via 1D k-means codebook.

    Storage:
      - centroids:  (C,)         fp16/fp32
      - indices:    (out, in)    int8/int16/int32  (smallest that fits C)
      - bias:       (out,)       fp16/fp32
      - lora_A/B:   (r, in) / (out, r)   optional

    Forward:
      W_hat = centroids[indices]
      y = F.linear(x, W_hat) + bias + scaling · (x @ A^T) @ B^T
    """

    is_custom_attention: bool = False
    is_custom_linear: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        linear: nn.Module,
        n_clusters: int = 256,
        lora_r: int = 0,
        lora_alpha: float = 1.0,
        kmeans_seed: int = 0xCAFE,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        target_mod = getattr(linear, "base_layer", linear)
        cls_lower = target_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_lower or hasattr(target_mod, "nf")
        raw_w = target_mod.weight.data

        if is_conv1d:
            W = raw_w.t().contiguous().float()
            self.in_features = raw_w.shape[0]
            self.out_features = raw_w.shape[1]
        else:
            W = raw_w.float()
            self.in_features = int(getattr(target_mod, "in_features", W.shape[1]))
            self.out_features = int(getattr(target_mod, "out_features", W.shape[0]))

        self.n_clusters = int(min(n_clusters, W.numel()))
        self._index_dtype = _dtype_for_clusters(self.n_clusters)

        # Detect existing LoRA
        detected_r, detected_alpha, src_A, src_B = _detect_lora(linear)
        self.lora_r = int(detected_r) if detected_r > 0 else int(lora_r)
        self.lora_alpha = float(detected_alpha) if detected_r > 0 else float(lora_alpha)
        self.scaling = (self.lora_alpha / self.lora_r) if self.lora_r > 0 else 1.0

        # Fit centroids and indices
        centroids, indices = fast_kmeans_1d(
            W,
            n_clusters=self.n_clusters,
            seed=kmeans_seed,
        )
        # KM-FIX-23: centroids frozen if LoRA exists (pure quantizer mode).
        centroids_trainable = self.lora_r == 0
        self.centroids = nn.Parameter(
            centroids.to(dtype=raw_w.dtype),
            requires_grad=centroids_trainable,
        )
        # Store indices as int8/int16/int32
        self.register_buffer("indices", indices.to(self._index_dtype))

        # Bias
        bias_holder = getattr(target_mod, "bias", None)
        if bias_holder is not None:
            self.bias = nn.Parameter(bias_holder.data.clone(), requires_grad=False)
        else:
            self.register_parameter("bias", None)

        # LoRA branch
        if self.lora_r > 0:
            dev = raw_w.device
            dt = raw_w.dtype
            self.lora_A = nn.Parameter(
                torch.empty((self.lora_r, self.in_features), dtype=dt, device=dev)
            )
            self.lora_B = nn.Parameter(
                torch.zeros((self.out_features, self.lora_r), dtype=dt, device=dev)
            )
            nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))

            if src_A is not None and src_B is not None:
                if src_A.shape == self.lora_A.shape:
                    self.lora_A.data.copy_(src_A.to(self.lora_A.device, dtype=self.lora_A.dtype))
                if src_B.shape == self.lora_B.shape:
                    self.lora_B.data.copy_(src_B.to(self.lora_B.device, dtype=self.lora_B.dtype))
        else:
            self.register_parameter("lora_A", None)
            self.register_parameter("lora_B", None)

        # KM-FIX-20: cache in eval
        self._cached_weight: torch.Tensor | None = None

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def get_weight(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        # KM-FIX-20: cache in eval mode
        if not self.training and self._cached_weight is not None:
            return self._cached_weight
        idx_long = self.indices.long().reshape(-1)
        W = self.centroids.index_select(0, idx_long).reshape(self.out_features, self.in_features)
        if not self.training:
            self._cached_weight = W
        return W

    @property
    def weight(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        return self.get_weight()

    def train(self, mode: bool = True) -> KMeansLinear:
        super().train(mode)
        if mode:
            self._cached_weight = None
        return self

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def storage_bytes(self) -> dict[str, int]:
        """KM-FIX-07/22: honest storage accounting."""

        def _sz(t):
            if t is None:
                return 0
            return t.numel() * t.element_size()

        return {
            "centroids": _sz(self.centroids.data),
            "indices": _sz(self.indices),
            "bias": _sz(self.bias.data) if self.bias is not None else 0,
            "lora_A": _sz(self.lora_A.data) if self.lora_A is not None else 0,
            "lora_B": _sz(self.lora_B.data) if self.lora_B is not None else 0,
        }

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        # Bias cast only when dtype/device mismatch
        if self.bias is not None:
            b = self.bias
            if b.dtype != x.dtype or b.device != x.device:
                b = b.to(dtype=x.dtype, device=x.device)
        else:
            b = None

        if self.training:
            out = KMeansSTEFunction.apply(self.centroids, self.indices, x, b)
        else:
            W = self.get_weight().to(dtype=x.dtype, device=x.device)
            out = F.linear(x, W, b)

        if self.lora_r > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
            lora_out = F.linear(F.linear(x, self.lora_A), self.lora_B)
            out = out + self.scaling * lora_out.to(out.dtype)

        return out

Linear layer with weight matrix quantized via 1D k-means codebook.

Storage -----= - centroids: (C,) fp16/fp32 - indices: (out, in) int8/int16/int32 (smallest that fits C) - bias: (out,) fp16/fp32 - lora_A/B: (r, in) / (out, r) optional

Forward -----= W_hat = centroids[indices] y = F.linear(x, W_hat) + bias + scaling · (x @ A^T) @ B^T

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

Ancestors

  • torch.nn.modules.module.Module

def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) ‑> torch.Tensor
Expand source code 
def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    # Bias cast only when dtype/device mismatch
    if self.bias is not None:
        b = self.bias
        if b.dtype != x.dtype or b.device != x.device:
            b = b.to(dtype=x.dtype, device=x.device)
    else:
        b = None

    if self.training:
        out = KMeansSTEFunction.apply(self.centroids, self.indices, x, b)
    else:
        W = self.get_weight().to(dtype=x.dtype, device=x.device)
        out = F.linear(x, W, b)

    if self.lora_r > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
        lora_out = F.linear(F.linear(x, self.lora_A), self.lora_B)
        out = out + self.scaling * lora_out.to(out.dtype)

    return out

Define the computation performed at every call.

Should be overridden by all subclasses.

Although the recipe for forward pass needs to be defined within this function, one should call the :class:Module instance afterwards instead of this since the former takes care of running the registered hooks while the latter silently ignores them.

def get_weight(self) ‑> torch.Tensor
Expand source code 
def get_weight(self) -> torch.Tensor:
    # KM-FIX-20: cache in eval mode
    if not self.training and self._cached_weight is not None:
        return self._cached_weight
    idx_long = self.indices.long().reshape(-1)
    W = self.centroids.index_select(0, idx_long).reshape(self.out_features, self.in_features)
    if not self.training:
        self._cached_weight = W
    return W
def storage_bytes(self) ‑> dict[str, int]

def storage_bytes(self) -> dict[str, int]:
    """KM-FIX-07/22: honest storage accounting."""

    def _sz(t):
        if t is None:
            return 0
        return t.numel() * t.element_size()

    return {
        "centroids": _sz(self.centroids.data),
        "indices": _sz(self.indices),
        "bias": _sz(self.bias.data) if self.bias is not None else 0,
        "lora_A": _sz(self.lora_A.data) if self.lora_A is not None else 0,
        "lora_B": _sz(self.lora_B.data) if self.lora_B is not None else 0,
    }

class KMeansSTEFunction (*args, **kwargs)
Expand source code 
class KMeansSTEFunction(torch.autograd.Function):
    """
    STE for codebook lookup.

    Forward:  out = F.linear(x, centroids[indices], bias)
    Backward: gradient to centroids via scatter-add; gradient to x flows
              through the same effective W.
    """

    @staticmethod
    def forward(ctx, centroids, indices, x, bias):
        # KM-FIX-17: safe indexing
        idx_long = indices.long()
        W = centroids.index_select(0, idx_long.reshape(-1)).reshape(
            indices.shape[0], indices.shape[1]
        )
        W = W.to(dtype=x.dtype)
        ctx.save_for_backward(centroids, idx_long, x, bias)
        return F.linear(x, W, bias)

    @staticmethod
    def backward(ctx, grad_output):
        centroids, idx_long, x, bias = ctx.saved_tensors
        dtype = grad_output.dtype
        device = grad_output.device

        # Reconstruct W
        W = (
            centroids.index_select(0, idx_long.reshape(-1))
            .reshape(idx_long.shape[0], idx_long.shape[1])
            .to(dtype=dtype, device=device)
        )

        x_cast = x.to(dtype=dtype, device=device)

        grad_x = grad_output.matmul(W) if ctx.needs_input_grad[2] else None

        grad_bias = None
        if bias is not None and ctx.needs_input_grad[3]:
            grad_bias = grad_output.sum(dim=list(range(grad_output.ndim - 1)))

        grad_centroids = None
        if ctx.needs_input_grad[0]:
            if x_cast.ndim == 2:
                grad_W = grad_output.t().contiguous().matmul(x_cast)
            else:
                go = grad_output.reshape(-1, grad_output.shape[-1])
                xf = x_cast.reshape(-1, x_cast.shape[-1])
                grad_W = go.t().contiguous().matmul(xf)

            grad_centroids = torch.zeros_like(centroids)
            flat_idx = idx_long.reshape(-1)
            flat_grad = grad_W.reshape(-1).to(dtype=centroids.dtype, device=centroids.device)
            grad_centroids.scatter_add_(0, flat_idx, flat_grad)

        return grad_centroids, None, grad_x, grad_bias

STE for codebook lookup.

Define a formula for differentiating the operation with backward mode automatic differentiation.

This function is to be overridden by all subclasses. (Defining this function is equivalent to defining the vjp function.)

It must accept a context :attr:ctx as the first argument, followed by as many outputs as the :func:forward returned (None will be passed in for non tensor outputs of the forward function), and it should return as many tensors, as there were inputs to :func:forward. Each argument is the gradient w.r.t the given output, and each returned value should be the gradient w.r.t. the corresponding input. If an input is not a Tensor or is a Tensor not requiring grads, you can just pass None as a gradient for that input.

The context can be used to retrieve tensors saved during the forward pass. It also has an attribute :attr:ctx.needs_input_grad as a tuple of booleans representing whether each input needs gradient. E.g., :func:backward will have ctx.needs_input_grad[0] = True if the first input to :func:forward needs gradient computed w.r.t. the output.

def forward(ctx, centroids, indices, x, bias)
Expand source code 
@staticmethod
def forward(ctx, centroids, indices, x, bias):
    # KM-FIX-17: safe indexing
    idx_long = indices.long()
    W = centroids.index_select(0, idx_long.reshape(-1)).reshape(
        indices.shape[0], indices.shape[1]
    )
    W = W.to(dtype=x.dtype)
    ctx.save_for_backward(centroids, idx_long, x, bias)
    return F.linear(x, W, bias)

Define the forward of the custom autograd Function.

This function is to be overridden by all subclasses. There are two ways to define forward:

Usage 1 (Combined forward and ctx)::

@staticmethod
def forward(ctx: Any, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
    pass
  • It must accept a context ctx as the first argument, followed by any number of arguments (tensors or other types).
  • See :ref:combining-forward-context for more details

Usage 2 (Separate forward and ctx)::

@staticmethod
def forward(*args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
    pass


@staticmethod
def setup_context(ctx: Any, inputs: Tuple[Any, ...], output: Any) -> None:
    pass
  • The forward no longer accepts a ctx argument.
  • Instead, you must also override the :meth:torch.autograd.Function.setup_context staticmethod to handle setting up the ctx object. output is the output of the forward, inputs are a Tuple of inputs to the forward.
  • See :ref:extending-autograd for more details

The context can be used to store arbitrary data that can be then retrieved during the backward pass. Tensors should not be stored directly on ctx (though this is not currently enforced for backward compatibility). Instead, tensors should be saved either with :func:ctx.save_for_backward if they are intended to be used in backward (equivalently, vjp) or :func:ctx.save_for_forward if they are intended to be used for in jvp.
