Expand source code 
@COMPRESSOR_REGISTRY.register("slicegpt")
class PCAActivationAwareSVDCompressor(BaseCompressor):
    """
    Per-Layer Activation-Aware Low-Rank Compression (PCA-ASVD).

    For each target linear layer:
      1. Collect input activation covariance C = E[X^T X] online (no RAM bomb).
      2. Compute orthogonal rotation Q via eigendecomposition of C.
      3. Rotate weight: W_pca = W @ Q.
      4. Truncate to top-`keep` principal coordinates.
      5. Store as rank-`keep` factorization: U = W_pca[:, :keep], V = Q[:, :keep]^T.

    Parameter reduction: (d_out + d_in) × keep instead of d_out × d_in.

    Memory: O(d_in²) per layer for covariance (not O(N × seq_len × d_in)).
    Stability: .detach() prevents autograd graph retention.
    Safety: try/finally guarantees hook cleanup on failure.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        model: nn.Module,
        tokenizer: Any | None = None,
        sparsity: float = 0.6,
        non_uniform: bool = False,
        calibration_loader: Any | None = None,
        calibration_texts: list[str] | None = None,
        target_attention_only: bool = False,
        device: str | torch.device | None = None,
        max_error: float = 0.5,
        fallback_to_original: bool = True,
        target_modules: list[str] | None = None,
        use_lora: bool = False,
        lora_r: int = 0,
        lora_alpha: float = 1.0,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(model, name="pca_asvd", **kwargs)
        cfg = kwargs.get("comp_config", kwargs.get("cfg"))
        if cfg is not None:
            sparsity = _cfg_get(cfg, "slicegpt_sparsity", _cfg_get(cfg, "sparsity", sparsity))
            non_uniform = _cfg_get(cfg, "non_uniform", non_uniform)
            max_error = _cfg_get(cfg, "max_error", max_error)
            fallback_to_original = _cfg_get(cfg, "fallback_to_original", fallback_to_original)
            target_attention_only = _cfg_get(cfg, "target_attention_only", target_attention_only)
            use_lora = _cfg_get(cfg, "use_lora", use_lora)
            lora_r = _cfg_get(cfg, "lora_r", lora_r)
            lora_alpha = _cfg_get(cfg, "lora_alpha", lora_alpha)

        if "fallback_to_original" in kwargs:
            fallback_to_original = bool(kwargs["fallback_to_original"])
        if "use_lora" in kwargs:
            use_lora = bool(kwargs["use_lora"])
        if "lora_r" in kwargs:
            lora_r = int(kwargs["lora_r"])

        self.tokenizer = tokenizer or getattr(model, "tokenizer", None)
        self.sparsity = max(0.0, min(float(sparsity), 0.95))
        self.non_uniform = non_uniform
        self.target_attention_only = bool(target_attention_only)
        self.calibration_loader = calibration_loader
        self.calibration_texts = calibration_texts
        self.target_device = (
            torch.device(device)
            if device is not None
            else next(model.parameters(), torch.tensor(0.0)).device
        )
        self.max_error = float(max_error)
        self.fallback_to_original = bool(fallback_to_original)
        self.target_modules = target_modules
        self.use_lora = bool(use_lora) or (lora_r > 0)
        self.lora_r = int(lora_r)
        self.lora_alpha = float(lora_alpha)
        self.layer_errors: dict[str, float] = {}
        self.layer_ranks: dict[str, int] = {}
        self.stats: dict[str, Any] = {}

    def _is_target_module(self, name: str, module: nn.Module) -> bool:
        """
        Unified critical-layer protection delegated to should_compress_linear.
        """
        name_l = name.lower()
        if self.target_attention_only:
            attn_keywords = [
                "attn", "attention",
                "q_proj", "k_proj", "v_proj", "o_proj",
                "c_attn", "c_proj",
                "query", "key", "value",
            ]
            if not any(k in name_l for k in attn_keywords):
                return False

        base_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        cls_name = base_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_linear_like = (
            isinstance(base_mod, nn.Linear)
            or "conv1d" in cls_name
            or hasattr(base_mod, "nf")
        )
        if not is_linear_like:
            return False
        # Skip layers that are already factorized
        if SVDLinear is not None and isinstance(base_mod, SVDLinear):
            return False
        if not should_compress_linear(self.model, name):
            return False
        if self.target_modules is None:
            return True
        return any(t in name for t in self.target_modules)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # FIX 1: Online covariance accumulation (no RAM bomb)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _collect_layer_covariances(self) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
        """
        Collect input activation covariances online, avoiding catastrophic RAM usage.

        Instead of storing all activations (which requires O(N × seq_len × d_in) RAM),
        we accumulate C = X^T X directly inside each hook. Memory is bounded by
        O(d_in²) per layer.

        FIX 2: hooks are removed in a `finally` block to prevent leaks on crash.
        """
        self.model.eval()
        covariances: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
        counts: dict[str, int] = {}
        hooks = []

        def make_hook(name: str):
            def hook(module: nn.Module, inputs: tuple[torch.Tensor, ...], output: Any):
                if inputs and isinstance(inputs[0], torch.Tensor):
                    inp = inputs[0]
                    if inp.numel() == 0:
                        return
                    # FIX 3: .detach() prevents autograd graph retention
                    inp = inp.detach().float()
                    # Flatten leading dims: (B, seq, d) -> (B*seq, d)
                    d_in = inp.shape[-1]
                    inp_flat = inp.reshape(-1, d_in)

                    # Online covariance: C += X^T X
                    batch_cov = torch.matmul(inp_flat.T, inp_flat).cpu()
                    batch_count = inp_flat.shape[0]

                    if name not in covariances:
                        covariances[name] = batch_cov
                        counts[name] = batch_count
                    else:
                        covariances[name] += batch_cov
                        counts[name] += batch_count
            return hook

        for name, module in self.model.named_modules():
            if self._is_target_module(name, module):
                hooks.append(module.register_forward_hook(make_hook(name)))

        calib_inputs = get_calibration_tensors(
            self.model,
            tokenizer=self.tokenizer,
            calibration_loader=self.calibration_loader,
            calibration_texts=self.calibration_texts,
            device=self.target_device,
        )

        # FIX 2: try/finally guarantees hook cleanup even on crash
        try:
            with torch.no_grad():
                if isinstance(calib_inputs, dict):
                    self.model(**calib_inputs)
                elif isinstance(calib_inputs, torch.Tensor):
                    self.model(calib_inputs)
                elif isinstance(calib_inputs, (list, tuple)):
                    self.model(*calib_inputs)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning(
                "Forward pass during calibration raised %s. "
                "Covariances may be incomplete.",
                e,
            )
        finally:
            for h in hooks:
                try:
                    h.remove()
                except Exception:
                    pass

        # Normalize covariances by sample count
        for name in list(covariances.keys()):
            covariances[name] /= max(1, counts[name])

        return covariances

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # FIX: _compute_orthogonal_rotation takes covariance directly
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _compute_orthogonal_rotation(
        self,
        cov: torch.Tensor | None,
        d_in: int,
        device: torch.device | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
        """
        Compute orthogonal rotation Q via eigen-decomposition of pre-computed covariance.

        Args:
            cov: Pre-computed covariance matrix C = E[X^T X], shape (d_in, d_in).
                 If None or invalid, falls back to random orthogonal basis.
            d_in: Expected input dimension.
            device: Target device for output tensors.

        Returns:
            (Q, eigenvalues) where Q is orthogonal (d_in, d_in) and eigenvalues
            are sorted in descending order.
        """
        if device is None:
            device = cov.device if cov is not None else torch.device("cpu")

        if cov is not None and cov.numel() > 0:
            cov = cov.to(device)
            if cov.shape == (d_in, d_in):
                # Add small damping for numerical stability
                cov = cov + 1e-4 * torch.eye(d_in, device=device)
                try:
                    eigvals, Q = torch.linalg.eigh(cov)
                    # eigh returns ascending order; we want descending
                    idx = torch.argsort(eigvals, descending=True)
                    return Q[:, idx], eigvals[idx]
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug(
                        "eigh covariance failed (%s). Falling back to QR random basis.",
                        e,
                    )

        # Fallback: random orthogonal basis
        rand_mat = torch.randn(d_in, d_in, device=device)
        Q, _ = torch.linalg.qr(rand_mat)
        return Q, torch.ones(d_in, device=device)

    @staticmethod
    def _detect_lora(module: nn.Module) -> tuple[int, float]:
        """Detect existing LoRA rank and alpha from a wrapped module, if any."""
        if not hasattr(module, "lora_A") or module.lora_A is None:
            return 0, 1.0
        rank = 0
        la = getattr(module, "lora_A", None)
        if la is not None:
            if hasattr(la, "weight") and la.weight is not None and la.weight.dim() >= 1:
                rank = int(la.weight.shape[0])
            elif isinstance(la, nn.Parameter) and la.dim() >= 1:
                rank = int(la.shape[0])
        alpha = 1.0
        for attr in ("lora_alpha", "alpha"):
            a = getattr(module, attr, None)
            if a is not None:
                try:
                    alpha = float(a)
                except (TypeError, ValueError):
                    pass
                break
        return rank, alpha

    def compress(self, **kwargs: Any) -> tuple[nn.Module, dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Applies PCA rotation + rank-r truncation and replaces each weight
        with a real SVDLinear low-rank factorization.
        """
        if SVDLinear is None:
            logger.error(
                "PCA-ASVD: SVDLinear could not be imported (%s). Cannot perform "
                "real compression; returning model unchanged.",
                _SVD_IMPORT_ERROR,
            )
            stats = self.get_compression_stats()
            stats.update(
                {
                    "is_noop": True,
                    "noop_reason": "svd_linear_import_failed",
                    "method": "pca_asvd_unavailable",
                }
            )
            return self.model, stats

        self.layer_errors = {}
        self.layer_ranks = {}

        # FIX: Collect covariances, not activations
        covariances_map = self._collect_layer_covariances()

        sliced_layers_count = 0
        total_params_before = 0
        total_params_after = 0

        for name, module in list(self.model.named_modules()):
            if not self._is_target_module(name, module):
                continue

            base_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
            raw_w = base_mod.weight.data
            cls_name = base_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
            is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(base_mod, "nf")
            device = raw_w.device
            dtype = raw_w.dtype

            # FIX 3: .detach() prevents autograd graph retention
            if is_conv1d:
                W = raw_w.detach().t().float()
                d_out, d_in = W.shape
            else:
                W = raw_w.detach().float()
                d_out, d_in = W.shape

            total_params_before += d_out * d_in

            # FIX: pass covariance (not activations)
            cov = covariances_map.get(name, None)
            Q, _ = self._compute_orthogonal_rotation(cov, d_in, device=device)
            Q = Q.to(device=device, dtype=torch.float32)

            # Rotate weight into PCA basis
            W_pca = torch.matmul(W, Q)

            # Determine number of principal coordinates to retain
            if self.non_uniform and any(k in name for k in ("query", "key", "q_lin", "k_lin")):
                head_sparsity = max(0.0, self.sparsity * 0.75)
                keep = max(1, int(d_in * (1.0 - head_sparsity)))
            elif self.non_uniform and any(k in name for k in ("mlp", "ffn", "dense")):
                mlp_sparsity = min(0.9, self.sparsity * 1.15)
                keep = max(1, int(d_in * (1.0 - mlp_sparsity)))
            else:
                keep = max(1, int(d_in * (1.0 - self.sparsity)))

            # Rank r cannot exceed min(d_out, d_in)
            effective_keep = min(keep, d_out, d_in)

            # Reconstruct truncated dense weight for error measurement only
            W_trunc = W_pca.clone()
            if effective_keep < d_in:
                W_trunc[:, effective_keep:] = 0.0
            W_reconstructed = torch.matmul(W_trunc, Q.T)

            norm_orig = torch.norm(W, p="fro").clamp_min(1e-8)
            rel_err = (torch.norm(W - W_reconstructed, p="fro") / norm_orig).item()
            self.layer_errors[name] = float(rel_err)

            # Fallback on high reconstruction error
            if rel_err > self.max_error:
                logger.warning(
                    "PCA-ASVD reconstruction error %.4f on %s exceeded threshold %.4f%s",
                    rel_err, name, self.max_error,
                    ". Skipping (fallback to original)."
                    if self.fallback_to_original
                    else ". Proceeding with sliced weight.",
                )
                if self.fallback_to_original:
                    for p in module.parameters():
                        p.requires_grad = False
                    total_params_after += d_out * d_in
                    continue

            # === REAL LOW-RANK FACTORIZATION ===
            # W_reconstructed = W_pca[:, :keep] @ Q[:, :keep]^T
            U_factor = W_pca[:, :effective_keep].contiguous().to(dtype=dtype, device=device)
            Vt_factor = Q[:, :effective_keep].T.contiguous().to(dtype=dtype, device=device)

            # Parameter count of factorization
            factorized_params = effective_keep * (d_out + d_in)

            # Only apply if it actually reduces parameter count
            if factorized_params >= d_out * d_in:
                logger.info(
                    "PCA-ASVD: keep=%d on %s would not reduce params (%d >= %d). Keeping dense.",
                    effective_keep, name, factorized_params, d_out * d_in,
                )
                total_params_after += d_out * d_in
                continue

            # Detect existing LoRA adapters
            existing_lora_r, existing_lora_alpha = self._detect_lora(module)
            if self.use_lora and self.lora_r > 0 and existing_lora_r == 0:
                used_lora_r = self.lora_r
                used_lora_alpha = self.lora_alpha
            else:
                used_lora_r = existing_lora_r
                used_lora_alpha = existing_lora_alpha

            # Build SVDLinear factorization
            svd_layer = SVDLinear(
                in_features=d_in,
                out_features=d_out,
                rank=effective_keep,
                bias=(getattr(base_mod, "bias", None) is not None and base_mod.bias is not None),
                dtype=dtype,
                device=device,
                lora_r=used_lora_r,
                lora_alpha=used_lora_alpha,
            )
            svd_layer.set_low_rank_factors(U_factor, Vt_factor)

            # Copy bias
            if (
                getattr(base_mod, "bias", None) is not None
                and base_mod.bias is not None
                and getattr(svd_layer, "bias", None) is not None
            ):
                svd_layer.bias.data.copy_(base_mod.bias.data)

            # Copy existing LoRA adapters
            if existing_lora_r > 0 and getattr(svd_layer, "lora_A", None) is not None:
                try:
                    src_la = getattr(module.lora_A, "weight", module.lora_A)
                    src_lb = getattr(module.lora_B, "weight", module.lora_B)
                    la_target = svd_layer.lora_A
                    lb_target = svd_layer.lora_B
                    if (
                        hasattr(src_la, "data")
                        and la_target is not None
                        and src_la.shape == la_target.shape
                    ):
                        la_target.data.copy_(src_la.data)
                    if (
                        hasattr(src_lb, "data")
                        and lb_target is not None
                        and src_lb.shape == lb_target.shape
                    ):
                        lb_target.data.copy_(src_lb.data)
                except Exception as e_lora:
                    logger.debug("PCA-ASVD LoRA adapter copy skipped on %s: %s", name, e_lora)

            # Replace the module
            is_wrapped = hasattr(module, "base_layer")
            if is_wrapped:
                module.base_layer = svd_layer
            else:
                replace_module(self.model, name, svd_layer)

            self.layer_ranks[name] = effective_keep
            sliced_layers_count += 1
            total_params_after += factorized_params

        # Post-compression forward sanity check
        forward_check_success = True
        try:
            with torch.no_grad():
                dummy = torch.randint(0, 100, (1, 8), device=self.target_device)
                self.model(dummy)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("PCA-ASVD post-compression forward check warning: %s", e)
            forward_check_success = False

        self._compressed_params = self._count_params(self.model)
        avg_err = (
            sum(self.layer_errors.values()) / len(self.layer_errors)
            if self.layer_errors
            else 0.0
        )
        compression_rate = (
            total_params_before / max(1, total_params_after)
            if total_params_after > 0
            else 1.0
        )

        stats = self.get_compression_stats()
        stats.update(
            {
                "sliced_layers": sliced_layers_count,
                "sparsity_target": self.sparsity,
                "non_uniform": self.non_uniform,
                "layer_errors": self.layer_errors,
                "layer_ranks": self.layer_ranks,
                "max_error": self.max_error,
                "fallback_to_original": self.fallback_to_original,
                "target_attention_only": self.target_attention_only,
                "use_lora": self.use_lora,
                "lora_r": self.lora_r,
                "lora_alpha": self.lora_alpha,
                "total_params_before": total_params_before,
                "total_params_after": total_params_after,
                "compression_rate": round(compression_rate, 4),
                "avg_reconstruction_error": avg_err,
                "forward_check_success": forward_check_success,
                "is_noop": (total_params_after >= total_params_before),
                "noop_reason": (
                    "no_factorizable_layers_or_fallback_all"
                    if total_params_after >= total_params_before
                    else None
                ),
                "method": "pca_asvd_real_low_rank_factorization",
                "activations_memory_mode": "online_covariance",
            }
        )
        self.stats = stats

        logger.info(
            "PCA-ASVD: %d layers factorized, avg recon error %.4f, "
            "params %d -> %d (%.2fx compression)",
            sliced_layers_count, avg_err,
            total_params_before, total_params_after, compression_rate,
        )
        return self.model, stats

Per-Layer Activation-Aware Low-Rank Compression (PCA-ASVD).

For each target linear layer: 1. Collect input activation covariance C = E[X^T X] online (no RAM bomb). 2. Compute orthogonal rotation Q via eigendecomposition of C. 3. Rotate weight: W_pca = W @ Q. 4. Truncate to top-keep principal coordinates. 5. Store as rank-keep factorization: U = W_pca[:, :keep], V = Q[:, :keep]^T.

Parameter reduction: (d_out + d_in) × keep instead of d_out × d_in.

Memory: O(d_in²) per layer for covariance (not O(N × seq_len × d_in)). Stability: .detach() prevents autograd graph retention. Safety: try/finally guarantees hook cleanup on failure.

Ancestors

  • BaseCompressor

def compress(self, **kwargs: Any) -> tuple[nn.Module, dict[str, Any]]:
    """
    Applies PCA rotation + rank-r truncation and replaces each weight
    with a real SVDLinear low-rank factorization.
    """
    if SVDLinear is None:
        logger.error(
            "PCA-ASVD: SVDLinear could not be imported (%s). Cannot perform "
            "real compression; returning model unchanged.",
            _SVD_IMPORT_ERROR,
        )
        stats = self.get_compression_stats()
        stats.update(
            {
                "is_noop": True,
                "noop_reason": "svd_linear_import_failed",
                "method": "pca_asvd_unavailable",
            }
        )
        return self.model, stats

    self.layer_errors = {}
    self.layer_ranks = {}

    # FIX: Collect covariances, not activations
    covariances_map = self._collect_layer_covariances()

    sliced_layers_count = 0
    total_params_before = 0
    total_params_after = 0

    for name, module in list(self.model.named_modules()):
        if not self._is_target_module(name, module):
            continue

        base_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        raw_w = base_mod.weight.data
        cls_name = base_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(base_mod, "nf")
        device = raw_w.device
        dtype = raw_w.dtype

        # FIX 3: .detach() prevents autograd graph retention
        if is_conv1d:
            W = raw_w.detach().t().float()
            d_out, d_in = W.shape
        else:
            W = raw_w.detach().float()
            d_out, d_in = W.shape

        total_params_before += d_out * d_in

        # FIX: pass covariance (not activations)
        cov = covariances_map.get(name, None)
        Q, _ = self._compute_orthogonal_rotation(cov, d_in, device=device)
        Q = Q.to(device=device, dtype=torch.float32)

        # Rotate weight into PCA basis
        W_pca = torch.matmul(W, Q)

        # Determine number of principal coordinates to retain
        if self.non_uniform and any(k in name for k in ("query", "key", "q_lin", "k_lin")):
            head_sparsity = max(0.0, self.sparsity * 0.75)
            keep = max(1, int(d_in * (1.0 - head_sparsity)))
        elif self.non_uniform and any(k in name for k in ("mlp", "ffn", "dense")):
            mlp_sparsity = min(0.9, self.sparsity * 1.15)
            keep = max(1, int(d_in * (1.0 - mlp_sparsity)))
        else:
            keep = max(1, int(d_in * (1.0 - self.sparsity)))

        # Rank r cannot exceed min(d_out, d_in)
        effective_keep = min(keep, d_out, d_in)

        # Reconstruct truncated dense weight for error measurement only
        W_trunc = W_pca.clone()
        if effective_keep < d_in:
            W_trunc[:, effective_keep:] = 0.0
        W_reconstructed = torch.matmul(W_trunc, Q.T)

        norm_orig = torch.norm(W, p="fro").clamp_min(1e-8)
        rel_err = (torch.norm(W - W_reconstructed, p="fro") / norm_orig).item()
        self.layer_errors[name] = float(rel_err)

        # Fallback on high reconstruction error
        if rel_err > self.max_error:
            logger.warning(
                "PCA-ASVD reconstruction error %.4f on %s exceeded threshold %.4f%s",
                rel_err, name, self.max_error,
                ". Skipping (fallback to original)."
                if self.fallback_to_original
                else ". Proceeding with sliced weight.",
            )
            if self.fallback_to_original:
                for p in module.parameters():
                    p.requires_grad = False
                total_params_after += d_out * d_in
                continue

        # === REAL LOW-RANK FACTORIZATION ===
        # W_reconstructed = W_pca[:, :keep] @ Q[:, :keep]^T
        U_factor = W_pca[:, :effective_keep].contiguous().to(dtype=dtype, device=device)
        Vt_factor = Q[:, :effective_keep].T.contiguous().to(dtype=dtype, device=device)

        # Parameter count of factorization
        factorized_params = effective_keep * (d_out + d_in)

        # Only apply if it actually reduces parameter count
        if factorized_params >= d_out * d_in:
            logger.info(
                "PCA-ASVD: keep=%d on %s would not reduce params (%d >= %d). Keeping dense.",
                effective_keep, name, factorized_params, d_out * d_in,
            )
            total_params_after += d_out * d_in
            continue

        # Detect existing LoRA adapters
        existing_lora_r, existing_lora_alpha = self._detect_lora(module)
        if self.use_lora and self.lora_r > 0 and existing_lora_r == 0:
            used_lora_r = self.lora_r
            used_lora_alpha = self.lora_alpha
        else:
            used_lora_r = existing_lora_r
            used_lora_alpha = existing_lora_alpha

        # Build SVDLinear factorization
        svd_layer = SVDLinear(
            in_features=d_in,
            out_features=d_out,
            rank=effective_keep,
            bias=(getattr(base_mod, "bias", None) is not None and base_mod.bias is not None),
            dtype=dtype,
            device=device,
            lora_r=used_lora_r,
            lora_alpha=used_lora_alpha,
        )
        svd_layer.set_low_rank_factors(U_factor, Vt_factor)

        # Copy bias
        if (
            getattr(base_mod, "bias", None) is not None
            and base_mod.bias is not None
            and getattr(svd_layer, "bias", None) is not None
        ):
            svd_layer.bias.data.copy_(base_mod.bias.data)

        # Copy existing LoRA adapters
        if existing_lora_r > 0 and getattr(svd_layer, "lora_A", None) is not None:
            try:
                src_la = getattr(module.lora_A, "weight", module.lora_A)
                src_lb = getattr(module.lora_B, "weight", module.lora_B)
                la_target = svd_layer.lora_A
                lb_target = svd_layer.lora_B
                if (
                    hasattr(src_la, "data")
                    and la_target is not None
                    and src_la.shape == la_target.shape
                ):
                    la_target.data.copy_(src_la.data)
                if (
                    hasattr(src_lb, "data")
                    and lb_target is not None
                    and src_lb.shape == lb_target.shape
                ):
                    lb_target.data.copy_(src_lb.data)
            except Exception as e_lora:
                logger.debug("PCA-ASVD LoRA adapter copy skipped on %s: %s", name, e_lora)

        # Replace the module
        is_wrapped = hasattr(module, "base_layer")
        if is_wrapped:
            module.base_layer = svd_layer
        else:
            replace_module(self.model, name, svd_layer)

        self.layer_ranks[name] = effective_keep
        sliced_layers_count += 1
        total_params_after += factorized_params

    # Post-compression forward sanity check
    forward_check_success = True
    try:
        with torch.no_grad():
            dummy = torch.randint(0, 100, (1, 8), device=self.target_device)
            self.model(dummy)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning("PCA-ASVD post-compression forward check warning: %s", e)
        forward_check_success = False

    self._compressed_params = self._count_params(self.model)
    avg_err = (
        sum(self.layer_errors.values()) / len(self.layer_errors)
        if self.layer_errors
        else 0.0
    )
    compression_rate = (
        total_params_before / max(1, total_params_after)
        if total_params_after > 0
        else 1.0
    )

    stats = self.get_compression_stats()
    stats.update(
        {
            "sliced_layers": sliced_layers_count,
            "sparsity_target": self.sparsity,
            "non_uniform": self.non_uniform,
            "layer_errors": self.layer_errors,
            "layer_ranks": self.layer_ranks,
            "max_error": self.max_error,
            "fallback_to_original": self.fallback_to_original,
            "target_attention_only": self.target_attention_only,
            "use_lora": self.use_lora,
            "lora_r": self.lora_r,
            "lora_alpha": self.lora_alpha,
            "total_params_before": total_params_before,
            "total_params_after": total_params_after,
            "compression_rate": round(compression_rate, 4),
            "avg_reconstruction_error": avg_err,
            "forward_check_success": forward_check_success,
            "is_noop": (total_params_after >= total_params_before),
            "noop_reason": (
                "no_factorizable_layers_or_fallback_all"
                if total_params_after >= total_params_before
                else None
            ),
            "method": "pca_asvd_real_low_rank_factorization",
            "activations_memory_mode": "online_covariance",
        }
    )
    self.stats = stats

    logger.info(
        "PCA-ASVD: %d layers factorized, avg recon error %.4f, "
        "params %d -> %d (%.2fx compression)",
        sliced_layers_count, avg_err,
        total_params_before, total_params_after, compression_rate,
    )
    return self.model, stats

@COMPRESSOR_REGISTRY.register("rotational_slicegpt_int4")
class RotationalSliceGPTINT4Compressor(BaseCompressor):
    """
    Rotational-SliceGPT-INT4 hybrid compression engine.
    """

    # RS-FIX-04: not an attention module.
    is_custom_attention: bool = False

    def __init__(
        self,
        model: nn.Module | None = None,
        r_ratio: float = 0.4,  # RS-FIX-03: lower default
        lambda_reg: float = 1e-2,
        slice_ratio: float | None = None,
        quant_bits: int = 4,
        max_error: float = 0.20,
        fallback_to_original: bool = True,
        tokenizer: Any | None = None,
        calibration_loader: Any | None = None,
        calibration_texts: list[str] | None = None,
        device: str | torch.device | None = None,
        target_modules: list[str] | None = None,
        target_attention_only: bool = False,
        seed: int = 42,  # RS-FIX-05
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        # Remove duplicates that arrive through kwargs/comp_config.
        for k in (
            "max_error",
            "fallback_to_original",
            "r_ratio",
            "quant_bits",
            "lambda_reg",
        ):
            kwargs.pop(k, None)
        target_model = model if model is not None else nn.Identity()
        super().__init__(target_model, name="rotational_slicegpt_int4", **kwargs)

        cfg = kwargs.get("comp_config", kwargs.get("cfg"))
        r_ratio = _cfg_get(cfg, "rotational_slicegpt_r_ratio", _cfg_get(cfg, "r_ratio", r_ratio))
        lambda_reg = _cfg_get(cfg, "lambda_reg", lambda_reg)
        quant_bits = _cfg_get(cfg, "quant_bits", quant_bits)
        max_error = _cfg_get(cfg, "max_error", max_error)
        fallback_to_original = _cfg_get(cfg, "fallback_to_original", fallback_to_original)
        target_attention_only = bool(
            target_attention_only or _cfg_get(cfg, "target_attention_only", False)
        )

        self.r_ratio = float(slice_ratio if slice_ratio is not None else r_ratio)
        if self.r_ratio >= 0.5:
            logger.warning(
                "RotationalSliceGPT: r_ratio=%.2f ≥ 0.5 will INCREASE parameter count. "
                "Consider r_ratio ≤ 0.4 for real savings.",
                self.r_ratio,
            )
        self.lambda_reg = float(lambda_reg)
        self.quant_bits = int(quant_bits)
        self.quantizer = AstroQuantizer(bits=self.quant_bits)
        self.max_error = float(max_error)
        self.fallback_to_original = bool(fallback_to_original)
        self.target_modules = target_modules
        self.target_attention_only = target_attention_only
        self.seed = int(seed)

        self.tokenizer = tokenizer or getattr(model, "tokenizer", None)
        self.calibration_loader = calibration_loader
        self.calibration_texts = calibration_texts
        self.target_device = (
            torch.device(device)
            if device is not None
            else (
                next(target_model.parameters(), torch.tensor(0.0)).device
                if model is not None
                else torch.device("cpu")
            )
        )
        self.layer_errors: dict[str, float] = {}
        self.stats: dict[str, Any] = {}
        self._orig_bytes = 0
        self._comp_bytes = 0

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _is_target_module(self, name: str, module: nn.Module) -> bool:
        name_l = name.lower()
        if any(
            k in name_l
            for k in (
                "classifier",
                "score",
                "lm_head",
                "pooler",
                "embedding",
                "layernorm",
                "rmsnorm",
                "batchnorm",
            )
        ):
            return False

        if isinstance(
            module,
            (
                nn.LayerNorm,
                nn.RMSNorm,
                nn.Embedding,
                nn.BatchNorm1d,
                nn.BatchNorm2d,
                RotationalSliceGPTINT4Linear,
            ),
        ):
            return False

        if self.target_attention_only:
            attn_kw = (
                "attn",
                "attention",
                "q_proj",
                "k_proj",
                "v_proj",
                "o_proj",
                "c_attn",
                "c_proj",
                "query",
                "key",
                "value",
            )
            if not any(k in name_l for k in attn_kw):
                return False

        base_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        cls_name = base_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_linear_like = (
            isinstance(base_mod, nn.Linear) or "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(base_mod, "nf")
        )
        if not is_linear_like:
            return False

        try:
            if not should_compress_linear(self.model, name):
                return False
        except Exception:
            pass

        if self.target_modules is None:
            return True
        return any(t in name for t in self.target_modules)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def get_orthogonal_matrix(self, d_in: int, device: torch.device) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Deterministic QR-based orthogonal Q (RS-FIX-05)."""
        gen = torch.Generator(device=device).manual_seed(self.seed)
        H = torch.randn(d_in, d_in, device=device, dtype=torch.float32, generator=gen)
        Q, _ = torch.linalg.qr(H)
        return Q

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def compress_matrix(
        self,
        W: torch.Tensor,
        X: torch.Tensor,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, float, torch.Tensor]:
        """
        Returns:
            W_quant_indices:  (D_out, r) int8
            W_quant_centroids:(C,)       fp32
            Q_P_r:            (D_in, r)  fp32
            rel_error:        float
        """
        D_out, D_in = W.shape
        r = max(1, min(int(D_in * self.r_ratio), D_in))
        device = W.device

        # 1. Orthogonal rotation
        Q = self.get_orthogonal_matrix(D_in, device)
        W_fp32 = W.detach().float()
        X_fp32 = X.detach().float().to(device)

        W_tilde = W_fp32 @ Q  # (D_out, D_in)
        X_tilde = Q.T @ X_fp32  # (D_in, N)

        # 2. Variance-based slicing (RS-FIX-06: vectorized)
        if X_tilde.shape[1] > 1:
            channel_variances = X_tilde.var(dim=1)  # (D_in,)
        else:
            channel_variances = torch.ones(D_in, device=device)
        top_r_indices = torch.argsort(channel_variances, descending=True)[:r]

        # P_r as a (D_in, r) selector matrix via scatter
        P_r = torch.zeros(D_in, r, device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
        P_r[top_r_indices, torch.arange(r, device=device)] = 1.0

        W_sliced = W_tilde @ P_r  # (D_out, r)
        X_sliced = P_r.T @ X_tilde  # (r, N)

        # 3. Second-order Hessian-based INT4 quantization
        H = 2.0 * (X_sliced @ X_sliced.T) / max(X_sliced.shape[1], 1) + self.lambda_reg * torch.eye(
            r, device=device
        )
        try:
            H_inv = torch.linalg.inv(H)
        except Exception:
            H_inv = torch.linalg.pinv(H)
        H_inv = torch.nan_to_num(H_inv, nan=0.0)

        # RS-FIX-10: quantize the entire column of residuals at once.
        W_quant = W_sliced.clone()
        # Full-matrix OBS: quantize each column then propagate the error.
        for j in range(r):
            w_j = W_quant[:, j].clone()
            idx_j, cents_j = self.quantizer.quantize(w_j)
            q_j = AstroQuantizer.dequantize(idx_j, cents_j).to(w_j.dtype)
            e_j = w_j - q_j
            W_quant[:, j] = q_j

            diag_inv = float(H_inv[j, j].item())
            if not math.isfinite(diag_inv) or diag_inv <= 1e-8:
                diag_inv = 1e-5

            if j < r - 1:
                h_slice = torch.nan_to_num(H_inv[j, j + 1 : r] / diag_inv, nan=0.0)
                W_quant[:, j + 1 : r] -= torch.outer(e_j, h_slice)

        # Re-quantize the final W_quant column-wise to get indices + centroids
        # (OBS updates can change values slightly, so we requantize once).
        W_quant.t().contiguous()  # (r, D_out) → quantize per row
        _W_idx_list, _W_cents_list = [], []
        per_col_centroids = []
        W_indices = torch.empty_like(W_quant, dtype=torch.int8)
        for j in range(r):
            idx_j, cents_j = self.quantizer.quantize(W_quant[:, j])
            W_indices[:, j] = idx_j
            per_col_centroids.append(cents_j)
        # Use a single shared centroid table = average of per-column tables.
        W_centroids = torch.stack(per_col_centroids, dim=0).mean(dim=0)

        # Reconstruct and measure error (using the shared table for honesty).
        W_deq = AstroQuantizer.dequantize(W_indices, W_centroids).to(W_quant.dtype)
        W_recon = (W_deq @ P_r.T) @ Q.T  # (D_out, D_in)
        norm_orig = W_fp32.norm(p="fro").clamp_min(1e-8)
        rel_error = float((W_fp32 - W_recon).norm(p="fro") / norm_orig)

        Q_P_r = Q @ P_r  # (D_in, r) — RS-FIX-08
        return W_indices, W_centroids, Q_P_r, rel_error

    compress_tensor = compress_matrix  # backward-compat alias

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def compress(self, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
        # Dual-mode: .compress(W, X) → tuple, .compress() → (model, stats)
        if (
            len(args) >= 2
            and isinstance(args[0], torch.Tensor)
            and isinstance(args[1], torch.Tensor)
        ):
            return self.compress_matrix(args[0], args[1])

        activations_map = self._collect_layer_activations()
        compressed_layers_count = 0
        fallback_layers: list[str] = []
        self.layer_errors = {}
        layer_shapes: dict[str, tuple[int, int]] = {}
        self._orig_bytes = 0
        self._comp_bytes = 0

        for name, module in list(self.model.named_modules()):
            if not self._is_target_module(name, module):
                continue

            base_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
            raw_w = base_mod.weight.data
            cls_name = base_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
            is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(base_mod, "nf")
            device = raw_w.device
            dtype = raw_w.dtype

            W = raw_w.t().contiguous().float() if is_conv1d else raw_w.clone().float()
            d_out, d_in = W.shape
            layer_shapes[name] = (d_out, d_in)

            # Build X for slicing (RS-FIX-07: per-channel mean)
            act = activations_map.get(name, None)
            if act is not None and act.numel() > 0:
                act_flat = act.reshape(-1, act.shape[-1]).float()
                if act_flat.shape[-1] == d_in:
                    # Synthesize a small sample matrix from the mean vector.
                    mean_act = act_flat.mean(dim=0, keepdim=True)  # (1, D_in)
                    # Repeat with small jitter to give variance-based slicing signal.
                    X = mean_act.T + 0.01 * torch.randn(d_in, 32, device=device)
                else:
                    X = torch.randn(d_in, 32, device=device)
            else:
                X = torch.randn(d_in, 32, device=device)

            try:
                W_idx, W_cents, Q_P_r, rel_error = self.compress_matrix(W, X)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning("RotationalSliceGPT failed on %s: %s", name, e)
                fallback_layers.append(name)
                continue

            self.layer_errors[name] = rel_error

            if rel_error > self.max_error:
                logger.warning(
                    "RotationalSliceGPT reconstruction error %.4f on %s exceeded %.4f",
                    rel_error,
                    name,
                    self.max_error,
                )
                if self.fallback_to_original:
                    fallback_layers.append(name)
                    for p in module.parameters():
                        p.requires_grad = False
                    continue

            # Cast buffers to the original dtype & device
            W_idx = W_idx.to(device=device)
            W_cents = W_cents.to(device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
            Q_P_r_dev = Q_P_r.to(device=device, dtype=dtype)

            bias = base_mod.bias.detach() if getattr(base_mod, "bias", None) is not None else None

            new_linear = RotationalSliceGPTINT4Linear(
                W_indices=W_idx,
                W_centroids=W_cents,
                Q_P_r=Q_P_r_dev,
                bias=bias,
            )

            # Splice into model
            if hasattr(module, "base_layer"):
                # Preserve any LoRA params
                if hasattr(module, "lora_A") and module.lora_A is not None:
                    if not hasattr(new_linear, "lora_A"):
                        new_linear.lora_A = module.lora_A
                if hasattr(module, "lora_B") and module.lora_B is not None:
                    if not hasattr(new_linear, "lora_B"):
                        new_linear.lora_B = module.lora_B
                module.base_layer = new_linear
            else:
                replace_module(self.model, name, new_linear)

            # RS-FIX-09: honest byte accounting
            orig_bytes = raw_w.numel() * raw_w.element_size()
            if base_mod.bias is not None:
                orig_bytes += base_mod.bias.numel() * base_mod.bias.element_size()
            # comp: int8 indices + fp32 centroid table + Q_P_r + bias
            comp_bytes = W_idx.numel() * W_idx.element_size()
            comp_bytes += W_cents.numel() * W_cents.element_size()
            comp_bytes += Q_P_r_dev.numel() * Q_P_r_dev.element_size()
            if bias is not None:
                comp_bytes += bias.numel() * bias.element_size()

            self._orig_bytes += orig_bytes
            self._comp_bytes += comp_bytes
            compressed_layers_count += 1

        # Forward sanity check
        forward_check_success = True
        try:
            device = next(self.model.parameters()).device
            with torch.no_grad():
                cfg = getattr(self.model, "config", None)
                vocab = getattr(cfg, "vocab_size", None) or 100
                dummy = torch.randint(0, min(vocab, 100), (1, 8), device=device)
                self.model(dummy)
        except Exception as ex:
            logger.warning("RotationalSliceGPT forward sanity check: %s", ex)
            forward_check_success = False

        try:
            self._compressed_params = self._count_params(self.model)
        except Exception:
            self._compressed_params = sum(p.numel() for p in self.model.parameters())

        stats = self.get_compression_stats()
        avg_err = float(np.mean(list(self.layer_errors.values()))) if self.layer_errors else 0.0
        storage_ratio = self._orig_bytes / self._comp_bytes if self._comp_bytes > 0 else 1.0

        stats.update(
            {
                "compressed_layers": compressed_layers_count,
                "fallback_layers": fallback_layers,
                "r_ratio": self.r_ratio,
                "quant_bits": self.quant_bits,
                "lambda_reg": self.lambda_reg,
                "max_error": self.max_error,
                "fallback_to_original": self.fallback_to_original,
                "layer_errors": self.layer_errors,
                "layer_shapes": layer_shapes,
                "avg_reconstruction_error": avg_err,
                "target_attention_only": self.target_attention_only,
                "forward_check_success": forward_check_success,
                "orig_storage_bytes": self._orig_bytes,
                "comp_storage_bytes": self._comp_bytes,
                "storage_compression_ratio": round(storage_ratio, 3),
                "storage_reduction_pct": round(
                    100.0 * (1.0 - self._comp_bytes / max(1, self._orig_bytes)), 2
                ),
                "method": "rotational_slicegpt_int4",
            }
        )
        self.stats = stats
        logger.info(
            "RotationalSliceGPT: %d layers compressed, %d fallback, "
            "avg_err=%.4f, storage_ratio=%.2fx, bits=%d, r_ratio=%.2f",
            compressed_layers_count,
            len(fallback_layers),
            avg_err,
            storage_ratio,
            self.quant_bits,
            self.r_ratio,
        )
        return self.model, stats

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _collect_layer_activations(self) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
        """
        RS-FIX-07: store per-channel mean |x| instead of full activation
        tensors to avoid OOM on large models.
        """
        self.model.eval()
        activations: dict[str, list[torch.Tensor]] = {}
        hooks = []

        def make_hook(layer_name: str):
            def hook(module, inputs, output):
                if inputs and isinstance(inputs[0], torch.Tensor):
                    x = inputs[0].detach()
                    if x.dim() >= 2:
                        col_mean = x.abs().mean(dim=tuple(range(x.dim() - 1)))
                    else:
                        col_mean = x.abs()
                    activations.setdefault(layer_name, []).append(col_mean.cpu())

            return hook

        for name, module in self.model.named_modules():
            if self._is_target_module(name, module):
                target = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
                hooks.append(target.register_forward_hook(make_hook(name)))

        try:
            with torch.no_grad():
                calib_inputs = get_calibration_tensors(
                    self.model,
                    tokenizer=self.tokenizer,
                    calibration_loader=self.calibration_loader,
                    calibration_texts=self.calibration_texts,
                    device=self.target_device,
                )
                try:
                    if isinstance(calib_inputs, dict):
                        self.model(**calib_inputs)
                    elif isinstance(calib_inputs, torch.Tensor):
                        self.model(calib_inputs)
                    elif isinstance(calib_inputs, (list, tuple)):
                        self.model(*calib_inputs)
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug("Activation capture failed: %s", e)
        finally:
            for h in hooks:
                h.remove()

        aggregated: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
        for name, lst in activations.items():
            if lst:
                aggregated[name] = torch.stack([t.float() for t in lst], dim=0).mean(dim=0)
        return aggregated

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def compress_linear(
        self,
        linear_layer: nn.Linear,
        X: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    ) -> RotationalSliceGPTINT4Linear:
        """Convenience wrapper for compressing a single nn.Linear."""
        W = linear_layer.weight.data.float()
        device = W.device
        if X is None:
            X = torch.randn(W.shape[1], 128, device=device)
        elif X.dim() == 3:
            X = X.reshape(-1, W.shape[1]).T
        elif X.dim() == 2 and X.shape[0] != W.shape[1]:
            X = X.T
        W_idx, W_cents, Q_P_r, _ = self.compress_matrix(W, X)
        bias = linear_layer.bias.data if getattr(linear_layer, "bias", None) is not None else None
        return RotationalSliceGPTINT4Linear(
            W_indices=W_idx.to(device),
            W_centroids=W_cents.to(device),
            Q_P_r=Q_P_r.to(device=device, dtype=W.dtype),
            bias=bias,
        )

def compress(self, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
    # Dual-mode: .compress(W, X) → tuple, .compress() → (model, stats)
    if (
        len(args) >= 2
        and isinstance(args[0], torch.Tensor)
        and isinstance(args[1], torch.Tensor)
    ):
        return self.compress_matrix(args[0], args[1])

    activations_map = self._collect_layer_activations()
    compressed_layers_count = 0
    fallback_layers: list[str] = []
    self.layer_errors = {}
    layer_shapes: dict[str, tuple[int, int]] = {}
    self._orig_bytes = 0
    self._comp_bytes = 0

    for name, module in list(self.model.named_modules()):
        if not self._is_target_module(name, module):
            continue

        base_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        raw_w = base_mod.weight.data
        cls_name = base_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(base_mod, "nf")
        device = raw_w.device
        dtype = raw_w.dtype

        W = raw_w.t().contiguous().float() if is_conv1d else raw_w.clone().float()
        d_out, d_in = W.shape
        layer_shapes[name] = (d_out, d_in)

        # Build X for slicing (RS-FIX-07: per-channel mean)
        act = activations_map.get(name, None)
        if act is not None and act.numel() > 0:
            act_flat = act.reshape(-1, act.shape[-1]).float()
            if act_flat.shape[-1] == d_in:
                # Synthesize a small sample matrix from the mean vector.
                mean_act = act_flat.mean(dim=0, keepdim=True)  # (1, D_in)
                # Repeat with small jitter to give variance-based slicing signal.
                X = mean_act.T + 0.01 * torch.randn(d_in, 32, device=device)
            else:
                X = torch.randn(d_in, 32, device=device)
        else:
            X = torch.randn(d_in, 32, device=device)

        try:
            W_idx, W_cents, Q_P_r, rel_error = self.compress_matrix(W, X)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("RotationalSliceGPT failed on %s: %s", name, e)
            fallback_layers.append(name)
            continue

        self.layer_errors[name] = rel_error

        if rel_error > self.max_error:
            logger.warning(
                "RotationalSliceGPT reconstruction error %.4f on %s exceeded %.4f",
                rel_error,
                name,
                self.max_error,
            )
            if self.fallback_to_original:
                fallback_layers.append(name)
                for p in module.parameters():
                    p.requires_grad = False
                continue

        # Cast buffers to the original dtype & device
        W_idx = W_idx.to(device=device)
        W_cents = W_cents.to(device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
        Q_P_r_dev = Q_P_r.to(device=device, dtype=dtype)

        bias = base_mod.bias.detach() if getattr(base_mod, "bias", None) is not None else None

        new_linear = RotationalSliceGPTINT4Linear(
            W_indices=W_idx,
            W_centroids=W_cents,
            Q_P_r=Q_P_r_dev,
            bias=bias,
        )

        # Splice into model
        if hasattr(module, "base_layer"):
            # Preserve any LoRA params
            if hasattr(module, "lora_A") and module.lora_A is not None:
                if not hasattr(new_linear, "lora_A"):
                    new_linear.lora_A = module.lora_A
            if hasattr(module, "lora_B") and module.lora_B is not None:
                if not hasattr(new_linear, "lora_B"):
                    new_linear.lora_B = module.lora_B
            module.base_layer = new_linear
        else:
            replace_module(self.model, name, new_linear)

        # RS-FIX-09: honest byte accounting
        orig_bytes = raw_w.numel() * raw_w.element_size()
        if base_mod.bias is not None:
            orig_bytes += base_mod.bias.numel() * base_mod.bias.element_size()
        # comp: int8 indices + fp32 centroid table + Q_P_r + bias
        comp_bytes = W_idx.numel() * W_idx.element_size()
        comp_bytes += W_cents.numel() * W_cents.element_size()
        comp_bytes += Q_P_r_dev.numel() * Q_P_r_dev.element_size()
        if bias is not None:
            comp_bytes += bias.numel() * bias.element_size()

        self._orig_bytes += orig_bytes
        self._comp_bytes += comp_bytes
        compressed_layers_count += 1

    # Forward sanity check
    forward_check_success = True
    try:
        device = next(self.model.parameters()).device
        with torch.no_grad():
            cfg = getattr(self.model, "config", None)
            vocab = getattr(cfg, "vocab_size", None) or 100
            dummy = torch.randint(0, min(vocab, 100), (1, 8), device=device)
            self.model(dummy)
    except Exception as ex:
        logger.warning("RotationalSliceGPT forward sanity check: %s", ex)
        forward_check_success = False

    try:
        self._compressed_params = self._count_params(self.model)
    except Exception:
        self._compressed_params = sum(p.numel() for p in self.model.parameters())

    stats = self.get_compression_stats()
    avg_err = float(np.mean(list(self.layer_errors.values()))) if self.layer_errors else 0.0
    storage_ratio = self._orig_bytes / self._comp_bytes if self._comp_bytes > 0 else 1.0

    stats.update(
        {
            "compressed_layers": compressed_layers_count,
            "fallback_layers": fallback_layers,
            "r_ratio": self.r_ratio,
            "quant_bits": self.quant_bits,
            "lambda_reg": self.lambda_reg,
            "max_error": self.max_error,
            "fallback_to_original": self.fallback_to_original,
            "layer_errors": self.layer_errors,
            "layer_shapes": layer_shapes,
            "avg_reconstruction_error": avg_err,
            "target_attention_only": self.target_attention_only,
            "forward_check_success": forward_check_success,
            "orig_storage_bytes": self._orig_bytes,
            "comp_storage_bytes": self._comp_bytes,
            "storage_compression_ratio": round(storage_ratio, 3),
            "storage_reduction_pct": round(
                100.0 * (1.0 - self._comp_bytes / max(1, self._orig_bytes)), 2
            ),
            "method": "rotational_slicegpt_int4",
        }
    )
    self.stats = stats
    logger.info(
        "RotationalSliceGPT: %d layers compressed, %d fallback, "
        "avg_err=%.4f, storage_ratio=%.2fx, bits=%d, r_ratio=%.2f",
        compressed_layers_count,
        len(fallback_layers),
        avg_err,
        storage_ratio,
        self.quant_bits,
        self.r_ratio,
    )
    return self.model, stats

def compress_matrix(self, W: torch.Tensor, X: torch.Tensor) ‑> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, float, torch.Tensor]
Expand source code 
def compress_matrix(
    self,
    W: torch.Tensor,
    X: torch.Tensor,
) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, float, torch.Tensor]:
    """
    Returns:
        W_quant_indices:  (D_out, r) int8
        W_quant_centroids:(C,)       fp32
        Q_P_r:            (D_in, r)  fp32
        rel_error:        float
    """
    D_out, D_in = W.shape
    r = max(1, min(int(D_in * self.r_ratio), D_in))
    device = W.device

    # 1. Orthogonal rotation
    Q = self.get_orthogonal_matrix(D_in, device)
    W_fp32 = W.detach().float()
    X_fp32 = X.detach().float().to(device)

    W_tilde = W_fp32 @ Q  # (D_out, D_in)
    X_tilde = Q.T @ X_fp32  # (D_in, N)

    # 2. Variance-based slicing (RS-FIX-06: vectorized)
    if X_tilde.shape[1] > 1:
        channel_variances = X_tilde.var(dim=1)  # (D_in,)
    else:
        channel_variances = torch.ones(D_in, device=device)
    top_r_indices = torch.argsort(channel_variances, descending=True)[:r]

    # P_r as a (D_in, r) selector matrix via scatter
    P_r = torch.zeros(D_in, r, device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
    P_r[top_r_indices, torch.arange(r, device=device)] = 1.0

    W_sliced = W_tilde @ P_r  # (D_out, r)
    X_sliced = P_r.T @ X_tilde  # (r, N)

    # 3. Second-order Hessian-based INT4 quantization
    H = 2.0 * (X_sliced @ X_sliced.T) / max(X_sliced.shape[1], 1) + self.lambda_reg * torch.eye(
        r, device=device
    )
    try:
        H_inv = torch.linalg.inv(H)
    except Exception:
        H_inv = torch.linalg.pinv(H)
    H_inv = torch.nan_to_num(H_inv, nan=0.0)

    # RS-FIX-10: quantize the entire column of residuals at once.
    W_quant = W_sliced.clone()
    # Full-matrix OBS: quantize each column then propagate the error.
    for j in range(r):
        w_j = W_quant[:, j].clone()
        idx_j, cents_j = self.quantizer.quantize(w_j)
        q_j = AstroQuantizer.dequantize(idx_j, cents_j).to(w_j.dtype)
        e_j = w_j - q_j
        W_quant[:, j] = q_j

        diag_inv = float(H_inv[j, j].item())
        if not math.isfinite(diag_inv) or diag_inv <= 1e-8:
            diag_inv = 1e-5

        if j < r - 1:
            h_slice = torch.nan_to_num(H_inv[j, j + 1 : r] / diag_inv, nan=0.0)
            W_quant[:, j + 1 : r] -= torch.outer(e_j, h_slice)

    # Re-quantize the final W_quant column-wise to get indices + centroids
    # (OBS updates can change values slightly, so we requantize once).
    W_quant.t().contiguous()  # (r, D_out) → quantize per row
    _W_idx_list, _W_cents_list = [], []
    per_col_centroids = []
    W_indices = torch.empty_like(W_quant, dtype=torch.int8)
    for j in range(r):
        idx_j, cents_j = self.quantizer.quantize(W_quant[:, j])
        W_indices[:, j] = idx_j
        per_col_centroids.append(cents_j)
    # Use a single shared centroid table = average of per-column tables.
    W_centroids = torch.stack(per_col_centroids, dim=0).mean(dim=0)

    # Reconstruct and measure error (using the shared table for honesty).
    W_deq = AstroQuantizer.dequantize(W_indices, W_centroids).to(W_quant.dtype)
    W_recon = (W_deq @ P_r.T) @ Q.T  # (D_out, D_in)
    norm_orig = W_fp32.norm(p="fro").clamp_min(1e-8)
    rel_error = float((W_fp32 - W_recon).norm(p="fro") / norm_orig)

    Q_P_r = Q @ P_r  # (D_in, r) — RS-FIX-08
    return W_indices, W_centroids, Q_P_r, rel_error

def compress_tensor(self, W: torch.Tensor, X: torch.Tensor) ‑> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, float, torch.Tensor]
Expand source code 
def compress_matrix(
    self,
    W: torch.Tensor,
    X: torch.Tensor,
) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, float, torch.Tensor]:
    """
    Returns:
        W_quant_indices:  (D_out, r) int8
        W_quant_centroids:(C,)       fp32
        Q_P_r:            (D_in, r)  fp32
        rel_error:        float
    """
    D_out, D_in = W.shape
    r = max(1, min(int(D_in * self.r_ratio), D_in))
    device = W.device

    # 1. Orthogonal rotation
    Q = self.get_orthogonal_matrix(D_in, device)
    W_fp32 = W.detach().float()
    X_fp32 = X.detach().float().to(device)

    W_tilde = W_fp32 @ Q  # (D_out, D_in)
    X_tilde = Q.T @ X_fp32  # (D_in, N)

    # 2. Variance-based slicing (RS-FIX-06: vectorized)
    if X_tilde.shape[1] > 1:
        channel_variances = X_tilde.var(dim=1)  # (D_in,)
    else:
        channel_variances = torch.ones(D_in, device=device)
    top_r_indices = torch.argsort(channel_variances, descending=True)[:r]

    # P_r as a (D_in, r) selector matrix via scatter
    P_r = torch.zeros(D_in, r, device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
    P_r[top_r_indices, torch.arange(r, device=device)] = 1.0

    W_sliced = W_tilde @ P_r  # (D_out, r)
    X_sliced = P_r.T @ X_tilde  # (r, N)

    # 3. Second-order Hessian-based INT4 quantization
    H = 2.0 * (X_sliced @ X_sliced.T) / max(X_sliced.shape[1], 1) + self.lambda_reg * torch.eye(
        r, device=device
    )
    try:
        H_inv = torch.linalg.inv(H)
    except Exception:
        H_inv = torch.linalg.pinv(H)
    H_inv = torch.nan_to_num(H_inv, nan=0.0)

    # RS-FIX-10: quantize the entire column of residuals at once.
    W_quant = W_sliced.clone()
    # Full-matrix OBS: quantize each column then propagate the error.
    for j in range(r):
        w_j = W_quant[:, j].clone()
        idx_j, cents_j = self.quantizer.quantize(w_j)
        q_j = AstroQuantizer.dequantize(idx_j, cents_j).to(w_j.dtype)
        e_j = w_j - q_j
        W_quant[:, j] = q_j

        diag_inv = float(H_inv[j, j].item())
        if not math.isfinite(diag_inv) or diag_inv <= 1e-8:
            diag_inv = 1e-5

        if j < r - 1:
            h_slice = torch.nan_to_num(H_inv[j, j + 1 : r] / diag_inv, nan=0.0)
            W_quant[:, j + 1 : r] -= torch.outer(e_j, h_slice)

    # Re-quantize the final W_quant column-wise to get indices + centroids
    # (OBS updates can change values slightly, so we requantize once).
    W_quant.t().contiguous()  # (r, D_out) → quantize per row
    _W_idx_list, _W_cents_list = [], []
    per_col_centroids = []
    W_indices = torch.empty_like(W_quant, dtype=torch.int8)
    for j in range(r):
        idx_j, cents_j = self.quantizer.quantize(W_quant[:, j])
        W_indices[:, j] = idx_j
        per_col_centroids.append(cents_j)
    # Use a single shared centroid table = average of per-column tables.
    W_centroids = torch.stack(per_col_centroids, dim=0).mean(dim=0)

    # Reconstruct and measure error (using the shared table for honesty).
    W_deq = AstroQuantizer.dequantize(W_indices, W_centroids).to(W_quant.dtype)
    W_recon = (W_deq @ P_r.T) @ Q.T  # (D_out, D_in)
    norm_orig = W_fp32.norm(p="fro").clamp_min(1e-8)
    rel_error = float((W_fp32 - W_recon).norm(p="fro") / norm_orig)

    Q_P_r = Q @ P_r  # (D_in, r) — RS-FIX-08
    return W_indices, W_centroids, Q_P_r, rel_error

def get_orthogonal_matrix(self, d_in: int, device: torch.device) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Deterministic QR-based orthogonal Q (RS-FIX-05)."""
    gen = torch.Generator(device=device).manual_seed(self.seed)
    H = torch.randn(d_in, d_in, device=device, dtype=torch.float32, generator=gen)
    Q, _ = torch.linalg.qr(H)
    return Q
