@COMPRESSOR_REGISTRY.register("rotational_slicegpt_int4")
class RotationalSliceGPTINT4Compressor(BaseCompressor):
    """
    Rotational-SliceGPT-INT4 hybrid compression engine.
    """

    # RS-FIX-04: not an attention module.
    is_custom_attention: bool = False

    def __init__(
        self,
        model: nn.Module | None = None,
        r_ratio: float = 0.4,  # RS-FIX-03: lower default
        lambda_reg: float = 1e-2,
        slice_ratio: float | None = None,
        quant_bits: int = 4,
        max_error: float = 0.20,
        fallback_to_original: bool = True,
        tokenizer: Any | None = None,
        calibration_loader: Any | None = None,
        calibration_texts: list[str] | None = None,
        device: str | torch.device | None = None,
        target_modules: list[str] | None = None,
        target_attention_only: bool = False,
        seed: int = 42,  # RS-FIX-05
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        # Remove duplicates that arrive through kwargs/comp_config.
        for k in (
            "max_error",
            "fallback_to_original",
            "r_ratio",
            "quant_bits",
            "lambda_reg",
        ):
            kwargs.pop(k, None)
        target_model = model if model is not None else nn.Identity()
        super().__init__(target_model, name="rotational_slicegpt_int4", **kwargs)

        cfg = kwargs.get("comp_config", kwargs.get("cfg"))
        r_ratio = _cfg_get(cfg, "rotational_slicegpt_r_ratio", _cfg_get(cfg, "r_ratio", r_ratio))
        lambda_reg = _cfg_get(cfg, "lambda_reg", lambda_reg)
        quant_bits = _cfg_get(cfg, "quant_bits", quant_bits)
        max_error = _cfg_get(cfg, "max_error", max_error)
        fallback_to_original = _cfg_get(cfg, "fallback_to_original", fallback_to_original)
        target_attention_only = bool(
            target_attention_only or _cfg_get(cfg, "target_attention_only", False)
        )

        self.r_ratio = float(slice_ratio if slice_ratio is not None else r_ratio)
        if self.r_ratio >= 0.5:
            logger.warning(
                "RotationalSliceGPT: r_ratio=%.2f ≥ 0.5 will INCREASE parameter count. "
                "Consider r_ratio ≤ 0.4 for real savings.",
                self.r_ratio,
            )
        self.lambda_reg = float(lambda_reg)
        self.quant_bits = int(quant_bits)
        self.quantizer = AstroQuantizer(bits=self.quant_bits)
        self.max_error = float(max_error)
        self.fallback_to_original = bool(fallback_to_original)
        self.target_modules = target_modules
        self.target_attention_only = target_attention_only
        self.seed = int(seed)

        self.tokenizer = tokenizer or getattr(model, "tokenizer", None)
        self.calibration_loader = calibration_loader
        self.calibration_texts = calibration_texts
        self.target_device = (
            torch.device(device)
            if device is not None
            else (
                next(target_model.parameters(), torch.tensor(0.0)).device
                if model is not None
                else torch.device("cpu")
            )
        )
        self.layer_errors: dict[str, float] = {}
        self.stats: dict[str, Any] = {}
        self._orig_bytes = 0
        self._comp_bytes = 0

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _is_target_module(self, name: str, module: nn.Module) -> bool:
        name_l = name.lower()
        if any(
            k in name_l
            for k in (
                "classifier",
                "score",
                "lm_head",
                "pooler",
                "embedding",
                "layernorm",
                "rmsnorm",
                "batchnorm",
            )
        ):
            return False

        if isinstance(
            module,
            (
                nn.LayerNorm,
                nn.RMSNorm,
                nn.Embedding,
                nn.BatchNorm1d,
                nn.BatchNorm2d,
                RotationalSliceGPTINT4Linear,
            ),
        ):
            return False

        if self.target_attention_only:
            attn_kw = (
                "attn",
                "attention",
                "q_proj",
                "k_proj",
                "v_proj",
                "o_proj",
                "c_attn",
                "c_proj",
                "query",
                "key",
                "value",
            )
            if not any(k in name_l for k in attn_kw):
                return False

        base_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        cls_name = base_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_linear_like = (
            isinstance(base_mod, nn.Linear) or "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(base_mod, "nf")
        )
        if not is_linear_like:
            return False

        try:
            if not should_compress_linear(self.model, name):
                return False
        except Exception:
            pass

        if self.target_modules is None:
            return True
        return any(t in name for t in self.target_modules)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def get_orthogonal_matrix(self, d_in: int, device: torch.device) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Deterministic QR-based orthogonal Q (RS-FIX-05)."""
        gen = torch.Generator(device=device).manual_seed(self.seed)
        H = torch.randn(d_in, d_in, device=device, dtype=torch.float32, generator=gen)
        Q, _ = torch.linalg.qr(H)
        return Q

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def compress_matrix(
        self,
        W: torch.Tensor,
        X: torch.Tensor,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, float, torch.Tensor]:
        """
        Returns:
            W_quant_indices:  (D_out, r) int8
            W_quant_centroids:(C,)       fp32
            Q_P_r:            (D_in, r)  fp32
            rel_error:        float
        """
        D_out, D_in = W.shape
        r = max(1, min(int(D_in * self.r_ratio), D_in))
        device = W.device

        # 1. Orthogonal rotation
        Q = self.get_orthogonal_matrix(D_in, device)
        W_fp32 = W.detach().float()
        X_fp32 = X.detach().float().to(device)

        W_tilde = W_fp32 @ Q  # (D_out, D_in)
        X_tilde = Q.T @ X_fp32  # (D_in, N)

        # 2. Variance-based slicing (RS-FIX-06: vectorized)
        if X_tilde.shape[1] > 1:
            channel_variances = X_tilde.var(dim=1)  # (D_in,)
        else:
            channel_variances = torch.ones(D_in, device=device)
        top_r_indices = torch.argsort(channel_variances, descending=True)[:r]

        # P_r as a (D_in, r) selector matrix via scatter
        P_r = torch.zeros(D_in, r, device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
        P_r[top_r_indices, torch.arange(r, device=device)] = 1.0

        W_sliced = W_tilde @ P_r  # (D_out, r)
        X_sliced = P_r.T @ X_tilde  # (r, N)

        # 3. Second-order Hessian-based INT4 quantization
        H = 2.0 * (X_sliced @ X_sliced.T) / max(X_sliced.shape[1], 1) + self.lambda_reg * torch.eye(
            r, device=device
        )
        try:
            H_inv = torch.linalg.inv(H)
        except Exception:
            H_inv = torch.linalg.pinv(H)
        H_inv = torch.nan_to_num(H_inv, nan=0.0)

        # RS-FIX-10: quantize the entire column of residuals at once.
        W_quant = W_sliced.clone()
        # Full-matrix OBS: quantize each column then propagate the error.
        for j in range(r):
            w_j = W_quant[:, j].clone()
            idx_j, cents_j = self.quantizer.quantize(w_j)
            q_j = AstroQuantizer.dequantize(idx_j, cents_j).to(w_j.dtype)
            e_j = w_j - q_j
            W_quant[:, j] = q_j

            diag_inv = float(H_inv[j, j].item())
            if not math.isfinite(diag_inv) or diag_inv <= 1e-8:
                diag_inv = 1e-5

            if j < r - 1:
                h_slice = torch.nan_to_num(H_inv[j, j + 1 : r] / diag_inv, nan=0.0)
                W_quant[:, j + 1 : r] -= torch.outer(e_j, h_slice)

        # Re-quantize the final W_quant column-wise to get indices + centroids
        # (OBS updates can change values slightly, so we requantize once).
        W_quant.t().contiguous()  # (r, D_out) → quantize per row
        _W_idx_list, _W_cents_list = [], []
        per_col_centroids = []
        W_indices = torch.empty_like(W_quant, dtype=torch.int8)
        for j in range(r):
            idx_j, cents_j = self.quantizer.quantize(W_quant[:, j])
            W_indices[:, j] = idx_j
            per_col_centroids.append(cents_j)
        # Use a single shared centroid table = average of per-column tables.
        W_centroids = torch.stack(per_col_centroids, dim=0).mean(dim=0)

        # Reconstruct and measure error (using the shared table for honesty).
        W_deq = AstroQuantizer.dequantize(W_indices, W_centroids).to(W_quant.dtype)
        W_recon = (W_deq @ P_r.T) @ Q.T  # (D_out, D_in)
        norm_orig = W_fp32.norm(p="fro").clamp_min(1e-8)
        rel_error = float((W_fp32 - W_recon).norm(p="fro") / norm_orig)

        Q_P_r = Q @ P_r  # (D_in, r) — RS-FIX-08
        return W_indices, W_centroids, Q_P_r, rel_error

    compress_tensor = compress_matrix  # backward-compat alias

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def compress(self, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
        # Dual-mode: .compress(W, X) → tuple, .compress() → (model, stats)
        if (
            len(args) >= 2
            and isinstance(args[0], torch.Tensor)
            and isinstance(args[1], torch.Tensor)
        ):
            return self.compress_matrix(args[0], args[1])

        activations_map = self._collect_layer_activations()
        compressed_layers_count = 0
        fallback_layers: list[str] = []
        self.layer_errors = {}
        layer_shapes: dict[str, tuple[int, int]] = {}
        self._orig_bytes = 0
        self._comp_bytes = 0

        for name, module in list(self.model.named_modules()):
            if not self._is_target_module(name, module):
                continue

            base_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
            raw_w = base_mod.weight.data
            cls_name = base_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
            is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(base_mod, "nf")
            device = raw_w.device
            dtype = raw_w.dtype

            W = raw_w.t().contiguous().float() if is_conv1d else raw_w.clone().float()
            d_out, d_in = W.shape
            layer_shapes[name] = (d_out, d_in)

            # Build X for slicing (RS-FIX-07: per-channel mean)
            act = activations_map.get(name, None)
            if act is not None and act.numel() > 0:
                act_flat = act.reshape(-1, act.shape[-1]).float()
                if act_flat.shape[-1] == d_in:
                    # Synthesize a small sample matrix from the mean vector.
                    mean_act = act_flat.mean(dim=0, keepdim=True)  # (1, D_in)
                    # Repeat with small jitter to give variance-based slicing signal.
                    X = mean_act.T + 0.01 * torch.randn(d_in, 32, device=device)
                else:
                    X = torch.randn(d_in, 32, device=device)
            else:
                X = torch.randn(d_in, 32, device=device)

            try:
                W_idx, W_cents, Q_P_r, rel_error = self.compress_matrix(W, X)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning("RotationalSliceGPT failed on %s: %s", name, e)
                fallback_layers.append(name)
                continue

            self.layer_errors[name] = rel_error

            if rel_error > self.max_error:
                logger.warning(
                    "RotationalSliceGPT reconstruction error %.4f on %s exceeded %.4f",
                    rel_error,
                    name,
                    self.max_error,
                )
                if self.fallback_to_original:
                    fallback_layers.append(name)
                    for p in module.parameters():
                        p.requires_grad = False
                    continue

            # Cast buffers to the original dtype & device
            W_idx = W_idx.to(device=device)
            W_cents = W_cents.to(device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
            Q_P_r_dev = Q_P_r.to(device=device, dtype=dtype)

            bias = base_mod.bias.detach() if getattr(base_mod, "bias", None) is not None else None

            new_linear = RotationalSliceGPTINT4Linear(
                W_indices=W_idx,
                W_centroids=W_cents,
                Q_P_r=Q_P_r_dev,
                bias=bias,
            )

            # Splice into model
            if hasattr(module, "base_layer"):
                # Preserve any LoRA params
                if hasattr(module, "lora_A") and module.lora_A is not None:
                    if not hasattr(new_linear, "lora_A"):
                        new_linear.lora_A = module.lora_A
                if hasattr(module, "lora_B") and module.lora_B is not None:
                    if not hasattr(new_linear, "lora_B"):
                        new_linear.lora_B = module.lora_B
                module.base_layer = new_linear
            else:
                replace_module(self.model, name, new_linear)

            # RS-FIX-09: honest byte accounting
            orig_bytes = raw_w.numel() * raw_w.element_size()
            if base_mod.bias is not None:
                orig_bytes += base_mod.bias.numel() * base_mod.bias.element_size()
            # comp: int8 indices + fp32 centroid table + Q_P_r + bias
            comp_bytes = W_idx.numel() * W_idx.element_size()
            comp_bytes += W_cents.numel() * W_cents.element_size()
            comp_bytes += Q_P_r_dev.numel() * Q_P_r_dev.element_size()
            if bias is not None:
                comp_bytes += bias.numel() * bias.element_size()

            self._orig_bytes += orig_bytes
            self._comp_bytes += comp_bytes
            compressed_layers_count += 1

        # Forward sanity check
        forward_check_success = True
        try:
            device = next(self.model.parameters()).device
            with torch.no_grad():
                cfg = getattr(self.model, "config", None)
                vocab = getattr(cfg, "vocab_size", None) or 100
                dummy = torch.randint(0, min(vocab, 100), (1, 8), device=device)
                self.model(dummy)
        except Exception as ex:
            logger.warning("RotationalSliceGPT forward sanity check: %s", ex)
            forward_check_success = False

        try:
            self._compressed_params = self._count_params(self.model)
        except Exception:
            self._compressed_params = sum(p.numel() for p in self.model.parameters())

        stats = self.get_compression_stats()
        avg_err = float(np.mean(list(self.layer_errors.values()))) if self.layer_errors else 0.0
        storage_ratio = self._orig_bytes / self._comp_bytes if self._comp_bytes > 0 else 1.0

        stats.update(
            {
                "compressed_layers": compressed_layers_count,
                "fallback_layers": fallback_layers,
                "r_ratio": self.r_ratio,
                "quant_bits": self.quant_bits,
                "lambda_reg": self.lambda_reg,
                "max_error": self.max_error,
                "fallback_to_original": self.fallback_to_original,
                "layer_errors": self.layer_errors,
                "layer_shapes": layer_shapes,
                "avg_reconstruction_error": avg_err,
                "target_attention_only": self.target_attention_only,
                "forward_check_success": forward_check_success,
                "orig_storage_bytes": self._orig_bytes,
                "comp_storage_bytes": self._comp_bytes,
                "storage_compression_ratio": round(storage_ratio, 3),
                "storage_reduction_pct": round(
                    100.0 * (1.0 - self._comp_bytes / max(1, self._orig_bytes)), 2
                ),
                "method": "rotational_slicegpt_int4",
            }
        )
        self.stats = stats
        logger.info(
            "RotationalSliceGPT: %d layers compressed, %d fallback, "
            "avg_err=%.4f, storage_ratio=%.2fx, bits=%d, r_ratio=%.2f",
            compressed_layers_count,
            len(fallback_layers),
            avg_err,
            storage_ratio,
            self.quant_bits,
            self.r_ratio,
        )
        return self.model, stats

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _collect_layer_activations(self) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
        """
        RS-FIX-07: store per-channel mean |x| instead of full activation
        tensors to avoid OOM on large models.
        """
        self.model.eval()
        activations: dict[str, list[torch.Tensor]] = {}
        hooks = []

        def make_hook(layer_name: str):
            def hook(module, inputs, output):
                if inputs and isinstance(inputs[0], torch.Tensor):
                    x = inputs[0].detach()
                    if x.dim() >= 2:
                        col_mean = x.abs().mean(dim=tuple(range(x.dim() - 1)))
                    else:
                        col_mean = x.abs()
                    activations.setdefault(layer_name, []).append(col_mean.cpu())

            return hook

        for name, module in self.model.named_modules():
            if self._is_target_module(name, module):
                target = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
                hooks.append(target.register_forward_hook(make_hook(name)))

        try:
            with torch.no_grad():
                calib_inputs = get_calibration_tensors(
                    self.model,
                    tokenizer=self.tokenizer,
                    calibration_loader=self.calibration_loader,
                    calibration_texts=self.calibration_texts,
                    device=self.target_device,
                )
                try:
                    if isinstance(calib_inputs, dict):
                        self.model(**calib_inputs)
                    elif isinstance(calib_inputs, torch.Tensor):
                        self.model(calib_inputs)
                    elif isinstance(calib_inputs, (list, tuple)):
                        self.model(*calib_inputs)
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug("Activation capture failed: %s", e)
        finally:
            for h in hooks:
                h.remove()

        aggregated: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
        for name, lst in activations.items():
            if lst:
                aggregated[name] = torch.stack([t.float() for t in lst], dim=0).mean(dim=0)
        return aggregated

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def compress_linear(
        self,
        linear_layer: nn.Linear,
        X: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    ) -> RotationalSliceGPTINT4Linear:
        """Convenience wrapper for compressing a single nn.Linear."""
        W = linear_layer.weight.data.float()
        device = W.device
        if X is None:
            X = torch.randn(W.shape[1], 128, device=device)
        elif X.dim() == 3:
            X = X.reshape(-1, W.shape[1]).T
        elif X.dim() == 2 and X.shape[0] != W.shape[1]:
            X = X.T
        W_idx, W_cents, Q_P_r, _ = self.compress_matrix(W, X)
        bias = linear_layer.bias.data if getattr(linear_layer, "bias", None) is not None else None
        return RotationalSliceGPTINT4Linear(
            W_indices=W_idx.to(device),
            W_centroids=W_cents.to(device),
            Q_P_r=Q_P_r.to(device=device, dtype=W.dtype),
            bias=bias,
        )

Rotational-SliceGPT-INT4 hybrid compression engine.

def compress(self, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
    # Dual-mode: .compress(W, X) → tuple, .compress() → (model, stats)
    if (
        len(args) >= 2
        and isinstance(args[0], torch.Tensor)
        and isinstance(args[1], torch.Tensor)
    ):
        return self.compress_matrix(args[0], args[1])

    activations_map = self._collect_layer_activations()
    compressed_layers_count = 0
    fallback_layers: list[str] = []
    self.layer_errors = {}
    layer_shapes: dict[str, tuple[int, int]] = {}
    self._orig_bytes = 0
    self._comp_bytes = 0

    for name, module in list(self.model.named_modules()):
        if not self._is_target_module(name, module):
            continue

        base_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        raw_w = base_mod.weight.data
        cls_name = base_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(base_mod, "nf")
        device = raw_w.device
        dtype = raw_w.dtype

        W = raw_w.t().contiguous().float() if is_conv1d else raw_w.clone().float()
        d_out, d_in = W.shape
        layer_shapes[name] = (d_out, d_in)

        # Build X for slicing (RS-FIX-07: per-channel mean)
        act = activations_map.get(name, None)
        if act is not None and act.numel() > 0:
            act_flat = act.reshape(-1, act.shape[-1]).float()
            if act_flat.shape[-1] == d_in:
                # Synthesize a small sample matrix from the mean vector.
                mean_act = act_flat.mean(dim=0, keepdim=True)  # (1, D_in)
                # Repeat with small jitter to give variance-based slicing signal.
                X = mean_act.T + 0.01 * torch.randn(d_in, 32, device=device)
            else:
                X = torch.randn(d_in, 32, device=device)
        else:
            X = torch.randn(d_in, 32, device=device)

        try:
            W_idx, W_cents, Q_P_r, rel_error = self.compress_matrix(W, X)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("RotationalSliceGPT failed on %s: %s", name, e)
            fallback_layers.append(name)
            continue

        self.layer_errors[name] = rel_error

        if rel_error > self.max_error:
            logger.warning(
                "RotationalSliceGPT reconstruction error %.4f on %s exceeded %.4f",
                rel_error,
                name,
                self.max_error,
            )
            if self.fallback_to_original:
                fallback_layers.append(name)
                for p in module.parameters():
                    p.requires_grad = False
                continue

        # Cast buffers to the original dtype & device
        W_idx = W_idx.to(device=device)
        W_cents = W_cents.to(device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
        Q_P_r_dev = Q_P_r.to(device=device, dtype=dtype)

        bias = base_mod.bias.detach() if getattr(base_mod, "bias", None) is not None else None

        new_linear = RotationalSliceGPTINT4Linear(
            W_indices=W_idx,
            W_centroids=W_cents,
            Q_P_r=Q_P_r_dev,
            bias=bias,
        )

        # Splice into model
        if hasattr(module, "base_layer"):
            # Preserve any LoRA params
            if hasattr(module, "lora_A") and module.lora_A is not None:
                if not hasattr(new_linear, "lora_A"):
                    new_linear.lora_A = module.lora_A
            if hasattr(module, "lora_B") and module.lora_B is not None:
                if not hasattr(new_linear, "lora_B"):
                    new_linear.lora_B = module.lora_B
            module.base_layer = new_linear
        else:
            replace_module(self.model, name, new_linear)

        # RS-FIX-09: honest byte accounting
        orig_bytes = raw_w.numel() * raw_w.element_size()
        if base_mod.bias is not None:
            orig_bytes += base_mod.bias.numel() * base_mod.bias.element_size()
        # comp: int8 indices + fp32 centroid table + Q_P_r + bias
        comp_bytes = W_idx.numel() * W_idx.element_size()
        comp_bytes += W_cents.numel() * W_cents.element_size()
        comp_bytes += Q_P_r_dev.numel() * Q_P_r_dev.element_size()
        if bias is not None:
            comp_bytes += bias.numel() * bias.element_size()

        self._orig_bytes += orig_bytes
        self._comp_bytes += comp_bytes
        compressed_layers_count += 1

    # Forward sanity check
    forward_check_success = True
    try:
        device = next(self.model.parameters()).device
        with torch.no_grad():
            cfg = getattr(self.model, "config", None)
            vocab = getattr(cfg, "vocab_size", None) or 100
            dummy = torch.randint(0, min(vocab, 100), (1, 8), device=device)
            self.model(dummy)
    except Exception as ex:
        logger.warning("RotationalSliceGPT forward sanity check: %s", ex)
        forward_check_success = False

    try:
        self._compressed_params = self._count_params(self.model)
    except Exception:
        self._compressed_params = sum(p.numel() for p in self.model.parameters())

    stats = self.get_compression_stats()
    avg_err = float(np.mean(list(self.layer_errors.values()))) if self.layer_errors else 0.0
    storage_ratio = self._orig_bytes / self._comp_bytes if self._comp_bytes > 0 else 1.0

    stats.update(
        {
            "compressed_layers": compressed_layers_count,
            "fallback_layers": fallback_layers,
            "r_ratio": self.r_ratio,
            "quant_bits": self.quant_bits,
            "lambda_reg": self.lambda_reg,
            "max_error": self.max_error,
            "fallback_to_original": self.fallback_to_original,
            "layer_errors": self.layer_errors,
            "layer_shapes": layer_shapes,
            "avg_reconstruction_error": avg_err,
            "target_attention_only": self.target_attention_only,
            "forward_check_success": forward_check_success,
            "orig_storage_bytes": self._orig_bytes,
            "comp_storage_bytes": self._comp_bytes,
            "storage_compression_ratio": round(storage_ratio, 3),
            "storage_reduction_pct": round(
                100.0 * (1.0 - self._comp_bytes / max(1, self._orig_bytes)), 2
            ),
            "method": "rotational_slicegpt_int4",
        }
    )
    self.stats = stats
    logger.info(
        "RotationalSliceGPT: %d layers compressed, %d fallback, "
        "avg_err=%.4f, storage_ratio=%.2fx, bits=%d, r_ratio=%.2f",
        compressed_layers_count,
        len(fallback_layers),
        avg_err,
        storage_ratio,
        self.quant_bits,
        self.r_ratio,
    )
    return self.model, stats

Return (compressed_model, statistics).

def compress_linear(self, linear_layer: nn.Linear, X: torch.Tensor | None = None) ‑> RotationalSliceGPTINT4Linear
Expand source code 
def compress_linear(
    self,
    linear_layer: nn.Linear,
    X: torch.Tensor | None = None,
) -> RotationalSliceGPTINT4Linear:
    """Convenience wrapper for compressing a single nn.Linear."""
    W = linear_layer.weight.data.float()
    device = W.device
    if X is None:
        X = torch.randn(W.shape[1], 128, device=device)
    elif X.dim() == 3:
        X = X.reshape(-1, W.shape[1]).T
    elif X.dim() == 2 and X.shape[0] != W.shape[1]:
        X = X.T
    W_idx, W_cents, Q_P_r, _ = self.compress_matrix(W, X)
    bias = linear_layer.bias.data if getattr(linear_layer, "bias", None) is not None else None
    return RotationalSliceGPTINT4Linear(
        W_indices=W_idx.to(device),
        W_centroids=W_cents.to(device),
        Q_P_r=Q_P_r.to(device=device, dtype=W.dtype),
        bias=bias,
    )

def compress_matrix(self, W: torch.Tensor, X: torch.Tensor) ‑> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, float, torch.Tensor]
Expand source code 
def compress_matrix(
    self,
    W: torch.Tensor,
    X: torch.Tensor,
) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, float, torch.Tensor]:
    """
    Returns:
        W_quant_indices:  (D_out, r) int8
        W_quant_centroids:(C,)       fp32
        Q_P_r:            (D_in, r)  fp32
        rel_error:        float
    """
    D_out, D_in = W.shape
    r = max(1, min(int(D_in * self.r_ratio), D_in))
    device = W.device

    # 1. Orthogonal rotation
    Q = self.get_orthogonal_matrix(D_in, device)
    W_fp32 = W.detach().float()
    X_fp32 = X.detach().float().to(device)

    W_tilde = W_fp32 @ Q  # (D_out, D_in)
    X_tilde = Q.T @ X_fp32  # (D_in, N)

    # 2. Variance-based slicing (RS-FIX-06: vectorized)
    if X_tilde.shape[1] > 1:
        channel_variances = X_tilde.var(dim=1)  # (D_in,)
    else:
        channel_variances = torch.ones(D_in, device=device)
    top_r_indices = torch.argsort(channel_variances, descending=True)[:r]

    # P_r as a (D_in, r) selector matrix via scatter
    P_r = torch.zeros(D_in, r, device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
    P_r[top_r_indices, torch.arange(r, device=device)] = 1.0

    W_sliced = W_tilde @ P_r  # (D_out, r)
    X_sliced = P_r.T @ X_tilde  # (r, N)

    # 3. Second-order Hessian-based INT4 quantization
    H = 2.0 * (X_sliced @ X_sliced.T) / max(X_sliced.shape[1], 1) + self.lambda_reg * torch.eye(
        r, device=device
    )
    try:
        H_inv = torch.linalg.inv(H)
    except Exception:
        H_inv = torch.linalg.pinv(H)
    H_inv = torch.nan_to_num(H_inv, nan=0.0)

    # RS-FIX-10: quantize the entire column of residuals at once.
    W_quant = W_sliced.clone()
    # Full-matrix OBS: quantize each column then propagate the error.
    for j in range(r):
        w_j = W_quant[:, j].clone()
        idx_j, cents_j = self.quantizer.quantize(w_j)
        q_j = AstroQuantizer.dequantize(idx_j, cents_j).to(w_j.dtype)
        e_j = w_j - q_j
        W_quant[:, j] = q_j

        diag_inv = float(H_inv[j, j].item())
        if not math.isfinite(diag_inv) or diag_inv <= 1e-8:
            diag_inv = 1e-5

        if j < r - 1:
            h_slice = torch.nan_to_num(H_inv[j, j + 1 : r] / diag_inv, nan=0.0)
            W_quant[:, j + 1 : r] -= torch.outer(e_j, h_slice)

    # Re-quantize the final W_quant column-wise to get indices + centroids
    # (OBS updates can change values slightly, so we requantize once).
    W_quant.t().contiguous()  # (r, D_out) → quantize per row
    _W_idx_list, _W_cents_list = [], []
    per_col_centroids = []
    W_indices = torch.empty_like(W_quant, dtype=torch.int8)
    for j in range(r):
        idx_j, cents_j = self.quantizer.quantize(W_quant[:, j])
        W_indices[:, j] = idx_j
        per_col_centroids.append(cents_j)
    # Use a single shared centroid table = average of per-column tables.
    W_centroids = torch.stack(per_col_centroids, dim=0).mean(dim=0)

    # Reconstruct and measure error (using the shared table for honesty).
    W_deq = AstroQuantizer.dequantize(W_indices, W_centroids).to(W_quant.dtype)
    W_recon = (W_deq @ P_r.T) @ Q.T  # (D_out, D_in)
    norm_orig = W_fp32.norm(p="fro").clamp_min(1e-8)
    rel_error = float((W_fp32 - W_recon).norm(p="fro") / norm_orig)

    Q_P_r = Q @ P_r  # (D_in, r) — RS-FIX-08
    return W_indices, W_centroids, Q_P_r, rel_error

def compress_matrix(
    self,
    W: torch.Tensor,
    X: torch.Tensor,
) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, float, torch.Tensor]:
    """
    Returns:
        W_quant_indices:  (D_out, r) int8
        W_quant_centroids:(C,)       fp32
        Q_P_r:            (D_in, r)  fp32
        rel_error:        float
    """
    D_out, D_in = W.shape
    r = max(1, min(int(D_in * self.r_ratio), D_in))
    device = W.device

    # 1. Orthogonal rotation
    Q = self.get_orthogonal_matrix(D_in, device)
    W_fp32 = W.detach().float()
    X_fp32 = X.detach().float().to(device)

    W_tilde = W_fp32 @ Q  # (D_out, D_in)
    X_tilde = Q.T @ X_fp32  # (D_in, N)

    # 2. Variance-based slicing (RS-FIX-06: vectorized)
    if X_tilde.shape[1] > 1:
        channel_variances = X_tilde.var(dim=1)  # (D_in,)
    else:
        channel_variances = torch.ones(D_in, device=device)
    top_r_indices = torch.argsort(channel_variances, descending=True)[:r]

    # P_r as a (D_in, r) selector matrix via scatter
    P_r = torch.zeros(D_in, r, device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
    P_r[top_r_indices, torch.arange(r, device=device)] = 1.0

    W_sliced = W_tilde @ P_r  # (D_out, r)
    X_sliced = P_r.T @ X_tilde  # (r, N)

    # 3. Second-order Hessian-based INT4 quantization
    H = 2.0 * (X_sliced @ X_sliced.T) / max(X_sliced.shape[1], 1) + self.lambda_reg * torch.eye(
        r, device=device
    )
    try:
        H_inv = torch.linalg.inv(H)
    except Exception:
        H_inv = torch.linalg.pinv(H)
    H_inv = torch.nan_to_num(H_inv, nan=0.0)

    # RS-FIX-10: quantize the entire column of residuals at once.
    W_quant = W_sliced.clone()
    # Full-matrix OBS: quantize each column then propagate the error.
    for j in range(r):
        w_j = W_quant[:, j].clone()
        idx_j, cents_j = self.quantizer.quantize(w_j)
        q_j = AstroQuantizer.dequantize(idx_j, cents_j).to(w_j.dtype)
        e_j = w_j - q_j
        W_quant[:, j] = q_j

        diag_inv = float(H_inv[j, j].item())
        if not math.isfinite(diag_inv) or diag_inv <= 1e-8:
            diag_inv = 1e-5

        if j < r - 1:
            h_slice = torch.nan_to_num(H_inv[j, j + 1 : r] / diag_inv, nan=0.0)
            W_quant[:, j + 1 : r] -= torch.outer(e_j, h_slice)

    # Re-quantize the final W_quant column-wise to get indices + centroids
    # (OBS updates can change values slightly, so we requantize once).
    W_quant.t().contiguous()  # (r, D_out) → quantize per row
    _W_idx_list, _W_cents_list = [], []
    per_col_centroids = []
    W_indices = torch.empty_like(W_quant, dtype=torch.int8)
    for j in range(r):
        idx_j, cents_j = self.quantizer.quantize(W_quant[:, j])
        W_indices[:, j] = idx_j
        per_col_centroids.append(cents_j)
    # Use a single shared centroid table = average of per-column tables.
    W_centroids = torch.stack(per_col_centroids, dim=0).mean(dim=0)

    # Reconstruct and measure error (using the shared table for honesty).
    W_deq = AstroQuantizer.dequantize(W_indices, W_centroids).to(W_quant.dtype)
    W_recon = (W_deq @ P_r.T) @ Q.T  # (D_out, D_in)
    norm_orig = W_fp32.norm(p="fro").clamp_min(1e-8)
    rel_error = float((W_fp32 - W_recon).norm(p="fro") / norm_orig)

    Q_P_r = Q @ P_r  # (D_in, r) — RS-FIX-08
    return W_indices, W_centroids, Q_P_r, rel_error

def get_orthogonal_matrix(self, d_in: int, device: torch.device) ‑> torch.Tensor
Expand source code 
def get_orthogonal_matrix(self, d_in: int, device: torch.device) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Deterministic QR-based orthogonal Q (RS-FIX-05)."""
    gen = torch.Generator(device=device).manual_seed(self.seed)
    H = torch.randn(d_in, d_in, device=device, dtype=torch.float32, generator=gen)
    Q, _ = torch.linalg.qr(H)
    return Q

@COMPRESSOR_REGISTRY.register("rotational_slicegpt_int4")
class RotationalSliceGPTINT4Compressor(BaseCompressor):
    """
    Rotational-SliceGPT-INT4 hybrid compression engine.
    """

    # RS-FIX-04: not an attention module.
    is_custom_attention: bool = False

    def __init__(
        self,
        model: nn.Module | None = None,
        r_ratio: float = 0.4,  # RS-FIX-03: lower default
        lambda_reg: float = 1e-2,
        slice_ratio: float | None = None,
        quant_bits: int = 4,
        max_error: float = 0.20,
        fallback_to_original: bool = True,
        tokenizer: Any | None = None,
        calibration_loader: Any | None = None,
        calibration_texts: list[str] | None = None,
        device: str | torch.device | None = None,
        target_modules: list[str] | None = None,
        target_attention_only: bool = False,
        seed: int = 42,  # RS-FIX-05
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        # Remove duplicates that arrive through kwargs/comp_config.
        for k in (
            "max_error",
            "fallback_to_original",
            "r_ratio",
            "quant_bits",
            "lambda_reg",
        ):
            kwargs.pop(k, None)
        target_model = model if model is not None else nn.Identity()
        super().__init__(target_model, name="rotational_slicegpt_int4", **kwargs)

        cfg = kwargs.get("comp_config", kwargs.get("cfg"))
        r_ratio = _cfg_get(cfg, "rotational_slicegpt_r_ratio", _cfg_get(cfg, "r_ratio", r_ratio))
        lambda_reg = _cfg_get(cfg, "lambda_reg", lambda_reg)
        quant_bits = _cfg_get(cfg, "quant_bits", quant_bits)
        max_error = _cfg_get(cfg, "max_error", max_error)
        fallback_to_original = _cfg_get(cfg, "fallback_to_original", fallback_to_original)
        target_attention_only = bool(
            target_attention_only or _cfg_get(cfg, "target_attention_only", False)
        )

        self.r_ratio = float(slice_ratio if slice_ratio is not None else r_ratio)
        if self.r_ratio >= 0.5:
            logger.warning(
                "RotationalSliceGPT: r_ratio=%.2f ≥ 0.5 will INCREASE parameter count. "
                "Consider r_ratio ≤ 0.4 for real savings.",
                self.r_ratio,
            )
        self.lambda_reg = float(lambda_reg)
        self.quant_bits = int(quant_bits)
        self.quantizer = AstroQuantizer(bits=self.quant_bits)
        self.max_error = float(max_error)
        self.fallback_to_original = bool(fallback_to_original)
        self.target_modules = target_modules
        self.target_attention_only = target_attention_only
        self.seed = int(seed)

        self.tokenizer = tokenizer or getattr(model, "tokenizer", None)
        self.calibration_loader = calibration_loader
        self.calibration_texts = calibration_texts
        self.target_device = (
            torch.device(device)
            if device is not None
            else (
                next(target_model.parameters(), torch.tensor(0.0)).device
                if model is not None
                else torch.device("cpu")
            )
        )
        self.layer_errors: dict[str, float] = {}
        self.stats: dict[str, Any] = {}
        self._orig_bytes = 0
        self._comp_bytes = 0

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _is_target_module(self, name: str, module: nn.Module) -> bool:
        name_l = name.lower()
        if any(
            k in name_l
            for k in (
                "classifier",
                "score",
                "lm_head",
                "pooler",
                "embedding",
                "layernorm",
                "rmsnorm",
                "batchnorm",
            )
        ):
            return False

        if isinstance(
            module,
            (
                nn.LayerNorm,
                nn.RMSNorm,
                nn.Embedding,
                nn.BatchNorm1d,
                nn.BatchNorm2d,
                RotationalSliceGPTINT4Linear,
            ),
        ):
            return False

        if self.target_attention_only:
            attn_kw = (
                "attn",
                "attention",
                "q_proj",
                "k_proj",
                "v_proj",
                "o_proj",
                "c_attn",
                "c_proj",
                "query",
                "key",
                "value",
            )
            if not any(k in name_l for k in attn_kw):
                return False

        base_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
        cls_name = base_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
        is_linear_like = (
            isinstance(base_mod, nn.Linear) or "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(base_mod, "nf")
        )
        if not is_linear_like:
            return False

        try:
            if not should_compress_linear(self.model, name):
                return False
        except Exception:
            pass

        if self.target_modules is None:
            return True
        return any(t in name for t in self.target_modules)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def get_orthogonal_matrix(self, d_in: int, device: torch.device) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Deterministic QR-based orthogonal Q (RS-FIX-05)."""
        gen = torch.Generator(device=device).manual_seed(self.seed)
        H = torch.randn(d_in, d_in, device=device, dtype=torch.float32, generator=gen)
        Q, _ = torch.linalg.qr(H)
        return Q

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def compress_matrix(
        self,
        W: torch.Tensor,
        X: torch.Tensor,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, float, torch.Tensor]:
        """
        Returns:
            W_quant_indices:  (D_out, r) int8
            W_quant_centroids:(C,)       fp32
            Q_P_r:            (D_in, r)  fp32
            rel_error:        float
        """
        D_out, D_in = W.shape
        r = max(1, min(int(D_in * self.r_ratio), D_in))
        device = W.device

        # 1. Orthogonal rotation
        Q = self.get_orthogonal_matrix(D_in, device)
        W_fp32 = W.detach().float()
        X_fp32 = X.detach().float().to(device)

        W_tilde = W_fp32 @ Q  # (D_out, D_in)
        X_tilde = Q.T @ X_fp32  # (D_in, N)

        # 2. Variance-based slicing (RS-FIX-06: vectorized)
        if X_tilde.shape[1] > 1:
            channel_variances = X_tilde.var(dim=1)  # (D_in,)
        else:
            channel_variances = torch.ones(D_in, device=device)
        top_r_indices = torch.argsort(channel_variances, descending=True)[:r]

        # P_r as a (D_in, r) selector matrix via scatter
        P_r = torch.zeros(D_in, r, device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
        P_r[top_r_indices, torch.arange(r, device=device)] = 1.0

        W_sliced = W_tilde @ P_r  # (D_out, r)
        X_sliced = P_r.T @ X_tilde  # (r, N)

        # 3. Second-order Hessian-based INT4 quantization
        H = 2.0 * (X_sliced @ X_sliced.T) / max(X_sliced.shape[1], 1) + self.lambda_reg * torch.eye(
            r, device=device
        )
        try:
            H_inv = torch.linalg.inv(H)
        except Exception:
            H_inv = torch.linalg.pinv(H)
        H_inv = torch.nan_to_num(H_inv, nan=0.0)

        # RS-FIX-10: quantize the entire column of residuals at once.
        W_quant = W_sliced.clone()
        # Full-matrix OBS: quantize each column then propagate the error.
        for j in range(r):
            w_j = W_quant[:, j].clone()
            idx_j, cents_j = self.quantizer.quantize(w_j)
            q_j = AstroQuantizer.dequantize(idx_j, cents_j).to(w_j.dtype)
            e_j = w_j - q_j
            W_quant[:, j] = q_j

            diag_inv = float(H_inv[j, j].item())
            if not math.isfinite(diag_inv) or diag_inv <= 1e-8:
                diag_inv = 1e-5

            if j < r - 1:
                h_slice = torch.nan_to_num(H_inv[j, j + 1 : r] / diag_inv, nan=0.0)
                W_quant[:, j + 1 : r] -= torch.outer(e_j, h_slice)

        # Re-quantize the final W_quant column-wise to get indices + centroids
        # (OBS updates can change values slightly, so we requantize once).
        W_quant.t().contiguous()  # (r, D_out) → quantize per row
        _W_idx_list, _W_cents_list = [], []
        per_col_centroids = []
        W_indices = torch.empty_like(W_quant, dtype=torch.int8)
        for j in range(r):
            idx_j, cents_j = self.quantizer.quantize(W_quant[:, j])
            W_indices[:, j] = idx_j
            per_col_centroids.append(cents_j)
        # Use a single shared centroid table = average of per-column tables.
        W_centroids = torch.stack(per_col_centroids, dim=0).mean(dim=0)

        # Reconstruct and measure error (using the shared table for honesty).
        W_deq = AstroQuantizer.dequantize(W_indices, W_centroids).to(W_quant.dtype)
        W_recon = (W_deq @ P_r.T) @ Q.T  # (D_out, D_in)
        norm_orig = W_fp32.norm(p="fro").clamp_min(1e-8)
        rel_error = float((W_fp32 - W_recon).norm(p="fro") / norm_orig)

        Q_P_r = Q @ P_r  # (D_in, r) — RS-FIX-08
        return W_indices, W_centroids, Q_P_r, rel_error

    compress_tensor = compress_matrix  # backward-compat alias

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def compress(self, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
        # Dual-mode: .compress(W, X) → tuple, .compress() → (model, stats)
        if (
            len(args) >= 2
            and isinstance(args[0], torch.Tensor)
            and isinstance(args[1], torch.Tensor)
        ):
            return self.compress_matrix(args[0], args[1])

        activations_map = self._collect_layer_activations()
        compressed_layers_count = 0
        fallback_layers: list[str] = []
        self.layer_errors = {}
        layer_shapes: dict[str, tuple[int, int]] = {}
        self._orig_bytes = 0
        self._comp_bytes = 0

        for name, module in list(self.model.named_modules()):
            if not self._is_target_module(name, module):
                continue

            base_mod = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
            raw_w = base_mod.weight.data
            cls_name = base_mod.__class__.__name__.lower()
            is_conv1d = "conv1d" in cls_name or hasattr(base_mod, "nf")
            device = raw_w.device
            dtype = raw_w.dtype

            W = raw_w.t().contiguous().float() if is_conv1d else raw_w.clone().float()
            d_out, d_in = W.shape
            layer_shapes[name] = (d_out, d_in)

            # Build X for slicing (RS-FIX-07: per-channel mean)
            act = activations_map.get(name, None)
            if act is not None and act.numel() > 0:
                act_flat = act.reshape(-1, act.shape[-1]).float()
                if act_flat.shape[-1] == d_in:
                    # Synthesize a small sample matrix from the mean vector.
                    mean_act = act_flat.mean(dim=0, keepdim=True)  # (1, D_in)
                    # Repeat with small jitter to give variance-based slicing signal.
                    X = mean_act.T + 0.01 * torch.randn(d_in, 32, device=device)
                else:
                    X = torch.randn(d_in, 32, device=device)
            else:
                X = torch.randn(d_in, 32, device=device)

            try:
                W_idx, W_cents, Q_P_r, rel_error = self.compress_matrix(W, X)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning("RotationalSliceGPT failed on %s: %s", name, e)
                fallback_layers.append(name)
                continue

            self.layer_errors[name] = rel_error

            if rel_error > self.max_error:
                logger.warning(
                    "RotationalSliceGPT reconstruction error %.4f on %s exceeded %.4f",
                    rel_error,
                    name,
                    self.max_error,
                )
                if self.fallback_to_original:
                    fallback_layers.append(name)
                    for p in module.parameters():
                        p.requires_grad = False
                    continue

            # Cast buffers to the original dtype & device
            W_idx = W_idx.to(device=device)
            W_cents = W_cents.to(device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
            Q_P_r_dev = Q_P_r.to(device=device, dtype=dtype)

            bias = base_mod.bias.detach() if getattr(base_mod, "bias", None) is not None else None

            new_linear = RotationalSliceGPTINT4Linear(
                W_indices=W_idx,
                W_centroids=W_cents,
                Q_P_r=Q_P_r_dev,
                bias=bias,
            )

            # Splice into model
            if hasattr(module, "base_layer"):
                # Preserve any LoRA params
                if hasattr(module, "lora_A") and module.lora_A is not None:
                    if not hasattr(new_linear, "lora_A"):
                        new_linear.lora_A = module.lora_A
                if hasattr(module, "lora_B") and module.lora_B is not None:
                    if not hasattr(new_linear, "lora_B"):
                        new_linear.lora_B = module.lora_B
                module.base_layer = new_linear
            else:
                replace_module(self.model, name, new_linear)

            # RS-FIX-09: honest byte accounting
            orig_bytes = raw_w.numel() * raw_w.element_size()
            if base_mod.bias is not None:
                orig_bytes += base_mod.bias.numel() * base_mod.bias.element_size()
            # comp: int8 indices + fp32 centroid table + Q_P_r + bias
            comp_bytes = W_idx.numel() * W_idx.element_size()
            comp_bytes += W_cents.numel() * W_cents.element_size()
            comp_bytes += Q_P_r_dev.numel() * Q_P_r_dev.element_size()
            if bias is not None:
                comp_bytes += bias.numel() * bias.element_size()

            self._orig_bytes += orig_bytes
            self._comp_bytes += comp_bytes
            compressed_layers_count += 1

        # Forward sanity check
        forward_check_success = True
        try:
            device = next(self.model.parameters()).device
            with torch.no_grad():
                cfg = getattr(self.model, "config", None)
                vocab = getattr(cfg, "vocab_size", None) or 100
                dummy = torch.randint(0, min(vocab, 100), (1, 8), device=device)
                self.model(dummy)
        except Exception as ex:
            logger.warning("RotationalSliceGPT forward sanity check: %s", ex)
            forward_check_success = False

        try:
            self._compressed_params = self._count_params(self.model)
        except Exception:
            self._compressed_params = sum(p.numel() for p in self.model.parameters())

        stats = self.get_compression_stats()
        avg_err = float(np.mean(list(self.layer_errors.values()))) if self.layer_errors else 0.0
        storage_ratio = self._orig_bytes / self._comp_bytes if self._comp_bytes > 0 else 1.0

        stats.update(
            {
                "compressed_layers": compressed_layers_count,
                "fallback_layers": fallback_layers,
                "r_ratio": self.r_ratio,
                "quant_bits": self.quant_bits,
                "lambda_reg": self.lambda_reg,
                "max_error": self.max_error,
                "fallback_to_original": self.fallback_to_original,
                "layer_errors": self.layer_errors,
                "layer_shapes": layer_shapes,
                "avg_reconstruction_error": avg_err,
                "target_attention_only": self.target_attention_only,
                "forward_check_success": forward_check_success,
                "orig_storage_bytes": self._orig_bytes,
                "comp_storage_bytes": self._comp_bytes,
                "storage_compression_ratio": round(storage_ratio, 3),
                "storage_reduction_pct": round(
                    100.0 * (1.0 - self._comp_bytes / max(1, self._orig_bytes)), 2
                ),
                "method": "rotational_slicegpt_int4",
            }
        )
        self.stats = stats
        logger.info(
            "RotationalSliceGPT: %d layers compressed, %d fallback, "
            "avg_err=%.4f, storage_ratio=%.2fx, bits=%d, r_ratio=%.2f",
            compressed_layers_count,
            len(fallback_layers),
            avg_err,
            storage_ratio,
            self.quant_bits,
            self.r_ratio,
        )
        return self.model, stats

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _collect_layer_activations(self) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
        """
        RS-FIX-07: store per-channel mean |x| instead of full activation
        tensors to avoid OOM on large models.
        """
        self.model.eval()
        activations: dict[str, list[torch.Tensor]] = {}
        hooks = []

        def make_hook(layer_name: str):
            def hook(module, inputs, output):
                if inputs and isinstance(inputs[0], torch.Tensor):
                    x = inputs[0].detach()
                    if x.dim() >= 2:
                        col_mean = x.abs().mean(dim=tuple(range(x.dim() - 1)))
                    else:
                        col_mean = x.abs()
                    activations.setdefault(layer_name, []).append(col_mean.cpu())

            return hook

        for name, module in self.model.named_modules():
            if self._is_target_module(name, module):
                target = getattr(module, "base_layer", module)
                hooks.append(target.register_forward_hook(make_hook(name)))

        try:
            with torch.no_grad():
                calib_inputs = get_calibration_tensors(
                    self.model,
                    tokenizer=self.tokenizer,
                    calibration_loader=self.calibration_loader,
                    calibration_texts=self.calibration_texts,
                    device=self.target_device,
                )
                try:
                    if isinstance(calib_inputs, dict):
                        self.model(**calib_inputs)
                    elif isinstance(calib_inputs, torch.Tensor):
                        self.model(calib_inputs)
                    elif isinstance(calib_inputs, (list, tuple)):
                        self.model(*calib_inputs)
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug("Activation capture failed: %s", e)
        finally:
            for h in hooks:
                h.remove()

        aggregated: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
        for name, lst in activations.items():
            if lst:
                aggregated[name] = torch.stack([t.float() for t in lst], dim=0).mean(dim=0)
        return aggregated

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def compress_linear(
        self,
        linear_layer: nn.Linear,
        X: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    ) -> RotationalSliceGPTINT4Linear:
        """Convenience wrapper for compressing a single nn.Linear."""
        W = linear_layer.weight.data.float()
        device = W.device
        if X is None:
            X = torch.randn(W.shape[1], 128, device=device)
        elif X.dim() == 3:
            X = X.reshape(-1, W.shape[1]).T
        elif X.dim() == 2 and X.shape[0] != W.shape[1]:
            X = X.T
        W_idx, W_cents, Q_P_r, _ = self.compress_matrix(W, X)
        bias = linear_layer.bias.data if getattr(linear_layer, "bias", None) is not None else None
        return RotationalSliceGPTINT4Linear(
            W_indices=W_idx.to(device),
            W_centroids=W_cents.to(device),
            Q_P_r=Q_P_r.to(device=device, dtype=W.dtype),
            bias=bias,
        )

def compress_matrix(self, W: torch.Tensor, X: torch.Tensor) ‑> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, float, torch.Tensor]
Expand source code 
def compress_matrix(
    self,
    W: torch.Tensor,
    X: torch.Tensor,
) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, float, torch.Tensor]:
    """
    Returns:
        W_quant_indices:  (D_out, r) int8
        W_quant_centroids:(C,)       fp32
        Q_P_r:            (D_in, r)  fp32
        rel_error:        float
    """
    D_out, D_in = W.shape
    r = max(1, min(int(D_in * self.r_ratio), D_in))
    device = W.device

    # 1. Orthogonal rotation
    Q = self.get_orthogonal_matrix(D_in, device)
    W_fp32 = W.detach().float()
    X_fp32 = X.detach().float().to(device)

    W_tilde = W_fp32 @ Q  # (D_out, D_in)
    X_tilde = Q.T @ X_fp32  # (D_in, N)

    # 2. Variance-based slicing (RS-FIX-06: vectorized)
    if X_tilde.shape[1] > 1:
        channel_variances = X_tilde.var(dim=1)  # (D_in,)
    else:
        channel_variances = torch.ones(D_in, device=device)
    top_r_indices = torch.argsort(channel_variances, descending=True)[:r]

    # P_r as a (D_in, r) selector matrix via scatter
    P_r = torch.zeros(D_in, r, device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
    P_r[top_r_indices, torch.arange(r, device=device)] = 1.0

    W_sliced = W_tilde @ P_r  # (D_out, r)
    X_sliced = P_r.T @ X_tilde  # (r, N)

    # 3. Second-order Hessian-based INT4 quantization
    H = 2.0 * (X_sliced @ X_sliced.T) / max(X_sliced.shape[1], 1) + self.lambda_reg * torch.eye(
        r, device=device
    )
    try:
        H_inv = torch.linalg.inv(H)
    except Exception:
        H_inv = torch.linalg.pinv(H)
    H_inv = torch.nan_to_num(H_inv, nan=0.0)

    # RS-FIX-10: quantize the entire column of residuals at once.
    W_quant = W_sliced.clone()
    # Full-matrix OBS: quantize each column then propagate the error.
    for j in range(r):
        w_j = W_quant[:, j].clone()
        idx_j, cents_j = self.quantizer.quantize(w_j)
        q_j = AstroQuantizer.dequantize(idx_j, cents_j).to(w_j.dtype)
        e_j = w_j - q_j
        W_quant[:, j] = q_j

        diag_inv = float(H_inv[j, j].item())
        if not math.isfinite(diag_inv) or diag_inv <= 1e-8:
            diag_inv = 1e-5

        if j < r - 1:
            h_slice = torch.nan_to_num(H_inv[j, j + 1 : r] / diag_inv, nan=0.0)
            W_quant[:, j + 1 : r] -= torch.outer(e_j, h_slice)

    # Re-quantize the final W_quant column-wise to get indices + centroids
    # (OBS updates can change values slightly, so we requantize once).
    W_quant.t().contiguous()  # (r, D_out) → quantize per row
    _W_idx_list, _W_cents_list = [], []
    per_col_centroids = []
    W_indices = torch.empty_like(W_quant, dtype=torch.int8)
    for j in range(r):
        idx_j, cents_j = self.quantizer.quantize(W_quant[:, j])
        W_indices[:, j] = idx_j
        per_col_centroids.append(cents_j)
    # Use a single shared centroid table = average of per-column tables.
    W_centroids = torch.stack(per_col_centroids, dim=0).mean(dim=0)

    # Reconstruct and measure error (using the shared table for honesty).
    W_deq = AstroQuantizer.dequantize(W_indices, W_centroids).to(W_quant.dtype)
    W_recon = (W_deq @ P_r.T) @ Q.T  # (D_out, D_in)
    norm_orig = W_fp32.norm(p="fro").clamp_min(1e-8)
    rel_error = float((W_fp32 - W_recon).norm(p="fro") / norm_orig)

    Q_P_r = Q @ P_r  # (D_in, r) — RS-FIX-08
    return W_indices, W_centroids, Q_P_r, rel_error

def compress_matrix(
    self,
    W: torch.Tensor,
    X: torch.Tensor,
) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, float, torch.Tensor]:
    """
    Returns:
        W_quant_indices:  (D_out, r) int8
        W_quant_centroids:(C,)       fp32
        Q_P_r:            (D_in, r)  fp32
        rel_error:        float

    X_sliced = P_r.T @ X_tilde  # (r, N)

    # 3. Second-order Hessian-based INT4 quantization
    H = 2.0 * (X_sliced @ X_sliced.T) / max(X_sliced.shape[1], 1) + self.lambda_reg * torch.eye(

    # Re-quantize the final W_quant column-wise to get indices + centroids
    # (OBS updates can change values slightly, so we requantize once).
    W_quant.t().contiguous()  # (r, D_out) → quantize per row
    _W_idx_list, _W_cents_list = [], []
    per_col_centroids = []
    W_indices = torch.empty_like(W_quant, dtype=torch.int8)
    for j in range(r):
        idx_j, cents_j = self.quantizer.quantize(W_quant[:, j])
        W_indices[:, j] = idx_j
        per_col_centroids.append(cents_j)
    # Use a single shared centroid table = average of per-column tables.
    W_centroids = torch.stack(per_col_centroids, dim=0).mean(dim=0)

    # Reconstruct and measure error (using the shared table for honesty).
    W_deq = AstroQuantizer.dequantize(W_indices, W_centroids).to(W_quant.dtype)
    W_recon = (W_deq @ P_r.T) @ Q.T  # (D_out, D_in)
    norm_orig = W_fp32.norm(p="fro").clamp_min(1e-8)
    rel_error = float((W_fp32 - W_recon).norm(p="fro") / norm_orig)

    Q_P_r = Q @ P_r  # (D_in, r) — RS-FIX-08
    return W_indices, W_centroids, Q_P_r, rel_error

def get_orthogonal_matrix(self, d_in: int, device: torch.device) ‑> torch.Tensor
Expand source code 
def get_orthogonal_matrix(self, d_in: int, device: torch.device) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Deterministic QR-based orthogonal Q (RS-FIX-05)."""
    gen = torch.Generator(device=device).manual_seed(self.seed)
    H = torch.randn(d_in, d_in, device=device, dtype=torch.float32, generator=gen)
    Q, _ = torch.linalg.qr(H)
    return Q

Deterministic QR-based orthogonal Q (RS-FIX-05).

class RotationalSliceGPTINT4Linear (W_indices: torch.Tensor,

Q_P_r: torch.Tensor,
bias: torch.Tensor | None = None)
Expand source code 
class RotationalSliceGPTINT4Linear(nn.Module):
    """
    Linear layer storing:
      - Q_P_r:        (D_in, r) fp16/32 — the projection matrix
      - W_indices:    (D_out, r) int8   — quantized weight in sliced space
      - W_centroids:  (C,)       fp32   — per-tensor centroid table
      - bias:         (D_out,)   or None

    Forward: y = (x @ Q_P_r) → dequantize(W_indices) → @ .T → + bias
    """

    is_custom_attention: bool = False

    def __init__(
        self,
        W_indices: torch.Tensor,  # (D_out, r) int8
        W_centroids: torch.Tensor,  # (C,) fp32
        Q_P_r: torch.Tensor,  # (D_in, r) float
        bias: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.register_buffer("Q_P_r", Q_P_r)
        self.register_buffer("W_indices", W_indices)
        self.register_buffer("W_centroids", W_centroids)

        if bias is not None:
            self.bias = nn.Parameter(bias.clone(), requires_grad=False)
        else:
            self.register_parameter("bias", None)

    def _dequantize_W(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        return AstroQuantizer.dequantize(self.W_indices, self.W_centroids).to(self.Q_P_r.dtype)

    @property
    def weight(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Effective weight matrix (out_features, in_features)."""
        W_q = self._dequantize_W()  # (D_out, r)
        return torch.matmul(W_q, self.Q_P_r.T)  # (D_out, D_in)

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        # x: (..., D_in)
        x_proj = torch.matmul(x, self.Q_P_r)  # (..., r)
        W_q = self._dequantize_W()  # (D_out, r)
        y = torch.matmul(x_proj, W_q.T)  # (..., D_out)
        if self.bias is not None:
            y = y + self.bias
        return y

Linear layer storing: - Q_P_r: (D_in, r) fp16/32 — the projection matrix - W_indices: (D_out, r) int8 — quantized weight in sliced space - W_centroids: (C,) fp32 — per-tensor centroid table - bias: (D_out,) or None

Forward: y = (x @ Q_P_r) → dequantize(W_indices) → @ .T → + bias

Although the recipe for forward pass needs to be defined within this function, one should call the :class:Module instance afterwards instead of this since the former takes care of running the registered hooks while the latter silently ignores them.

class STEFakeQuantize (*args, **kwargs)
Expand source code 
class STEFakeQuantize(torch.autograd.Function):
    """
    Straight-Through Estimator (STE) fake-quantization autograd function.
    Clamps inputs to [qmin, qmax] in discrete integer space, de-quantizes back,
    and passes gradients through uninhibited across all channels.
    """

    @staticmethod
    def forward(
        ctx: Any,
        x: torch.Tensor,
        scale: torch.Tensor,
        zero_point: torch.Tensor,
        qmin: float | torch.Tensor,
        qmax: float | torch.Tensor,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        ctx.save_for_backward(x, scale, zero_point)
        # Quantize, clamp to discrete range, and dequantize
        q = torch.clamp(torch.round(x / scale) + zero_point, qmin, qmax)
        dq = (q - zero_point) * scale
        return dq

    @staticmethod
    def backward(ctx: Any, grad_output: torch.Tensor) -> tuple[torch.Tensor | None, ...]:
        # Straight-Through Estimator passes gradient unmodified
        grad_x = grad_output.clone()
        return grad_x, None, None, None, None

Straight-Through Estimator (STE) fake-quantization autograd function. Clamps inputs to [qmin, qmax] in discrete integer space, de-quantizes back, and passes gradients through uninhibited across all channels.

def backward(ctx: Any, grad_output: torch.Tensor) -> tuple[torch.Tensor | None, ...]:

Define a formula for differentiating the operation with backward mode automatic differentiation.

This function is to be overridden by all subclasses. (Defining this function is equivalent to defining the vjp function.)

It must accept a context :attr:ctx as the first argument, followed by as many outputs as the :func:forward returned (None will be passed in for non tensor outputs of the forward function), and it should return as many tensors, as there were inputs to :func:forward. Each argument is the gradient w.r.t the given output, and each returned value should be the gradient w.r.t. the corresponding input. If an input is not a Tensor or is a Tensor not requiring grads, you can just pass None as a gradient for that input.

The context can be used to retrieve tensors saved during the forward pass. It also has an attribute :attr:ctx.needs_input_grad as a tuple of booleans representing whether each input needs gradient. E.g., :func:backward will have ctx.needs_input_grad[0] = True if the first input to :func:forward needs gradient computed w.r.t. the output.

def forward(ctx: Any,
x: torch.Tensor,
scale: torch.Tensor,
zero_point: torch.Tensor,
qmin: float | torch.Tensor,

Expand source code 
@staticmethod
def forward(
    ctx: Any,
    x: torch.Tensor,
    scale: torch.Tensor,
    zero_point: torch.Tensor,
    qmin: float | torch.Tensor,
    qmax: float | torch.Tensor,
) -> torch.Tensor:
    ctx.save_for_backward(x, scale, zero_point)
    # Quantize, clamp to discrete range, and dequantize
    q = torch.clamp(torch.round(x / scale) + zero_point, qmin, qmax)
    dq = (q - zero_point) * scale
    return dq

Define the forward of the custom autograd Function.

This function is to be overridden by all subclasses. There are two ways to define forward:

Usage 1 (Combined forward and ctx)::

@staticmethod
def forward(ctx: Any, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
    pass
  • It must accept a context ctx as the first argument, followed by any number of arguments (tensors or other types).
  • See :ref:combining-forward-context for more details

Usage 2 (Separate forward and ctx)::

@staticmethod
def forward(*args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
    pass


@staticmethod
def setup_context(ctx: Any, inputs: Tuple[Any, ...], output: Any) -> None:
    pass
  • The forward no longer accepts a ctx argument.
  • Instead, you must also override the :meth:torch.autograd.Function.setup_context staticmethod to handle setting up the ctx object. output is the output of the forward, inputs are a Tuple of inputs to the forward.
  • See :ref:extending-autograd for more details

The context can be used to store arbitrary data that can be then retrieved during the backward pass. Tensors should not be stored directly on ctx (though this is not currently enforced for backward compatibility). Instead, tensors should be saved either with :func:ctx.save_for_backward if they are intended to be used in backward (equivalently, vjp) or :func:ctx.save_for_forward if they are intended to be used for in jvp.

class SVDLinear (in_features: int,

lora_alpha: float = 1.0,
lora_r: int | None = None,
freeze_base: bool = False)
Expand source code 
class SVDLinear(nn.Module):
    """
    Low-rank factorized linear layer:
        y = (x @ V.T) @ U.T + LoRA(x) * scaling + b
    Parameter count: r·(d_in + d_out) + LoRA params.
    """

    # AASVD-FIX-01
    is_custom_attention: bool = False

    def __init__(
        self,
        in_features: int,
        out_features: int,
        rank: int,
        bias: bool = True,
        use_fused_kernel: bool = False,
        dtype: torch.dtype = torch.float32,
        device: torch.device | None = None,
        lora_rank: int = 0,
        lora_alpha: float = 1.0,
        lora_r: int | None = None,
        freeze_base: bool = False,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.in_features = int(in_features)
        self.out_features = int(out_features)
        self.rank = max(1, min(rank, self.in_features, self.out_features))
        self.use_fused_kernel = bool(use_fused_kernel)

        # Accept both lora_rank and legacy lora_r
        eff_lora = int(lora_r) if (lora_r is not None and lora_r > 0) else max(0, int(lora_rank))
        self.lora_rank = eff_lora
        self.lora_r = eff_lora
        self.lora_alpha = float(lora_alpha)
        self.lora_scaling = (self.lora_alpha / self.lora_rank) if self.lora_rank > 0 else 1.0
        self.scaling = self.lora_scaling

        requires_grad = not (self.lora_rank > 0 or freeze_base)
        fk = {"dtype": dtype, "device": device}

        self.V = nn.Parameter(
            torch.empty(self.rank, self.in_features, **fk), requires_grad=requires_grad
        )
        self.U = nn.Parameter(
            torch.empty(self.out_features, self.rank, **fk), requires_grad=requires_grad
        )

        if bias:
            self.bias = nn.Parameter(
                torch.empty(self.out_features, **fk), requires_grad=requires_grad
            )
        else:
            self.register_parameter("bias", None)

        if self.lora_rank > 0:
            self.lora_A = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.lora_rank, self.in_features, **fk))
            self.lora_B = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(self.out_features, self.lora_rank, **fk))
            with torch.no_grad():
                nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
        else:
            self.register_parameter("lora_A", None)
            self.register_parameter("lora_B", None)

        # AASVD-FIX-07: input mean is not tracked; remove the buffer.
        self._input_mean: torch.Tensor | None = None

        self.reset_parameters()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @property
    def weight(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        return self.get_weight()

    def get_weight(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        w = torch.matmul(self.U, self.V)
        if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
            w = w + torch.matmul(self.lora_B, self.lora_A) * self.lora_scaling
        return w

    @property
    def use_lora(self) -> bool:
        return bool(self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None)

    def freeze_base_weights(self) -> None:
        if self.V.requires_grad:
            self.V.requires_grad = False
        if self.U.requires_grad:
            self.U.requires_grad = False
        if self.bias is not None and self.bias.requires_grad:
            self.bias.requires_grad = False
        if self.lora_A is not None:
            self.lora_A.requires_grad = True
        if self.lora_B is not None:
            self.lora_B.requires_grad = True

    def reset_parameters(self) -> None:
        with torch.no_grad():
            for param in (self.V, self.U):
                if param.dtype in (torch.float16, torch.bfloat16):
                    tmp = torch.empty_like(param, dtype=torch.float32, device="cpu")
                    _orthogonal_init(tmp)
                    param.copy_(tmp.to(dtype=param.dtype, device=param.device))
                else:
                    _orthogonal_init(param)
            if self.bias is not None:
                nn.init.zeros_(self.bias)
            if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
                nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
                nn.init.zeros_(self.lora_B)

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        if self._input_mean is not None:
            x = x - self._input_mean.to(x.device, x.dtype)

        if self.use_fused_kernel and (self.lora_rank == 0 or self.lora_A is None):
            try:
                from neuralops.attentions.fused_svd_kernel import fused_svd_linear

                return fused_svd_linear(x=x, V=self.V, U=self.U, bias=self.bias, use_rms_norm=False)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("SVDLinear fused kernel failed (%s); using F.linear.", e)

        latent = F.linear(x, self.V)
        out = F.linear(latent, self.U)

        if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
            a = self.lora_A.to(x.dtype) if self.lora_A.dtype != x.dtype else self.lora_A
            b = self.lora_B.to(x.dtype) if self.lora_B.dtype != x.dtype else self.lora_B
            lora_latent = F.linear(x, a)
            out = out + F.linear(lora_latent, b) * self.lora_scaling

        if self.bias is not None:
            out = out + self.bias.to(out.dtype)
        return out

    def set_low_rank_factors(self, U_factor: torch.Tensor, V_factor: torch.Tensor) -> None:
        with torch.no_grad():
            r = min(self.rank, U_factor.size(1), V_factor.size(0))
            self.U.data.zero_()
            self.V.data.zero_()
            self.U.data[:, :r].copy_(U_factor[:, :r].to(self.U.device, self.U.dtype))
            self.V.data[:r, :].copy_(V_factor[:r, :].to(self.V.device, self.V.dtype))

def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    if self._input_mean is not None:
        x = x - self._input_mean.to(x.device, x.dtype)

    if self.use_fused_kernel and (self.lora_rank == 0 or self.lora_A is None):
        try:
            from neuralops.attentions.fused_svd_kernel import fused_svd_linear

            return fused_svd_linear(x=x, V=self.V, U=self.U, bias=self.bias, use_rms_norm=False)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("SVDLinear fused kernel failed (%s); using F.linear.", e)

    latent = F.linear(x, self.V)
    out = F.linear(latent, self.U)

    if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
        a = self.lora_A.to(x.dtype) if self.lora_A.dtype != x.dtype else self.lora_A
        b = self.lora_B.to(x.dtype) if self.lora_B.dtype != x.dtype else self.lora_B
        lora_latent = F.linear(x, a)
        out = out + F.linear(lora_latent, b) * self.lora_scaling

    if self.bias is not None:
        out = out + self.bias.to(out.dtype)
    return out
